Folded in: `_fmt_date` re-converts an aware datetime to `current_org_tz()` before `.date()`; **OVERDUE is now `now_is_working`-gated** (closes R55 D-5's weekend-pierce exemption — a weekend/holiday overdue notice defers to the next working day, doc 10 §9.5); `cli/backfill_review_dates.py` recomputes stored `next_review_due` into the canonical tz (idempotent, `--dry-run`; MR next-due is DERIVED → no backfill). **NO migration** (head stays `0067`), **NO new permission key** (catalog 102), **NO web/FE change** (`review_state`/MR badge are server-computed — values just get more correct), env var KEPT as the bottom fallback, `organization.timezone` & `working_calendar.timezone` stay independently editable (NO S-notify-7 regression). New register **R56** + amend-notes on R8 & R55.

⚠ **THE crux the final whole-branch review (opus + diff-critic CONVERGED) caught — a load-bearing false-PASS the per-task gates + the local `mypy`/targeted-unit gates ALL missed:** the §3.2.2 `using_org_tz` wrap was DROPPED on the WORKER paths. Three sweeps (`sweep_reviews`, `sweep_mgmt_reviews`, the ack `sweep_acks`) enqueue the `task.assigned` "Due by: {{task.due_at|date}}" template with the contextvar UNSET → `_fmt_date` reframed the `snap_to_working_day`-produced **cal.tz-aware** `due_at` to the previous UTC day (**REGRESSING the Codex #291 P2 fix** — pre-slice `_fmt_date` did a bare `.date()` on the cal.tz object = correct), AND `_cutover` computed the STORED `next_review_due` via `current_org_tz()` — unset in the `release_due` Beat → env/UTC. **ALL east-of-UTC-only → masked on the dev Chicago org (west) + CI (UTC)** = ships green. Fix (in-PR): wrap each worker enqueue in `using_org_tz(cal.tz)` (mirror the escalation sweep, already wrapped); `_cutover` resolves `resolve_org_tz(session, doc.org_id)` EXPLICITLY (fixes both the request `release()` AND the Beat). `engine.py:222` (skips DOC_ACK/PERIODIC_REVIEW, request-context for the rest), `spawn.py:114` (request), `service.py:88` (request) correctly need no wrap. diff-critic also caught the ack site opus missed.

⚠ **Two MORE issues surfaced AFTER the local gate — both why running the FULL suite before the PR is load-bearing:** (a) the full `-m unit` (run by `/pr`, NOT my targeted per-task suites) caught a PRE-EXISTING Codex #291 test (`test_duedate_snap.py::test_snapped_value_renders_calendar_date_not_utc`) that calls `_fmt_date` DIRECTLY with no contextvar — the §3.4 change made it re-convert to UTC → wrap it in `using_org_tz(tokyo)` to model the (now-wrapped) production render. (b) CI's integration shard caught a date-math bug in the new sweep-render verification test — `(7 - isoweekday()) % 7` targets the next **SUNDAY** (weekday 7), not Monday; it set `next_review_due` to a Sunday, the sweep correctly **SNAPPED** `due_at` forward to Monday, and the test asserted the unsnapped Sunday → `(1 - isoweekday()) % 7` for a real Monday. **The CI run PROVED the production fix correct** (the body rendered the Tokyo Monday `2026-06-29`, not the UTC Sunday); both fixes were TEST-ONLY, production code untouched.

⚠ **Other load-bearing notes:** a `ContextVar.set()` in an `async` FastAPI dependency propagates to the handler/serializers (same task) + no reset needed (per-request task isolation, the `request_id_var` precedent) — but a WORKER (multi-org sweep) MUST use `using_org_tz` (reset) so it can't leak across tasks. The HTTP propagation integration test is only mutation-distinguishing ~14/24h (the UTC-vs-cal "today" straddle) → backstopped by a deterministic monkeypatched `get_current_user` unit test. The OVERDUE gate reversal broke 3 existing weekend/holiday-OVERDUE unit tests (asserted `[OVERDUE]` on a Sat/holiday → now `[]`) — updated to the new gated semantics. CI `mypy` is `mypy src` (does NOT check tests). The ruff `--fix` PostToolUse hook repeatedly stripped just-added imports before their usage landed (F401→F821) — add the using code first. **Named residuals (not faked):** the 5 ship-as-is review Minors [`resolve_org_tz` docstring "never raises" is aspirational (DB I/O propagates); the propagation test doesn't restore `set_approver_release` (matches precedent); `test_date_filter_formats_datetime` now implicitly relies on the UTC default; no `--dry-run`=True backfill test; the backfill test asserts `!=wrong` not `==target` (idempotency covers it)] · the mutation-verify (`resolve_org_tz`→UTC) is CI/live-smoke-authoritative · the R55 "Named residuals" prose still names the old D-5 exemption (superseded by the appended R56 amend-note, per the append-don't-rewrite convention). Process: brainstorm (owner: cal-canonical-with-fallback + hybrid mechanism + all 3 scope adds) → spec → 10-task plan → subagent-driven (fresh implementer + task-reviewer each, per-task fixes folded) → final whole-branch (opus + diff-critic, 1 root cause / 4 sites, fixed) → green local gate → PR → `/pr` full-`-m unit` caught (a) → CI integration caught (b). (S-orgtz-unify, BE+CLI+docs, NO migration [head stays `0067`], NO new key [catalog 102], api unit 1052→1062 [+~12 incl. the new `test_org_clock`], +5 integration, web 1292 unchanged, PR #294 squash `0008b08`; final review 1 root-cause/4 sites + 2 post-gate test fixes.)

### S-duedate-snap — snap a task's `due_at` to a working day at materialize (R29 weekend-OVERDUE close-out, **R55**; BE-only; NO migration [head stays `0067`]; PR #291 squash `f879395`)

**What shipped — the un-numbered residual S-notify-6 AND S-notify-7 both deferred.** S-notify-6 wired `working_calendar` into the timer **OFFSETS** (reminders/escalation skip weekends) but KEPT **OVERDUE raw** (`now >= due_at`) because the root cause is upstream: `due_at` itself is raw wall-clock (`engine._due_at` = `now()+timedelta(hours=…)`; seeded `default_sla={'hours':120}` = 120 clock-hours) and frequently lands on a weekend → an unshifted OVERDUE fires `task.overdue` (escalation-class, quiet-hours-pierce-eligible) on a Saturday, which **doc 10 §9.5 forbids**. This slice **numbers it R55** and fixes it **at the source**: when a `Task.due_at` is materialized, if its date (in the calendar's tz) is a **non-working day** it is snapped **FORWARD to the next working day, preserving local time-of-day**; a working-day `due_at` is unchanged. So OVERDUE + every business-day offset land on a working day **with no OVERDUE special-casing in `due_steps`** (the timer is behaviorally unchanged). **NO migration** (head stays `0067`), **NO new permission key** (engine logic, R38, catalog stays 102), **NO web/openapi**, **NO WORM/authz/signature_event touch** (this only changes a `Task.due_at` value).

**The five materialize sites (exhaustive — grep-confirmed by 2 reviewers).** A pure `services/notifications/timer.py::snap_to_working_day(due_at, cal)` (forward-only · idempotent · the returned instant's cal.tz date is ALWAYS a working day [a spring-forward midnight-gap can normalize the reconstructed wall time ACROSS midnight onto a non-working day → each candidate is **RE-CHECKED** on the resolved instant, the IDEM-1 fix] · exhaustion **fail-safe returns the input UNCHANGED**, never a never-overdue far-future sentinel = fail-OPEN) + a tiny seam `services/notifications/duedate.py` (`resolve_calendar`/`snap_due_at`, **lazy-imports** `resolve_working_calendar` to avoid the `escalation→workflow.__init__→engine` cycle — load-bearing). **INSTANT sites** (a true instant, no calendar-day intent — just snap in cal.tz): `workflow/engine.py::_materialize_stage` (every engine-materialized task; the single chokepoint for instantiate AND stage-advance) + the DOC_ACK sweep override (`ack/sweep.py`, a NEW `snapped_due_at` local fed to BOTH the `update(Task)` AND the `due_at_override` — never reassign the org-spanning loop-invariant). **DATE-anchored sites** (D-5): `vault/review.py` (PERIODIC_REVIEW), `mgmt_review/spawn.py` (MR_ACTION, `_action_due_at` now takes `cal`), `mgmt_review/cadence.py` (MR_INPUT) now resolve the calendar FIRST and **build `due_at` at midnight in `cal.tz`** (not the env `easysynq_org_timezone`), then snap. `workflow/service.py::instantiate_approval` correctly has **no `due_at`** (NULL → excluded by the timer's `due_at IS NOT NULL` claim) → NOT a site.

**Owner decisions (AskUserQuestion ×5, ratified 2026-06-25).** D-1 R55/forward-only/preserve-time · D-2 all 5 sites (every `TaskType` has a seeded `SlaPolicy` → any can fire a weekend OVERDUE) · D-3 snap the PERIODIC_REVIEW **task** due_at + **accept the `review_state` badge divergence** (stored `next_review_due` UNCHANGED) · D-4 new-only, **NO backfill** · **D-5 (surfaced by spec-validation TZ-1):** the 3 date-anchored sites build in `cal.tz` so build+snap+timer share ONE business-day frame (the S-notify-7 "the calendar owns the business-day tz" authority) — **zero behavior change** under a correctly-configured deployment (env tz == calendar tz). ⚠ **The crux the 6-lens pre-code spec-validation (19 agents, ~2.3M tok, 7 confirmed findings ALL folded) caught: TZ-1 (MAJOR)** — the 3 date-anchored sites built `due_at` in the **env** `easysynq_org_timezone` (default UTC) while the snap/timer judge in the **DB** `working_calendar.timezone` (the dev box already diverges: env UTC, cal America/Chicago) → under divergence the snap re-judged an operator's date in the wrong frame (could push an operator-set Monday to Tuesday); resolved by D-5. **IDEM-1** — the DST-gap re-check (empirically reproduced on America/Nuuk 2024-03-29: a Saturday 23:30 candidate normalizes to a non-working Sunday). Both MAJOR/real, both fixed pre-code.

**Reconcile (the heart — why the timer needs no change + no double-shift):** `business_threshold` computes offsets FROM `due_at`; with `due_at` already a working day, REMIND/ESCALATE/OVERDUE all land on working days — nothing snaps twice (`business_threshold` still receives ONE `due_at`, now already a working day). The S-notify-6 offset tests are unaffected (`due_steps` byte-unchanged; only a new pure helper is ADDED to `timer.py`). The escalation/reminder timings re-anchor to the real (working-day) due date (never earlier than the §9.5 intent — benign, documented).

⚠ **Build traps.** The PostToolUse ruff `--fix` strips a just-added import if the using code isn't present yet (F401) — re-added at spawn.py/cadence.py/the periodic test after the usage landed (the engineering-patterns trap). ⚠ **Shared-DB test design:** the session DB's default-org calendar tz is unpredictable across files (a throwaway org with a committed calendar can't be cleaned — REVOKE DELETE / the S-notify-4/7 leaked-org trap that breaks `test_restore`'s `scalar_one`) → every integration assertion RESOLVES the org's **actual** calendar and checks against `snap_to_working_day(raw, cal)` + `is_working_day(...)` (robust + mutation-distinguishing). The pre-existing `test_periodic_review.py:301` exact-`due_at` assert was de-flaked the same way (RECON-1 sites). The MR `2026-12-31` assertion stays green (a Thursday → snap no-op; cal.tz==UTC seed) — a reviewer-noted latent coupling left as-is (no near-term risk; both reviewers rated "no action required").

**Process.** spec-first → 6-lens adversarial spec-validation (ultracode Workflow) → plan → 3-stage TDD build (pure helper+seam → wire 5 sites → integration+e2e) → **mutation-verified** (neutering `snap_to_working_day` fails 8 unit + all 3 key integration tests) → 2-lens whole-branch review (diff-critic **CLEAN** · whole-branch **READY**, 120 integration tests run live) → **live-smoke** (BE-only, deployed-worker snippets: ORG_EXAMPLE calendar resolved live = Mon-Fri/Chicago; `snap_due_at` Sat→Mon in cal.tz [time-of-day preserved]; D-5 `_action_due_at(Sat, cal)` built at Mon **00:00 Chicago**; a real `timer_sweep()`→`{tasks:0,steps:0}` clean; the deployed `due_steps` proved OLD raw-Sat OVERDUE-at-Sunday=True [the bug] vs NEW snapped OVERDUE-at-Sunday=False / at-Monday=True [fixed, on a working day]).

⚠ **Codex round 1 — 1 P2 (genuine, fixed `snap_to_working_day` to RETURN in `cal.tz`).** For an **east-of-UTC** calendar a date-anchored due that snaps to local Monday midnight returned as `Sun 15:00 UTC`; the notification renderer `notifications/render._fmt_date` formats `value.date()` at ENQUEUE (dispatch.py:163) without re-converting → the email showed **Sunday** for a Monday-due task (undercutting the slice's intent; the dev org Chicago/west-of-UTC was unaffected). Fix = return the snapped instant **in `cal.tz`** (the INSTANT is unchanged → persistence/`now`-comparisons unaffected; only the in-memory `.date()` render aligns); the working-day branch already returned the cal.tz-built input for date-anchored sites. Locked by a mutation-distinguishing unit test (`out.date()` is the Monday *local* date + `_fmt_date(out) == "2026-06-29"`, both fail on a UTC return). The broader `_fmt_date`-renders-UTC-`.date()` issue for an UNSNAPPED instant-site due on a non-UTC org is pre-existing (folds into the deferred `_org_tz()`-unification slice).

**Named residuals (not faked):** OVERDUE can still fire on a non-working day in two narrow edges — a sweep delayed across a working→non-working boundary (OVERDUE has no `now_is_working` gate, the ratified D-5 weekend-pierce exemption) + a post-materialize `working_calendar` edit (S-notify-7) turning an already-due date into a holiday · the env `easysynq_org_timezone` vs DB `working_calendar.timezone` remain two **un-unified** org-tz sources (unifying `_org_tz()` onto the calendar — which also fixes `_fmt_date` for unsnapped non-UTC due dates — is a deferred cross-cutting slice) · the `test_mgmt_review.py` UTC-coupling hardening · no backfill of existing open tasks · the carried Notification tail (claim-threshold-filter tautology · distinct `remind_2` · `escalate_2` · `capa.overdue` · multiple named calendars/org · holiday recurrence/bulk-import). **Tests:** api unit **1037→1052** (+15: weekend/holiday/idempotent/monotonic snaps · eastward+westward tz · the Nuuk midnight-DST-gap · exhaustion fail-safe · `_action_due_at` build-in-cal.tz [D-5 lock] · the cal.tz-return render lock [Codex P2]); **+3 integration** (the seam resolve+snap · the engine materialize e2e · a dedicated PERIODIC_REVIEW weekend-review snap [next_review_due unchanged, D-3]) + the de-flaked existing periodic assert; web unchanged (**1292**). (S-duedate-snap, BE-only, NO migration [head stays `0067`], NO new key [catalog 102], **R55**, api unit 1037→1052 +3 integration, web 1292, PR #291 squash `f879395`; spec-validation 7 findings folded · diff-critic+whole-branch CLEAN · Codex 1 round / 1 P2 fixed.)

### S-notify-7 — the `working_calendar` admin editor: make the business-day calendar configurable (R29 editor residual close-out; BE+FE; NO migration [head stays `0067`]; PR #289 squash `03419ce`)

**What shipped — the last R29 residual.** S-notify-6 wired `working_calendar` into the `timer_sweep` but left it **frozen at the Mon-Fri/`[]`/`<org-tz>` seed** — no in-app way to set the working days, add a holiday, rename it, or fix its timezone. This slice adds a **`config.update`-gated admin editor** for the org's **default** calendar, in the existing `/admin/config` Config tab (next to the S-notify-5b notification toggles + delivery-health panel). **NO migration** (the table + its `INSERT/SELECT/UPDATE` grants exist from `0067`; DELETE stays REVOKEd; head stays `0067`), **NO new permission key** (reuses `config.update`, catalog stays **102**), **audit reuses `CONFIG_UPDATED`** (object_type=config — no new `event_type`, no `ALTER TYPE`), **NO WORM touch** (the editor only edits config; N9/R53/R32 unchanged).

**BE.** A shared **pure** parser module `services/notifications/calendar_spec.py` (`parse_working_days` [moved verbatim from `escalation._parse_working_days`] · `parse_holiday` [keeps the resolver's `str()` coercion so an int entry like `20260101` still parses → resolver byte-identical] · `is_valid_timezone`) is imported by **both** the fail-safe `resolve_working_calendar` (refactored to it, the private fn deleted) **and** the fail-loud editor — so **validation parity can't drift, by construction (one code path, not two parallel assertions)**. A thin service `calendar_admin.py`: `get_working_calendar` (the `is_default` row `.limit(1)` as a view, **sanitized** through the parsers kept-good, OR a synthesized Mon-Fri default `{tz=organization.timezone, exists:false}` on the no-row edge) + `update_working_calendar` (validate fail-loud → **atomic `ON CONFLICT (org_id) WHERE is_default DO UPDATE`** upsert → audit-on-diff; **does NOT commit — the route commits**, the `api/config.py` precedent). `GET`/`PUT /admin/notifications/working-calendar` (both `Depends(config.update)`, org from `caller.org_id`) + openapi (2 paths + 2 schemas). The PUT body is **`list[Any]`** (NOT `list[int]`/`list[str]`).

**FE.** A self-contained `admin/WorkingCalendarEditor.tsx` in `ConfigAdmin` (between the toggles and the health panel; relies on the parent's GET-`/admin/config`-403 gate for no-access): a labelled Mantine **`Checkbox.Group`** week mask (string[]→`Number()`→sorted-unique ints; ≥1 enforced) · a native **`<input type=date>`** holiday list (blank-Add guard, deduped+sorted, distinct `Remove holiday {date}` labels) · an **editable** curated/searchable timezone `Select` (the `timezones.ts` idiom) · a **value-equality dirty-diff over canonical (sorted-unique) arrays** · a single Save (PUT). + 2 hooks (`useWorkingCalendar`/`useUpdateWorkingCalendar`, the unsaved-edit-clobber guards), 2 types, + MSW base GET/PUT handlers.

**Confirmed forks (AskUserQuestion).** (1) **Timezone EDITABLE here** — a REVERSAL of the spec's first-pass "slaved to org profile": the pre-code spec-validation proved the slaving premise FALSE — `set_org_profile` is reachable only from the `SetupWizard`, which `App.tsx` redirects away from once OPERATIONAL, so there is **no operational UI to change the org tz**; a read-only "change it on the org profile" note would point at a dead surface. So the editor owns the business-day tz operationally (it may diverge from the R8 effective-date tz — accepted). (2) native date input, **allow past**, deduped+sorted. (3) GET synthesizes a Mon-Fri default (`exists:false`) on the no-row edge, PUT upserts.

**Process: spec-first → a 6-lens adversarial spec-validation → a 6-task subagent-driven TDD build → a 3-lens whole-branch review → live-smoke → Codex.** ⚠ **The spec-validation (6 lenses → dedup → refute-first verify → triage) found 22 distinct findings, ALL confirmed real, and folded every one in BEFORE code** — it reversed the timezone premise (above) and **empirically pinned the central parity fix:** on this project's **pydantic 2.13.4 (lax mode)** a `working_days: list[int]` body **silently coerces** `[true]→[1]`, `["1"]→[1]`, `[1.0]→[1]` (HTTP 200) and 422s `[1.5]` **at Pydantic** → the strict bool/float/string guards would be **dead code** and the "rejects exactly what the resolver degrades" guarantee FALSE → declare the body **`list[Any]`** so every value reaches the strict service parser (one uniform service 422 mirroring the resolver's degrade). Other folded findings: **duplicate `working_days` are deduped + ACCEPTED** (NOT 422 — the resolver dedups; a 422 would itself break parity); **holiday parity is asymmetric** (the resolver SKIPS-kept-good a bad holiday, the editor REJECTS it → framed "editor ⊇ resolver", not bidirectional); the **atomic `ON CONFLICT` upsert** (a bare check-then-INSERT races the partial-unique `uq_working_calendar_one_default` → an unhandled IntegrityError/500); **value-equality dirty-diff** over canonical arrays (reference `!==` reads permanently-dirty after the post-save reseed — the S-notify-3b post-save-reset class); **MSW base handlers** for the 2 new endpoints (else `ConfigAdmin.test.tsx`'s success-path suites break under `onUnhandledRequest:"error"` once the editor mounts); input bounds (holidays ≤1000 / working_days ≤31); audit `CONFIG_UPDATED` **only on a real diff** (no no-op WORM rows); the **no-commit-service / route-commits** boundary (makes the INSERT-branch test leak-free — the app role can't DELETE a `working_calendar` row and the org FK is RESTRICT, so a *committed* calendar in a throwaway org can't be cleaned up — the S-notify-4 leaked-org trap). ⚠ **The 3-lens whole-branch review: diff-critic CLEAN · web-test-trap-reviewer CLEAN (both anti-tautology claims mutation-verified — removing the `onSuccess` invalidate and dropping `hasDay` each correctly broke a test) · opus whole-branch READY** (all 7 load-bearing invariants verified end-to-end). A per-task review caught one **Important** mid-build (the INSERT-branch could skip the audit if the first Save matched the synthesized defaults → `before_fields={}` when `not exists` so the audit always fires on first-create).

**Live-smoke (Chrome MCP, end-to-end).** Rebuilt web/api/worker/beat (no migration); demo (System Administrator) holds `config.update` natively (no override). Drove `/admin/config` → Working calendar: the editor loaded ORG_EXAMPLE's seed exactly (Mon-Fri, no holidays, America/Chicago, "Default", Save disabled) → added holiday `2026-07-03` (chip + distinct remove button + Save enabled) → Save → "Saved." + Save re-disabled (value-equality reset) + persisted → **a worker-container snippet proved the saved holiday SHIFTS a business-day threshold** (a reminder 1 business day before Mon 2026-07-06 lands Fri 07-03 WITHOUT the holiday, **Thu 07-02 WITH** it — the end-to-end SLA proof) + the `CONFIG_UPDATED` audit-on-diff (add+remove = 2 rows, no no-op pollution) → removed → ORG_EXAMPLE restored pristine.

⚠ **Codex — round 1, 3×P2 (all genuine, all fixed + thread-resolved; CI green; owner merged on approval).** (1) **calendar-tz durability** — the editor's tz was NOT durable: `set_org_profile` is API-reachable (latch-exempt `/api/v1/setup/*`) and *unconditionally* re-synced `working_calendar.timezone`, clobbering an operator-set tz → `set_org_profile` now syncs the default calendar **only where `timezone == <org's old tz>`** (preserve a customized tz; on first setup the seed calendar tz == org old tz so the S-notify-6 sync still applies). (2) **raw audit `before`** — the audit `before` used the *sanitized* `get_working_calendar` view, hiding a malformed/legacy stored value the upsert overwrites → now reads the **raw `is_default` row** (`_load_default`); a clean row's raw values already equal the canonical `after`, so no-op detection is unchanged. (3) **save synthesized calendar** — a calendar-less org (`exists:false`) couldn't Save the synthesized default (not dirty → Save disabled) → `canSave = (dirty || !cal.data.exists) && validName && hasDay`. Each fix has a covering test (the raw-`[5,1,3]`-survives audit test · the customized-tz-preserved setup test · `exists:false`→Save-enabled).

**Named residuals (not faked):** the **due_at-snap at materialize** (un-numbered) · the **claim-threshold-filter tautology** (`remind_2_sent_at IS NULL` always true) · the **distinct `remind_2`** · **escalate_2** · **`capa.overdue`** · **multiple named calendars per org** (this editor governs the single `is_default` row only) · **holiday recurrence / bulk import** (a flat date list) · a **2-session no-row INSERT race test** (structurally moot for the atomic `ON CONFLICT` — the plan downgraded it to optional). **Tests:** api unit **1032→1037** (+5: `test_calendar_spec` — valid/dedup-accept · the bool/float/string/empty/range rejections · `parse_holiday` str-coercion incl. the int-`20260101` byte-identical pin · `is_valid_timezone`; `test_working_calendar_resolve` repointed to the shared module, unchanged count); **+9 api integration** (`test_working_calendar_admin`: GET synth-vs-persisted-round-trip · INSERT-no-commit · validation-parity matrix [service-originated 422s + dup-accept] · UPDATE+audit-count + no-op-no-audit · 403 · the raw-audit-`before` test; + 1 setup customized-tz-preserved); web **1286→1292** (+6: `WorkingCalendarEditor` render/a11y · dirty round-trip reset · ≥1-weekday mutation-verify · holiday add/remove/blank-guard · the 2 `exists` Save-gate tests). (S-notify-7, BE+FE, NO migration [head stays `0067`], NO new key [catalog 102], api unit 1032→1037 +9 integration, web 1286→1292, PR #289 squash `03419ce`; Codex 1 round / 3 P2s all fixed.)

### S-notify-6 — business-day escalation SLAs: wire `working_calendar` into the timer sweep (R29 close-out; BE-only; migration `0067`; PR #287 squash `e08da6e`)

**What shipped — the second functional half of R29.** S-notify-4 shipped the durable `timer_sweep` Beat + the manager-graph escalation recipient resolution, but computed reminder/overdue/escalation thresholds with **raw wall-clock `timedelta`s** (a "3 days before due" reminder fired across the weekend; a "1 day after due" escalation pinged a manager on a Saturday). This slice adds the missing **`working_calendar`** entity and computes the **reminder (before-due) + escalation (after-due) OFFSETS** as **business days** against the org's calendar (skip weekends + holidays). **NO new permission key** (R38, catalog stays **102**), **NO web/openapi change** (BE-only), **NO WORM touch**.

**Migration `0067`:** a new operational `working_calendar` table — `id, org_id` (FK→organization RESTRICT), `name`, `working_days` jsonb (ISO weekday mask 1=Mon..7=Sun), `holidays` jsonb (`YYYY-MM-DD` list), **`timezone`** (IANA TEXT default `UTC` — additive to doc-14's column list, **owner fork D-2**: the calendar owns its own tz, seeded = `organization.timezone`), `is_default` bool, `created_at`/`updated_at` + a **partial unique index** `uq_working_calendar_one_default (org_id) WHERE is_default` (migration-managed in `env.py._MIGRATION_MANAGED_INDEXES`, absent from the ORM — the `ix_task_timer_pending` precedent) + **REVOKE DELETE** (keep INSERT/SELECT/UPDATE — operational config for the deferred editor, the notification-ledger posture, NOT `sla_policy`'s SELECT-only) + a **per-org seed** (one Mon-Fri `is_default` calendar, `timezone = organization.timezone`, raw `INSERT … CAST(:x AS JSONB)` + `json.dumps` for bulletproof serialization). `sla_policy` UNCHANGED — the stored Interval offsets are reinterpreted as business days (the S-notify-4 "slots in with no schema change" prediction held). **Downgrade** = `drop_index` then `drop_table` (no inbound FK → clean on a populated DB, no NOT-EXISTS guard).

**The math** (`services/notifications/timer.py`, kept pure/session-free): a frozen `Calendar(working_weekdays, holidays, tz)` value object + `is_working_day` · `shift_business_days(anchor, n, direction)` (walk day-by-day, count only working days, anchor NOT counted, bounded loop) · `business_threshold(due_at, offset, direction)` (UTC due_at → `cal.tz` local date → shift N working days → recombine local time-of-day → back to UTC; whole-day component walks business days, any sub-day remainder applied as wall-clock) · `DEFAULT_CALENDAR` (Mon-Fri/UTC). `due_steps` gains a `calendar` param: REMIND_1/2 use `BEFORE` (fire EARLIER than raw — skip the weekend backward, giving real working-day lead time), ESCALATE_1 uses `AFTER` (fire LATER — never over a weekend/holiday); **OVERDUE stays `now >= due_at`, unshifted (D-5).** The existing `due_steps` unit tests pass via an `ALL_DAYS` (7-day) calendar that degenerates to raw. **Wiring** (`escalation.py`): `resolve_working_calendar(session, org_id)` reads the org's `is_default` row → builds the pure `Calendar`, **fail-safe** (missing row / structurally-broken `working_days` / unknown `timezone` → `DEFAULT_CALENDAR`+warn; per-bad-holiday skip kept-good; a **non-list `holidays` scalar** → no holidays [the review fix]); resolved **once per locked per-task txn**, threaded into `due_steps`. The advisory-lock + FOR-UPDATE-SKIP-LOCKED + populate_existing + one-commit-per-task idempotency is UNCHANGED; `_due_task_ids` (stamp-null superset) is UNCHANGED.

**The OVERDUE decision (D-5, owner-ratified post-spec-validation).** ⚠ **A pre-code 5-lens/8-agent spec-validation (lens L1) proved the spec's original OVERDUE rationale FACTUALLY FALSE:** it claimed `due_at` is "already business-day-derived via a `due_in: N business_days` materialize path" — that path **does not exist**. `engine._due_at` is raw wall-clock (`now()+timedelta(hours=…)`; seeded `default_sla={'hours':120}` = 120 clock-hours), so `due_at` frequently lands on a weekend → an unshifted OVERDUE fires `task.overdue` (escalation-class, pierce-eligible) on weekends, which doc 10 §9.5 forbids. **Owner fork (AskUserQuestion):** keep OVERDUE raw at `due_at` (this slice shifts only the OFFSETS — overdue has no offset) + name the upstream **due_at-snap at materialize** as a still-deferred reconcile; *rejected* snapping OVERDUE in the timer (it would defeat the critical-overdue weekend pierce). ⚠ **The "R39" label was a stale mis-citation** — `engine._due_at`'s "deferred, R39" docstring (which the spec echoed) is wrong; the register's R39 is the Audits/CAPA decision. **The due_at-snap residual is UN-NUMBERED** (its own future register call); the engine docstring + register R29 as-built note + the spec §8 were corrected in this slice.

**Owner forks (AskUserQuestion ×2 + the D-5 fork):** BE-only (the `config.update`-gated admin editor deferred) · the `timezone` column on the calendar seeded from `organization.timezone` · keep OVERDUE raw.

**Process: spec-first → pre-code 5-lens spec-validation → 7-task TDD build → 3-lens convergence review → live-smoke.** ⚠ **The spec-validation (L2/L3/L4 design-sound; L1+L5 design-flawed, all confirmed, zero refuted) caught two MAJORS before code:** L1 (the false OVERDUE premise, above) + L5 (the test harness — "create-own-org + own SlaPolicy" is UNWORKABLE: the app role is SELECT-only on `sla_policy` so a fresh org has no policy + the `is_default` resolver + the per-org default-unique index + the DELETE-revoked `working_calendar` cleanup deadlock all conspire → rewrote to reuse-ORG_EXAMPLE's-seeded-policies + pre-stamp + build dates in the calendar's own tz + UPDATE-ORG_EXAMPLE-and-restore). ⚠ **The 3-lens convergence review:** diff-critic CLEAN · migration-reviewer CLEAN (independently round-tripped PG16 incl. the CI-blind seed path + verified grants/FK/index live) · worker-safety READY with **one Minor** (a non-list `holidays` JSONB scalar `5`/`true` raised an uncaught `TypeError` at `for h in row.holidays`, silently stalling that org's tasks forever — the broad sweep `except` swallows it cohort-wide but the org never fires; fixed `isinstance(list) else []`, symmetric with the `working_days` guard, + a regression test). ⚠ **THE test traps (build-discovered):** (1) once the timer applies the Mon-Fri calendar, the ~14 existing escalation integration tests using `datetime.now(UTC)` + `due_at = now-2d` became **weekday-flaky** (break when CI runs on a Sunday: `due_at`=Friday → business escalate threshold=Monday > Sunday `now`) → pin `now`/`_BASE` to a fixed **Wednesday**; (2) but `_BASE` was `2032` and `audit_event` is **monthly-partitioned by `occurred_at` with ONLY 2026-06/07/08 created at install** (migration 0010; the `roll_partitions` Beat doesn't run in tests) → the `TASK_ESCALATED` audit insert failed `"no partition of relation audit_event found"` → `_BASE` = `2026-06-24` (a Wednesday IN the 2026-06 partition) serves both the non-audit reminder/overdue tests AND the escalation tests. The anti-tautology weekend test was **mutation-verified** (FAILS against the old raw escalate math). ⚠ The PostToolUse **ruff `--fix` hook strips a just-added import** if no code uses it yet (F401) → add the using code first.

**Live-smoke (BE-driven, no UI):** rebuilt api/worker/beat/migrate, applied **0066→0067 on the POPULATED dev DB** (head `0067`); the seed produced `Default / [1,2,3,4,5] / [] / America/Chicago` (the dev org's **real, non-UTC** tz — proving per-org tz propagation on real data) `is_default=t`; grants exactly `INSERT,SELECT,UPDATE` (REVOKE DELETE live); a real `resolve_working_calendar` → Mon-Fri/America-Chicago + a real `timer_sweep()` → `{tasks:0, steps:0}` (the wired path runs clean on real data). The business-day BEHAVIOR (weekend escalation skip · resolver · fail-safe · idempotency · 2-session concurrency) is integration-test-proven against a real migrated PG16.

⚠ **Codex — 2 rounds, 6 findings (all genuine, none nitpicks, all fixed + thread-resolved; CI green each round).** **Round 1 (3×P2):** (1) **off-calendar firing** — a DELAYED sweep (worker down / template missing over a weekend) fired a reminder/escalation on a non-working day even though the threshold was a working day → `due_steps` now gates REMIND/ESCALATE on `is_working_day(now.astimezone(cal.tz).date())` (OVERDUE exempt, D-5) → defers the ping to the next working day; (2) **non-array `working_days`** — a JSON *string* `"67"` is iterable → wrongly became `{6,7}` → require `isinstance(list)`; (3) **setup tz sync** — the 0067 seed captured the org tz at MIGRATION time (UTC pre-setup), and `set_org_profile` updated only `Organization.timezone` → it now also UPDATEs the default `working_calendar.timezone` (a non-UTC org's SLAs were evaluating against UTC). **Round 2 (2×P2 + 1×P3) — all refinements of round-1's own fixes:** (4) **fallback dropped a valid tz** — round-1's `is_working_day(now)` gate *created* this: a `working_days` fallback to `DEFAULT_CALENDAR` (UTC) made the gate judge weekends in UTC for a non-UTC org → `resolve_working_calendar` restructured to resolve the **tz FIRST** and keep it through any weekdays fallback (only the weekdays → Mon-Fri); (5) **silent int coercion** — `int(x)` coerced floats/bools (`[1.9,2]`/`[true,2]`, since `bool` is an `int` subclass) → a new pure `_parse_working_days` requires a non-empty array of real ints 1..7 (no bool/float/string); (6) **`shift_business_days` exhaustion** (P3) — a sparse-workweek + >1yr-holiday calendar exhausted the bounded loop and returned an arbitrary (possibly non-working) date → could fire a timer EARLY → now returns a far-future sentinel `date(9999,1,1)` (NOT `date.max` — combining `date.max` with a westward-tz offset overflows `datetime` in `business_threshold`). Each fix has a test.

**Named residuals (not faked):** the **due_at-snap at materialize** (un-numbered — the upstream weekend-OVERDUE fix) · the **`config.update`-gated admin editor** (GET/PUT + Config-tab UI) · the **claim-threshold-filter tautology** (`remind_2_sent_at IS NULL` is always true → every open task re-claimed each sweep; 0-step no-op, bounded) · the **distinct second reminder** (`remind_2`) · **escalate_2** (reassign-from-pool / NEEDS_ATTENTION, doc 10 §9.5 E2) · **`capa.overdue`** · **multiple named calendars per org** (v1 uses `is_default` only) · **holiday recurrence / bulk import** (v1 is a flat date list) · the (v1-unused) **sub-day-remainder** offset behavior (pinned by a unit test; no production caller). **Tests:** api unit **1005→1032** (+27: the business-day helpers · `due_steps`-with-calendar · sub-day-remainder · the working-day-gate weekend+holiday gates · the shift exhaustion + westward-tz no-overflow · 9 `_parse_working_days` cases); **api integration** the resolver suite (reads-default / missing→DEFAULT / malformed-holidays / bad-working_days-keeps-tz / the anti-tautology weekend wiring test) + the setup org-profile calendar-tz-sync assertion; the ~14 existing escalation sweep tests hardened to the fixed Wednesday `_BASE`; web unchanged (**1286**). (S-notify-6, BE-only, migration **0067** [head `0066`→`0067`], api unit 1005→1032, web 1286, PR #287 squash `e08da6e`; Codex 2 rounds / 6 findings all fixed.)

### S-notify-5c — SSE for the notification bell (slice 5c, the LAST Notification-family slice; BE+FE+1 Caddy line; NO migration [head stays `0066`]; PR #285 squash `7505e58`)

Replaces the slice-2 **60 s bell poll** with near-real-time **server-push**. **Publisher:** a ~10 s Celery Beat `pubsub_sweep` (`services/notifications/pubsub.py`, task **co-located** in `tasks/notifications.py` — no new task module) scans `read_at IS NULL AND created_at > now()-LOOKBACK(120 s)` notifications and `PUBLISH`es a **content-free** per-user nudge to `notify:user:{id}`, **de-duping on the immutable `notification.id`** via Redis `SET … NX EX` (TTL 240 s = 2×LOOKBACK). **Subscriber:** `GET /api/v1/notifications/stream` (`api/_sse.py::event_stream` + `api/notifications.py`) — authenticated-self, a pure per-user Redis subscriber returning a `StreamingResponse(text/event-stream)`. **Consumer:** `features/notifications/stream.ts` (`parseSseFrame` CRLF-tolerant · `openNotificationStream` — a `fetch`/`ReadableStream` reader carrying the bearer on the `Authorization` header [native `EventSource` can't set headers; **no token in any URL**] · `sleepWithSignal`) + the `useNotificationStream` hook (mounted in `NotificationBell`); `useNotificationCount` `refetchInterval 60_000→300_000` (the 5-min backstop kept). **Infra:** a Caddy `@sse` block (`path /api/v1/notifications/stream`) with `flush_interval -1`, **before** `@api` (first-match-wins); the dead `/events` token dropped. openapi documents the path (`text/event-stream`/401). **NO migration** (head stays `0066`), **NO new permission key** (authenticated-self, catalog `102`), **NO WORM**.

⚠ **THE crux the pre-code spec-validation (5-lens / 15-agent) caught BEFORE code:** a `created_at` **watermark is BROKEN** — `created_at = func.now() = transaction_timestamp()` (the txn *START*), so a notification enqueued via the SAVEPOINT inside a long/SERIALIZABLE caller txn (the ack sweep mints an org's whole DOC_ACK batch in ONE multi-second txn; `_cutover` is SERIALIZABLE) carries a `created_at` far behind its eventual COMMIT → a monotone watermark advances past it → **permanently missed** → would regress the highest-volume awareness source from 60 s to the 5-min backstop. **Fix = id-de-dup in Redis** (migration-free, immune to the txn-start clock, read-only sweep [no `FOR UPDATE`/lock], self-healing; over-publish harmless, under-publish caught by the backstop). Regression-tested.

⚠ **The DB-session crux:** `get_session` is a yield-dependency held until the `StreamingResponse` body exhausts — and an SSE body is **infinite** → a route streaming on `Depends(get_session)` would **pin a pooled DB connection per open stream** (`psycopg3`/QueuePool default 15/worker). Fix = auth in a **short-lived session** via `_stream_caller` (`async with get_sessionmaker()()` that CLOSES before streaming); the generator touches **NO DB**; `caller.id` captured into a local (`expire_on_commit=False` → detached-safe). `get_current_user`'s body extracted to a reusable `resolve_current_user` (byte-identical; the existing auth suite is the backstop). `event_stream`'s **nested** `try/finally` always `unsubscribe`+`aclose`s the pubsub AND `aclose`s the redis client on **every** exit (the **heartbeat write** is the real abrupt-drop detector; `: ping` every 20 s).

⚠ **The FE reconnect storm:** resetting backoff on *open* lets an accept-then-close server (a worker recycle / a build that trips gunicorn `--timeout 60`) spin a ~1 s reconnect+refetch loop (the on-connect nudge fires an invalidate each time). Fix = reset only after a **healthy duration** (`HEALTHY_MS`) + a hard **`MIN_RECONNECT_MS=3000` floor** (cap `30000`); `sleepWithSignal` is abort-aware so an unmount mid-backoff cancels the pending reconnect. ⚠ **A base MSW handler** (`test/msw/handlers.ts`, a **closeable** `: ping` stream) is load-bearing — without it the mounted hook fires an unhandled `/notifications/stream` fetch that breaks all 4 bell/shell suites under `onUnhandledRequest:"error"` (and a never-closing stream hangs the serial `maxWorkers:1` pool).

⚠ **The 2-UvicornWorker fact makes the Redis bus REQUIRED, not optional** (in-process fan-out wouldn't reach a connection on the other worker); Redis pub/sub fans one `PUBLISH` to all workers. The gunicorn `--timeout 60` × async UvicornWorker survives the long stream (smoke-verified >60 s idle). ⚠ **httpx's in-process `ASGITransport` BUFFERS an infinite SSE body** → an HTTP-200-stream integration test **HANGS** (even `resp.status_code` never arrives — empirically `PYTEST_EXIT=124` twice) → reworked to a direct `event_stream`-against-real-testcontainer-Redis test + the 401 test; the full HTTP 200 stream is **live-smoke-only** (the reviewer's named structural gap).

**Process:** brainstorm (3 owner forks: Beat-sweep→Redis pub/sub · nudge-only · keep a backstop) → pre-code spec-validation (caught 3 majors before code) → spec+plan → 9-task subagent-driven build (one stall on the ASGITransport-hang test, controller-reworked) → 3-lens whole-branch review (whole-branch opus **YES** · diff-critic **CLEAN** vs pinned Starlette 1.2.1/redis-py 6.4.0 · web-test-trap **CLEAN, mutation-verified** [4 prod mutations each went red]) → a fix wave folding the 2 recommended nets (cross-user channel isolation + the unsubscribe-raises teardown) → full gates green → **live-smoke PASS** (bell **0→1→2 via SSE without a refresh**, ~10 s sub-backstop; notification rendered in the popover; mark-all-read clears; **no token in URL**; >60 s idle survival; a dev Keycloak token-expiry correctly showed the three-state "(count unavailable)") → PR #285, **Codex 👍** (no findings).

**Named residuals (not faked):** full-payload push (owner: nudge-only — would add a 2nd count source + an R32 surface) · drop the backstop / a faster degraded cadence / a server `sse_enabled` kill-switch · a **`pushed_at` stamp column** (+partial index) for exact non-window de-dup (eliminates the LOOKBACK scan + the dedup-key churn; owner: migration-free for 5c) · a `created_at`/partial index to make the look-back scan index-served · push for **other surfaces** (the admin Health panel, the `/tasks` inbox count, register summaries) · a **`Last-Event-ID`** replay buffer · the publish-fails-mid-loop unit test (diff-critic, low-value) · the slice-4 timer-sweep claim-threshold filter (unrelated). **With 5c the whole Notification family (slices 1 → 5c) is COMPLETE.**

**Tests:** api unit **996→1005** (+9: `test_notification_pubsub` 5 [id-de-dup-once / same-user-collapse / flush-re-nudge / dedup-key / registration] · `test_sse` 4 [`sse_event` format · `event_stream` initial+message+heartbeat+teardown · aclose-teardown · unsubscribe-raises-teardown]) + **6** new integration in `test_notification_stream.py` (3 sweep [recent-unread nudges · read/old skipped · txn-start-clock-within-LOOKBACK still nudged] · `test_stream_requires_bearer` 401 · `event_stream`-against-real-Redis · cross-user isolation); web **1273→1286** (+13: `stream.test` 9 · `useNotificationStream` 3 [nudge→invalidate · storm-floor · mid-backoff-abort] · `NotificationBell` +1 mount). (S-notify-5c, BE+FE+1 Caddy line, NO migration [head `0066`], NO new key [catalog 102], api unit 996→1005 +6 integration, web 1273→1286, PR #285 squash `7505e58`.)

### S-notify-5b — notification delivery-health panel + the first admin Config tab (slice 5b; FE-heavy + one new read-only BE endpoint; NO migration [head stays `0066`]; PR #283 squash `45dff1e`)

**What shipped — the first admin Config tab + a read-only notification delivery-health panel, made discoverable by the first admin nav entry.** **BE:** a pure, org-scoped aggregator `services/notifications/health.py::get_delivery_health(session, org_id)` — conditional `count(*) FILTER(...)` + `min(created_at) FILTER(...)` over `notification_email` (failed / pending-now / scheduled-retry / suppressed + oldest-pending) + a count/min over `awareness_event` (the fan-out backlog) + the org's `notifications_email_enabled` echo + the last-10 `recent_failures` (`failed_at DESC NULLS LAST`) — behind `GET /admin/notifications/health` in `api/config.py` (gated the existing SYSTEM-domain `config.update`; org from `caller.org_id`, no org_id param → strict isolation) + the openapi path + the `NotificationDeliveryHealth`/`NotificationEmailFailure` schemas. **NO migration (head stays `0066`), NO ORM/model change, NO new permission key (catalog stays `102`), NO WORM touch.** **FE:** `lib/types` (`OrgConfig`/`OrgConfigUpdate`/`NotificationDeliveryHealth`/`NotificationEmailFailure`, pinned to the BE serializers) + `admin/hooks.ts` (`useOrgConfig` [derives a `forbidden` flag], `useUpdateOrgConfig` [PATCH; invalidates BOTH `["admin-config"]` AND `["notification-health"]`], `useNotificationHealth`) + `admin/NotificationHealthPanel.tsx` (read-only: stat cards, the recent-failures table, the email-off banner, AsOf+Refresh) + `admin/ConfigAdmin.tsx` (the two org toggles, working-state+dirty-diff+partial-PATCH save, renders the panel) + wiring (the AdminShell **Config tab**, the `/admin/config` route, a `config.update`-gated TopBar **Administration** entry). **Load-bearing (the spec-validation MAJOR):** the page no-access gate keys on the GET `/admin/config` **403 forbidden flag** (the `ProcessesAdmin`/`CapaBoardPage` precedent), NOT `usePermissions().can()` — a `can()` page-gate FLASHES NoAccessState to a legit admin on a cold `/admin` deep-link (the `/admin` subtree never warms the `/me/permissions` cache); `ConfigAdmin` imports NO `usePermissions`, while TopBar uses it ONLY for affordance-hiding (a transient `false` merely hides the menu item — harmless). `pending_now` mirrors the drain's real claim predicate (`PENDING & (next_attempt_at IS NULL OR ≤ now())`); `pending_scheduled` is its exact complement. `recent_failures` is operational-only (recipient + `last_error` = the SMTP error `str(exc)[:1000]`, R32-safe — a near-subset of the existing `system.email_delivery_failed` admin payload), rendered as a React text node. The Failed count carries the danger glyph `✕` + AA `--es-danger-text` ONLY when `>0` (DP-5) with a dedicated ZERO-count negative test (anti-tautology). The integration-test cleanup **UPDATE-neutralizes** the seeded rows (status→SENT / fanned_out_at→now), NOT `DELETE` — the app role has `REVOKE DELETE` on `notification_email`/`awareness_event` (0063/0066). **Owner forks (AskUserQuestion ×4):** Moderate health depth (not Minimal/Rich) · notification flags only (not all 6 org-config toggles) · read-only panel (no requeue) · add the TopBar admin entry (not URL-only).

**Process:** spec → a 6-lens pre-code spec-validation workflow (authz/cross-org/leak · query-correctness · contract/scope ALL clean with zero findings; the MAJOR data-403 finding + 2 minor + 2 nit folded) → a 7-task subagent-driven build (each TDD + task-reviewed) → a 3-lens final whole-branch review (opus whole-branch **Ready-to-merge YES** · diff-critic **CLEAN** · web-test-trap **1 MAJOR + 1 MINOR**, both test-infra: the DEFAULT PATCH `/admin/config` MSW handler echoed the request body → de-echoed to return the static fixture [a module-level mutable `current` would LEAK across tests — `resetHandlers` doesn't reset base-handler closures]; the AdminShell tab test asserted mere existence → `{ selected: true }`) → the full `/check-api` (caught + fixed an E501 + 2 unformatted test files the per-task per-file runs missed) + `/check-web` + `/check-contracts` all green → a live-smoke verified end-to-end. ⚠ **Codex round 1 — 3 P2s, ALL genuine + ALL fixed (`4030291`):** (1) the `config.update`-gated Admin entry linked to `/admin` whose index redirects to `/admin/users` (a `user.read`-gated page) → relink to `/admin/config` (open the surface the gate authorizes); (2) the health aggregate's WHERE filtered only `org_id` with all status checks inside `FILTER`s → it scanned the org's ever-growing `SENT` history (the `(status,next_attempt_at)` index can't help while status isn't a WHERE predicate) → add `status IN (FAILED,PENDING,SUPPRESSED)` to the WHERE (lossless — no bucket counts SENT — and lets the index skip sent rows); (3) the awareness backlog rendered only the count → add the oldest-pending age line. All 3 threads resolved; Codex 👍-approved.

**Live-smoke (dev stack, org ORG_EXAMPLE, `demo` login):** the rebuilt web+api images served the slice; the new TopBar **Administration** entry rendered (gated `config.update`, granted to all ORG_EXAMPLE users) → the **Config** tab → `/admin/config`; both toggles reflected the real config (email OFF, pierce ON), Save disabled-until-dirty; a seeded FAILED `notification_email` lit **Failed: ✕ 1** + the recent-failures row (recipient/error/attempts/relative-when), and a seeded PENDING row drained → **Suppressed: 1** (org email off — the panel reads real engine-processed state); the email-off banner showed; the **save round-trip** (toggle→Save→"Saved."→dirty-reset, both directions) PERSISTED across a full reload (server-side), and flipping the toggle hid/showed the banner (the dual `["notification-health"]` invalidation, live); bonus — S-deeplink-login preserved `/admin/config` through the full-reload SSO round-trip. **Named residuals (not faked):** a **requeue/redeliver** action for FAILED emails (read-only for 5b) · the other 4 org-config flags in the UI (own slice) · **rich delivery stats** (latency histogram, per-`last_error` distribution, digest backlog, last-drain timestamp) · a more-inclusive admin-nav gate (mostly moot now the entry links to `/admin/config`) · **5c (SSE)** replacing the slice-2 60s bell poll (the LAST Notification-family slice) · the slice-4 timer-sweep claim-threshold filter (unrelated). **Tests:** api unit **996 (unchanged — the new BE tests are integration)** + 3 new integration tests (`test_notification_health` [delta + org-isolation, UPDATE-neutralized cleanup] · `test_admin_notifications_health` [200 for a `config.update` holder / 403 without]); web **1261→1273** (+12: NotificationHealthPanel 5 [glyph ±, empty, email-off, error] + ConfigAdmin 4 [reflect+axe, save-partial-PATCH+stays, no-access-without-probe, load-error] + AdminShell Config-tab 1 + TopBar shown/hidden 2). (S-notify-5b, FE-heavy + 1 read-only BE endpoint, NO migration [head `0066`], NO new key [catalog 102], api unit 996 unchanged +3 integration, web 1261→1273, PR #283 squash `45dff1e`.)

### S-notify-5a — awareness events: `doc.released`, read-scope-filtered (slice 5a; BE-only; migration `0066`; PR #279 squash `7e5ffaf`)

**What shipped — the FIRST awareness event (doc 10 §9.2): a new Effective version (`doc.released`) now reaches exactly the users who can READ the document — a broadcast filtered by `document.read`, never a blast to all org members.** Architecture = a transactional **awareness outbox + read-scope fan-out worker** (the family's R53 "decouple the expensive 1→N off the release hot path" applied to a 1→N event): (1) a best-effort **SAVEPOINT emit hook** in the SERIALIZABLE `_cutover` (`services/vault/lifecycle.py`, after the RELEASED/SUPERSEDED audit, before the single commit) writes ONE `awareness_event` outbox row, atomic with the release; (2) a **`awareness_fan_out` Beat** (@120s) claims pending events (`FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` + `populate_existing`, fresh session per event, ONE commit per event, **NO per-event advisory lock** — the `outbox_drain` precedent), resolves the read-scoped audience, subtracts the actor (self-suppression), and creates per-recipient notification (+ digest/email) rows via the slice-3a machinery. **The crux = `services/authz/audience.py::resolve_document_readers`** — the per-user `gather_grants` + `authorize(document.read)` loop (deny-wins, ABAC-complete; a SQL join can't honor DENY overrides or time-windowed predicates), built on a new `services/authz/resource.py::build_document_resource_context` extracted from `api/documents._document_scope_by_id` (now a thin delegate; the api gate + the resolver share one builder; services never imports api). `source_ip=None` (the worker has no request IP) → an `ip_allow`-gated grant fails to match → fail-safe UNDER-inclusion; the resolver NEVER over-includes. Migration **0066**: an `awareness_event` outbox table (**REVOKE DELETE** — operational, INSERT/SELECT/UPDATE only — the 0010 ALTER-DEFAULT-PRIVILEGES trap) + a nullable `notification.subject_version_id` column + two migration-managed partial indexes (`ix_awareness_event_pending` WHERE `fanned_out_at IS NULL`; `uq_notification_dedup_awareness` on `(recipient,event_key,subject_type,subject_id,subject_version_id)` WHERE `task_id IS NULL` — both in `env.py`'s `_MIGRATION_MANAGED_INDEXES`, absent from the ORM) + the seeded `doc.released` template (raw `ON CONFLICT … WHERE is_effective`, NOT-EXISTS-guarded downgrade). **NO new permission key** (R38, catalog stays **102** — the resolver only *consumes* `document.read`), **NO web/openapi change**. The welded task path (`dispatch._enqueue_one`) was kept behaviour-identical via a DRY-extracted shared `resolve_delivery` helper (the slice-1..4 `test_notification_*` suites are the parity backstop — 60 unmoved). **Owner decisions (AskUserQuestion):** fire `doc.released` for ALL FSM releases incl. OBJ/MR + the singleton register heads (read-scope keeps each relevant); the re-release dedup fix = a **`subject_version_id` discriminator**; the send-time read filter = an accepted **R32-bounded fan-out-time residual** (no send-time re-check).

⚠ **A pre-code SPEC-VALIDATION workflow (8 agents: code-anchor verifiers + an adversarial refute panel) caught 3 real design flaws BEFORE any code** — the headline being the **version-LESS dedup**: the originally-specced `(recipient,event_key,subject_type,subject_id)` key (no version) would SILENTLY suppress every RE-RELEASE (the same doc_id re-enters `release()`/`_cutover`; the surviving release-#1 notification row dedups release-#2) — WORST for the singleton register heads (RSK/CTX/IPR) + OBJ/MR which republish through one stable head id → notified ONCE EVER → fixed by the `subject_version_id` discriminator so each new Effective version re-notifies; plus the **SERIALIZABLE-aware swallow** (`record_awareness_event` runs inside the SERIALIZABLE `_cutover`, NOT the Read-Committed task-creation txn the family's `enqueue_task_notifications` assumes — a blanket swallow of a `40001/40P01/23505` would mask a serialization conflict + turn a clean 409 into a 500 → it RE-RAISES exactly `_is_race_loss`'s SQLSTATE set so the race loser gets the clean 409, swallows only non-serialization); and the §4/§9.2 wording downgraded from "send-time-accurate" to fan-out-time. ⚠ **The final 4-lens whole-branch review (migration-reviewer + diff-critic + opus whole-branch + worker-safety/authz) probed the four crux mechanisms — no over-inclusion · exactly-once fan-out · R53 SERIALIZABLE emit · version-discriminated dedup — and ALL HELD; it found ONE real defect the per-task reviews missed: the fan-out template-miss path `await session.rollback()` THEN read `event.event_key` → the S-ing-4 `MissingGreenlet` trap (rollback expires the ORM instance → a synchronous lazy-refresh on an async session) — fixed by capturing the scalars into locals + dropping the rollback (reads-only branch; the lock releases on context exit, `fanned_out_at` stays NULL → re-claimed), mirroring the escalation no-template precedent, + a regression test that was green-suite-invisible before.** ⚠ **Codex — 2 P2, BOTH genuine + fixed** (round 2 = 👍): **P2#1** the per-recipient template-miss TOCTOU (the pre-loop probe covers probe-time absence, but if the template vanishes mid-loop `enqueue_awareness_one` returns `no_template` and the loop ignored it → still stamped → missed recipients never retried) → the loop now treats `no_template` as a retry (return without commit/stamp → re-claimed); **P2#2** the 3a digest renderer grouped by `(event_key, subject_id)` only → two Effective versions of one doc in a digest window collapse to the first title `×N`, omitting the later version → the grouping key += `subject_version_id` (NULL-safe → task-row digests unchanged).

**Live-smoke (BE-driven, dev stack org ORG_EXAMPLE):** the populated dev DB upgraded 0065→0066 (the real upgrade CI's fresh DB can't prove); the `awareness_fanout` Beat registered+scheduled; a real `doc.released` emit for IPR-GEN-001 (Rev A) → the REAL `fan_out_awareness` → `{events:1, notifications:3}` (Mara/Priya/Demo, title "IPR-GEN-001 Rev A is now Effective" — template + version-label SUBSTITUTED, deep_link `/documents/{id}`, `task_id=None`, `subject_version_id` set); VERIFIED actor Ken **self-suppressed**, every recipient a `document.read` holder, idempotency (2nd sweep `{events:0,notifications:0}`). (All 4 dev users hold `document.read` on the head → the non-reader EXCLUSION is e2e-covered not shown live; the self-suppression exclusion IS shown live.) **Named residuals (not faked):** the spec-§11 resolver edge-case tests (PROCESS/FOLDER/time-windowed/`ip_allow` — de-risked: the resolver reuses the shared request-path PDP, the deny-wins backstop IS tested) · a dedicated concurrent-fan-out test + a serialization-injected-at-INSERT test (idempotency + the SERIALIZABLE re-raise are structurally proven + naturally-raced/unit-covered) · the unused `_pending_event_ids(now)` (the deferred slice-4 claim-threshold filter) · `_is_serialization_error` vs `_is_race_loss` not DRY (agree under psycopg3) · imported-baseline awareness (the ingestion path bypasses `_cutover` — a deliberate exclusion) · the **remaining awareness event keys** (`doc.approved`/`doc.obsoleted`/`dcr.raised`/`audit.scheduled`/`finding.assigned`/`capa.stage_changed`/`mr.scheduled` — these rails carry them, several with non-read-scope role/stakeholder audiences) · **5b** (the Health delivery-failure panel + the net-new admin Config tab) · **5c** (SSE replacing the slice-2 60s bell poll). **Tests:** api unit **988→996** (+4 `_is_serialization_error` classifier, +2 Beat-registration, +2 digest-version-grouping render) + the awareness integration suite (`test_audience_resolver` [deny-wins/SYSTEM/inactive/self-suppression], `test_authz_resource_extraction` [parity], `test_awareness_enqueue` [created/deduped/re-release], `test_awareness_emit` [one-event + concurrent-release 409 + version-id], `test_awareness_e2e` [read-scope filter + self-suppression + idempotency + re-release + org-off + template-miss]); web unchanged (**1261**). (S-notify-5a, BE-only, migration **0066** [head `0065`→`0066`], api unit 988→996, web 1261, PR #279 squash `7e5ffaf`.)

### S-notify-4 — escalation timers: reminders + overdue + escalate-to-manager (slice 4; BE-only; migration `0065`; PR #276 squash `6c41c90`)

**What shipped — the durable `timer_sweep` Beat (doc 10 §9.5, R29): off a `Task`'s `due_at` + an `SlaPolicy` it sends a pre-due REMINDER (`task.due_soon`), marks/notifies OVERDUE (`task.overdue` — which finally makes the S-notify-3a quiet-hours PIERCE reachable end-to-end, the named 3a/3b deferral), and ESCALATES an overdue workflow task to the assignee's manager (`task.escalated`, QMS-Owner fallback), idempotent + audited.** Migration **0065**: a new `sla_policy` table (keyed by `TaskType`, INTERVAL reminder/escalate offsets, **SELECT-only for the app role** — REVOKE the `0010` ALTER-DEFAULT-PRIVILEGES auto-grant since it's a TABLE — seed-managed reference data, one active row per type) + 4 nullable task timer-stamp columns (`remind_1_sent_at`/`remind_2_sent_at`/`overdue_notified_at`/`escalated_1_at`, the idempotency guard) + a migration-managed partial index `ix_task_timer_pending` (in `env.py`'s `_MIGRATION_MANAGED_INDEXES`, absent from the ORM) + an additive `ALTER TYPE event_type ADD VALUE TASK_ESCALATED` (autocommit_block, appended-last → auto in `EVENT_TYPE_VALUES`) + seeds (12 policies + 3 `en` templates). **NO new permission key** (R38, catalog stays **102**), **NO web/openapi change** (BE-only). **The sweep mirrors the 3a digest-sweep safety VERBATIM** (`services/notifications/escalation.py`): pure step-math in `timer.py::due_steps` (threshold-passed AND stamp-null, chronological, OVERDUE always-on at `due_at`); `process_task_timers` = a per-task `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext(task_id))` → `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED` + `populate_existing=True` [the S-drift-1 stale-attr trap] → per step: reminders/overdue via `resolve_recipients`, escalate via `resolve_escalation_recipients` (manager → QMS-Owner holders → []) + a `_recipient_for_user` builder + ONE `TASK_ESCALATED` `AuditEvent` (`actor_type=system`, `object_type=workflow_instance`, `scope_ref=task.id`, hashes NULL → the `audit.chain_link` Beat) + the stamp — **all in ONE per-task commit** (atomic stamp+enqueue+audit → idempotent under `task_acks_late` redelivery + concurrent sweeps); `sweep_task_timers` = a fresh session per task + per-task try/except. **N9 (locked): the sweep ONLY reminds/notifies/escalates — NO auto-decide, NO auto-reassign, NO task-state flip** (the `escalated_1_at` stamp is the only "already-escalated" marker). Wall-clock (the `working_calendar` business-day refinement is a documented R29 reconcile-defer; the offsets are INTERVAL so it slots in with no schema change). Beat `easysynq.notifications.timer_sweep` @300s (registered in `tasks/app.py` + a task-registration unit test). **Owner decisions (AskUserQuestion ×2, review-surfaced):** ONE pre-due reminder for the MVP (`remind_2_before` seeded NULL — a distinct second reminder is a named follow-up); WORKFLOW tasks only escalate (`escalate_1_after` NULL for personal `DOC_ACK` + `PERIODIC_REVIEW` — they get reminders/overdue but don't ping a manager).

⚠ **The 4-reviewer final whole-branch pass (migration-reviewer · diff-critic · opus · worker-safety) found two real bugs the per-task reviews' fixtures had MASKED:** (1) both pre-due reminders shared `event_key=task.due_soon`, so the notification dedup partial-unique `(recipient, task, event_key)` COLLAPSED the second (`ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` → no row, no email) yet the step still stamped → on the default seed `remind_2` DELIVERED NOTHING; the test had PRE-STAMPED `remind_2` (a false-PASS that hid it) → owner-resolved to the one-reminder MVP; (2) the seed escalated ALL 12 task types incl. personal `DOC_ACK`/`PERIODIC_REVIEW` to managers → owner-resolved to workflow-tasks-only. ⚠ **a CI-BLIND downgrade abort** (migration-reviewer + diff-critic): the downgrade `DELETE FROM notification_template` aborts on a POPULATED DB once the sweep has run (`notification.template_id` RESTRICT FK) — the fresh-DB round-trip is GREEN → fixed with a `NOT EXISTS` guard (the 0023 precedent). ⚠ worker-safety verified the 2-session `asyncio.gather` concurrency test would genuinely FAIL without the advisory lock (escalates exactly once). **Codex — 4 ROUNDS, 14 findings, ALL genuine + ALL fixed** (a migration+worker slice does NOT converge — the engineering-patterns rhythm; the owner chose to "merge at end of round 4"): **R1** (multi-org seed loop · cross-org manager → QM · no-stamp-on-template-miss · audit `escalated_to`=notified-only · re-upgrade-safe `ON CONFLICT` templates); **R2** (FREEZE the migration's task-types to a frozen tuple — a from-scratch replay with newer code would seed a future `TaskType` the DB enum lacks · stamp a no-recipient TERMINAL step via `should_stamp = notified or attempted==0`, else infinite re-claim · keep the IN-APP row for a no-email active recipient — only the email SEND needs an address · org-check the QM FALLBACK too, not just the manager); **R3** (reject a SELF-manager `manager_id==assignee.id` → QM · a DEDUP means the notification EXISTS — `_enqueue_one`'s dedup branch returns emitted, not a delivery-miss; only a template-miss retries — else infinite re-claim after a 0065 downgrade→re-upgrade drops/recreates the stamps); **R4** (write the `TASK_ESCALATED` audit ONLY for a genuinely-NEW escalation — `_enqueue_one`→a 3-state `created/deduped/no_template`; audit on `created_ids`, stamp on `exists_ids`, so a deduped re-sweep stamps WITHOUT a 2nd WORM audit row · correct `via=qm_fallback` for the self-manager-is-sole-QM edge). ⚠ **a CI-RED I introduced** (the R2-4 cross-org test): it created a 2nd `Organization` without cleanup → `test_restore.py`'s `select(Organization.id).scalar_one()` aborted `MultipleResultsFound` once the data-driven shard rebalance landed it after my test → fixed with FK-ordered org cleanup in a `finally` (the don't-pollute-the-shared-DB rule; the offending test is in OUR file, not `test_restore`).

**Live-smoke (BE-driven, slice 4 has no UI):** migration 0065 applied cleanly to the POPULATED dev DB (0064→0065 — the real upgrade CI's fresh DB can't prove); the per-type seed verified (12 policies, `remind_2` NULL all, `escalate` NULL only for DOC_ACK+PERIODIC_REVIEW, 1d for the 10 workflow types); a real `timer_sweep()` on a 2-day-overdue APPROVE task → `{tasks:1, steps:3}` → notifications `task.due_soon`+`task.overdue`→assignee, `task.escalated`→manager (templates SUBSTITUTED, no literal `{{}}` — the slice-3b `VARIABLE_WHITELIST` lesson, live), a `TASK_ESCALATED` audit (`via=manager`, `escalated_to`=[manager], notified-only), stamps remind1+overdue+escalated set (remind2 NOT); the 2nd sweep `{tasks:1, steps:0}` (idempotent, no dup notification/audit). 📌 **The live-smoke SHARPENED a deferral:** with `remind_2` unused, `remind_2_sent_at IS NULL` is a TAUTOLOGY in `_due_task_ids`'s OR-of-4 claim + the `ix_task_timer_pending` predicate → every open due task is re-claimed each sweep (a 0-step no-op; correctness-safe + bounded — this is the deferred Codex P2 "claim-threshold filter"; v1.x should drop the `remind_2` disjunct or remove the unused column). **Named residuals (not faked):** the **claim-threshold filter** (above) · `escalate_2` (reassign / NEEDS_ATTENTION) · `working_calendar` (business-day SLAs) · `capa.overdue` · the escalation/Health **UI + SSE (slice 5)** · the distinct **second reminder** · flipping the task state to `ESCALATED`. **Tests:** api unit **980→988** (+timer step-math + task-registration); +the `test_notification_timer_sweep` + `test_notification_escalation` integration suites (idempotency · the 2-session concurrency · no-email→in-app · no-recipient→stamped+no-reprocess · cross-org→filtered · self-manager→QM · dedup→stamped-no-2nd-audit · template-miss→no-stamp guard); web unchanged (**1261**). (S-notify-4, BE-only, migration **0065** [head `0064`→`0065`], api unit 980→988, web 1261, PR #276 squash `6c41c90`.)

### S-notify-3b — the preference matrix UI: per-class cadence + digest hour/timezone + quiet hours (slice 3b; FE-only; no migration [head `0064`]; PR #273 squash `9d03390`)

Puts the face on the 3a digest engine — `/settings/notifications` is rebuilt from the slice-2 master-email stopgap into the full **per-event-class email-cadence matrix**, consuming the already-shipped (S-notify-3a) `GET/PUT /me/notification-preferences`. **No migration (head stays `0064`), no new permission key (catalog `102`, authenticated-self), NO contract/openapi change (the 3a schemas already exist), NO `apps/api` change.** The page is one form (local working-state + a `dirty` diff + a single partial-PUT Save): an in-app-vs-email **banner** ("Your in-app bell is always immediate — these settings control email only"), the master email kill-switch + an "email off" note, **"Email cadence by type"** = 4 stacked class rows (name + helper text + an `Immediate/Daily/Off` SegmentedControl; `admin_ops` tagged "in-app only today"; distinct `aria-label`s; categorical NEUTRAL styling, never RAG), and **"Daily digest" timing** = a 24-slot hour Select, a curated-shortlist + searchable IANA timezone Select, and a both-or-neither quiet-hours Switch + two `TextInput type="time"` + a soft "digest hour falls in quiet hours" note. Data layer: widened `lib/types` (`NotificationDigestMode`/`NotificationClass`/`NotificationPreferences`/`…Update`), `useUpdateNotificationPreferences` (partial PUT, invalidate `["notification-preferences"]`), new `classMeta.ts` + `timezones.ts` helpers, and a `satisfies`-pinned + **stateful** MSW. **4 owner forks (AskUserQuestion ×4):** defer the admin pierce-flag toggle to its own slice (there is NO admin-config FE surface at all today — `grep notifications_email_enabled` over `apps/web` = zero; AdminShell has only Users/Roles/Processes → the toggle needs a net-new Config tab); stacked class rows + SegmentedControl; curated shortlist + searchable all-zones; top banner + per-row helper copy.

**Build + review:** subagent-driven (3 TDD tasks, fresh implementer + spec/quality review per task: helpers on haiku, the page on sonnet) + a **6-reviewer final whole-branch pass** (`diff-critic` · `web-test-trap` · opus whole-branch · a11y/DP-5 · gating-scope · MSW-fidelity) + 2 fix waves. ⚠ **THE bug the per-task tests MISSED, caught by the final pass (diff-critic, empirically) + verified LIVE: the curated timezone resting list was DEAD in production** — Mantine v7 drives a CONTROLLED `searchValue` to the selected option's label ("UTC") on mount, so `tzData` always took the search branch and the user never saw the ~12 curated zones at rest; the `timezones.test.ts` helper test passed in isolation (classic false-PASS). FIX = `onDropdownOpen={() => setTzSearch("")}` (open resets the search → `tzData` returns `restingZones`) + a rest-state test. ⚠ **a test-driven `onClick` + imperative `tzRef.current.value=""` hack** (added to stop `userEvent.type` appending) was caught + removed — the TEST does `user.clear(tz)` instead (the house rule: never alter a production component to pass a test). ⚠ **the save path was over-built** — a dead optimistic `qc.setQueryData(merged)` (its structural-sharing rationale didn't hold + it made the "Saved." test hollow) → reverted to the spec's invalidate-only + made the save tests STATEFUL (a `statefulPrefs` GET+PUT sharing `let current` so the refetch models production) + a post-save "stays checked" assertion. ⚠ **the "redundant" Switch `aria-label` finding was empirically REJECTED** — removing it breaks the accessible name (Mantine v7 folds the `description` into the label's text node → name becomes label+description verbose); the `aria-label` IS the concise accessible name, kept. ⚠ **live-smoke caught an ENV gap (NOT a 3b bug):** the owner's dev stack ran the pre-3a backend (DB `0063`, the old `{email_enabled}`-only API) → the page white-screened on the old response shape (`TypeError reading 'action_required'`) → fixed by rebuilding `api`/`worker`/`beat` + applying `0064`; then verified live (matrix → effective view; save → PUT 200 → persisted across a hard reload; the curated timezone dropdown + a non-curated search). **Codex round 1: 3 P2s, 2 fixed + 1 false-alarm:** P2 #1 the load-error gate (`prefs.isError` must be checked BEFORE `isLoading || !working`, else a failed GET spins forever — `working` stays null on error; FIX + a regression test); P2 #2 the bare prod `QueryClient` (`main.tsx`) → `refetchOnWindowFocus`/`Reconnect` default true → a focus refetch could clobber unsaved edits → disabled both on the prefs query (post-save invalidate still resets); **P2 #3 (timezone aliases) = FALSE ALARM, adversarially verified vs the running backend** — `zoneinfo.available_timezones()` = 599 zones and INCLUDES `Asia/Calcutta`/`Europe/Kiev`/`America/Buenos_Aires` (the Intl set ⊂ the backend set → no 422); all 3 threads replied + resolved.

**Named residuals (not faked):** the **admin pierce-flag tab** (`notifications_escalation_pierce_quiet_hours` + the org `notifications_email_enabled` need a net-new `config.update`-gated AdminShell Config tab — its own slice, alongside slice 4) · **slice 4** escalation timers (`SlaPolicy`/`working_calendar`/`timer_sweep`) → the `critical`-class events that exercise the quiet-hours pierce end-to-end · **slice 5** awareness events + the Health delivery-failure panel + SSE (replacing the 60s poll) · **hourly/weekly cadences** (enum-reserved, not added) · a **robustness gap**: the page has no error boundary / defensive guard against a malformed prefs response (white-screens the whole app — can't happen in prod since api+web ship together). **Tests:** web **1246→1261 (+15)** (classMeta 3 · timezones 3 · the rebuilt `NotificationSettingsPage` suite: cadence/master/error-gate/disabled-save + the timing 5 + the curated-rest-state test); api unchanged (FE-only). (S-notify-3b, FE-only, no migration [head `0064`], web 1246→1261, api 980, PR #273 squash `9d03390`.)

### S-notify-3a — the digest engine: preferences + digests + quiet hours (slice 3a; BE-only; migration 0064; PR #261 squash `93b8a57`)

**What shipped — the BE notification DIGEST ENGINE (doc 10 §9.4, R54): per-user per-event-class email cadence + a daily-digest Beat + quiet hours, running off seeded code defaults (the matrix UI is the deferred 3b slice).** Migration **0064** (additive, **NO new table** → 0010's `ALTER DEFAULT PRIVILEGES` GRANT trap doesn't fire → no REVOKE block), **NO new permission key** (reads stay authenticated-self, catalog **102**), contract = the widened `/me/notification-preferences` schema + a `NotificationDigestMode` enum + the admin `notifications_escalation_pierce_quiet_hours` flag. **Owner forks (AskUserQuestion ×4):** split BE-first (engine now, matrix UI = 3b) · per **event-class** matrix (a code map, NOT per-event-type) · **org-gated escalation pierce built now** (predicate unit-tested; exercised end-to-end once slice-4 `*.overdue` events fire) · cadences = **Immediate/Daily/Off** only in v1 (hourly/weekly reserved). **Schema (0064):** 2 enums (`notification_digest_mode`, `notification_email_kind`); `notification_preference` += 4 per-class mode columns (NULL ⇒ the class code default) + `digest_hour`/`timezone`/`quiet_start`/`quiet_end`; `notification` += `digest_due_at`/`digested_at` + a partial index `ix_notification_digest_pending` (Codex); `notification_email` += `email_kind`/`recipient_user_id`/`item_count` with `notification_id` now nullable (partial-unique `WHERE notification_id IS NOT NULL`); `system_config` += the pierce flag; seeds the `digest.daily` template. **Engine:** an event-class code map (`services/notifications/classes.py`) → `action_required`/`awareness`/`critical`/`admin_ops` (defaults daily/daily/immediate/immediate); class-aware enqueue (`dispatch.py`: `immediate` → email now, held to `window_end` in quiet hours unless a critical pierce; `daily` → set `digest_due_at`, NO immediate email; `off` → in-app only — the in-app row is ALWAYS created, the mode governs EMAIL only); the hourly `notification_digest_sweep` Beat (`digest.py`: bundle a user's due rows into ONE summary email, idempotent via a `digested_at` stamp in the same txn as the email insert, **a per-user `pg_advisory_xact_lock`** + `FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED`, a fresh session per user [the MissingGreenlet guard], re-check eligibility at send-prep → stamp-without-email when ineligible; the existing `outbox_drain` sends it); `drain.py` resolves org/user from the email row's own columns for a digest (`notification_id IS NULL`) — sends, doesn't SUPPRESS, and `_emit_failure` uses a `digest:<id>` ref. **Fold-ins:** **DOC_ACK email switched on** (drop the slice-1 `subject_type != DOC_ACK` exclusion → bundled into the daily digest by the `action_required` default); **OBJ/MR subtype deep-link routing** (`subjects.py`: a DOCUMENT whose `document_type.code` is OBJ/MR → `/objectives/{id}` ÷ `/management-reviews/{id}` — both shared-PK subtypes — **after probing the satellite row exists**, else fall back to `/documents/{id}`). The preferences `GET` returns the effective view (NULL → code defaults); `PUT` is a partial, `model_fields_set`-driven, validated update (422 on bad class/mode/hour/timezone/time + value-level both-or-neither quiet hours).

⚠ **Behavior change (owner-confirmed, spec §13):** `action_required` defaults to **daily**, so `task.assigned` no longer emails immediately by default — the bell stays immediate, the email bundles into the daily digest (the §9.4 anti-overwhelm intent). Slice-1 regression tests were updated (opt a test user into IMMEDIATE, or assert `digest_due_at`); the obsolete unit DOC_ACK-suppression case was dropped. ⚠ **THE migration trap (CI-BLIND, caught by migration-reviewer + diff-critic, MISSED by the opus whole-branch + the Task-1 sonnet reviewer):** `op.create_check_constraint("ck_notification_preference_digest_hour", …)` passes the FULL name → the `ck_%(table_name)s_%(constraint_name)s` convention **re-tokenizes it into a DOUBLED, hash-truncated** `ck_notification_preference_ck_notification_preference_d_991e` ≠ the ORM's bare-token `ck_notification_preference_digest_hour`. `alembic check` does NOT compare CHECK names + the up↔down round-trip is wrong-both-ways → **GREEN despite the bug**; only a live `pg_constraint` query surfaces it. Fix = the **bare token** `"digest_hour"` in BOTH create + drop (matching the ORM). ⚠ **a migration-managed partial index** (the new `ix_notification_digest_pending` + the existing `uq_notification_email_one_per_notification`) must be in `env.py`'s `_MIGRATION_MANAGED_INDEXES` AND absent from the ORM model (the `uq_notification_dedup_task` precedent). ⚠ **a digest's `notification_id` is NULL** → `_still_eligible`/`_emit_failure` must resolve org/user from the email row, not `session.get(Notification, None)` (else the digest is wrongly SUPPRESSED). ⚠ **concurrent sweeps can SKIP-LOCKED-SPLIT one user's rows** → each sends a digest (sequential idempotency held, concurrent did NOT) → a per-user advisory lock serializes them. ⚠ **the prior OBJ/MR routing tests were FALSE-PASSING** — they created OBJ/MR docs via `POST /documents` (which never seeds the satellite) → they asserted the dedicated route on orphans the whole time; the satellite probe forced seeding the satellites directly + an orphan-fallback test.

**Build + review:** subagent-driven (10 tasks, fresh implementer + spec/quality review per task; 5 pure helpers on haiku, the integration/API tasks on sonnet) + a 3-way final whole-branch review (opus cross-task-coherence + `diff-critic` + `migration-reviewer`) that caught **2 real defects** (the CI-blind CHECK name; a stale DOC_ACK test passing only incidentally) — both fixed + verified (live `pg_constraint` = the single name). **Codex: 2 rounds, 7 P2s, ALL genuine + ALL fixed** (r1: one-sided quiet-hour update, the missing sweep index, the first-write upsert race, a blank-display-name `IndexError` aborting the whole sweep; r2: empty-string quiet hours, the OBJ/MR orphan-reroute 404, the concurrent-sweep double-send) — each re-verified by diff-critic/migration-reviewer + replied/resolved on-thread; no defer, no loop (every round surfaced real issues). **Named residuals (not faked):** **S-notify-3b** (the per-class matrix UI on `/settings/notifications`) · **hourly/weekly cadences** (enum-reserved) · **slice 4** (`SlaPolicy`/`working_calendar`/escalation events → the `critical` class + end-to-end pierce) · **slice 5** (awareness-event emission + the Health delivery-failure panel + SSE) · the dead `Recipient.email_enabled` re-read (a harmless 2-file cleanup) · an MR-orphan-fallback test (symmetric to the shipped OBJ one). ⚠ A pre-existing `-k "notification or config"` batch `test_setup.py::test_configure_auth_*` ordering flake reproduces on `main` (CI shards apart) — NOT this slice. **Tests:** api unit **956→980 (+24)** (classes 6 · preferences 2 · quiet 6 · schedule 4 · digest-render 2 · subtype-deeplink 3 · task-registration +1, minus the dropped DOC_ACK unit case) + new integration (`test_notification_digest.py` enqueue/sweep/drain/concurrency, `test_notification_preferences_api.py` ×~16, `test_notification_subtype_routing.py` OBJ/MR/plain/orphan); web unchanged (**1246**). **R53 was backfilled into the decisions-register with R54** (S-notify-1 recorded it only in its spec §13). (S-notify-3a, BE-only, migration **0064** [head 0063→0064], api unit 956→980, web 1246, PR #261 squash `93b8a57`.)

### S-deeplink-login — preserve the requested path through the Keycloak login round-trip (FE-only; PR #259 squash `61dc442`)

**What shipped — closes the Codex #257 P2 (the named Notification-track follow-up): a logged-out deep-link now lands on its route after sign-in, not Home.
