- **S-render-1** (detect externally-linked Office resources before render → R26 source-only, then adopt gotenberg 8.34) — **closes a WORM/mirror integrity hazard the 8.34 bump introduces.** **Why:** gotenberg 8.34 changed LibreOffice conversion so external/local **linked** resources (`http(s)://`, `file://`, UNC `\\host\share`, drive/POSIX-absolute, and external-mode *relative* targets) still convert (HTTP 200) but are **no longer rendered into the PDF**. The worker (`GotenbergRenderSink`) caches any successful convert as the mirror's CONTROLLED COPY, so a doc referencing a linked logo/include/picture would silently cache an **INCOMPLETE** controlled copy — a violation of "the controlled copy is a faithful rendition of the controlled source." So before any convert, the worker runs a structural scan; a linked source is marked **non-renderable (R26, doc 04 §11.4)** → the mirror keeps source bytes + `no_controlled_rendition` instead of a lossy PDF. The gotenberg 8.34 adoption (declined earlier as Dependabot #157, pending exactly this guard) ships in the SAME PR. **100% BACKEND-ONLY** — **no migration** (head stays `0052`), **no new permission key / endpoint / OpenAPI contract** (gate = `/check-api` + `/check-migrations` n/a). **What shipped:** a NEW pure-stdlib module `services/vault/linked_resources.py::scan_linked_resources(mime, source_bytes) -> LinkScan(has_external_links, reason)` (mirrors the `domain/ingestion/minhash.py` house rules: **pure / no-I/O / stdlib-only** [`zipfile`/`re`/`xml.etree`] / **bounded** [8 MB member cap, 4 MiB RTF/OLE prefix, 512-member + 64 MB zip budgets] / **ReDoS-safe** [no nested quantifiers, anchored bounded classes] / **XXE-closed** [refuses any `<!DOCTYPE` before the default non-fetching expat parser — no defusedxml, D4] / **fail-open** [any parse error → `LinkScan(False)`, defer to the normal render path]). Routing: RTF + legacy-OLE by mime; **OOXML/ODF by CONTAINER CONTENT** (a top-level `content.xml` → ODF scan; `[Content_Types].xml`/any `*.rels` → OOXML scan — NOT a fixed mime allowlist, so every `.dotx/.xltx/.potx/.ppsx`/`.ott/.ots/.otp/.odg` variant the render path converts is inspected). **Hyperlinks are EXCLUDED** (OOXML `Type=.../hyperlink`, ODF `text:a`/`draw:a`, RTF `HYPERLINK` command) — 8.34 renders a clickable annotation fine, and flagging them would source-only nearly every real document. **Doctrine direction:** a false-positive is SAFE (→ R26 source-only, source always retained); a false-negative is the hazard (an incomplete cached copy). **Wiring (threading unchanged across all rounds):** scan runs in `GotenbergRenderSink.render` AFTER `_is_non_renderable`, BEFORE `_convert`; `RenderResult.reason` (new field) → `_resolve_rendition`'s 5-tuple → `mirror._metadata` writes `no_controlled_rendition_reason` into `metadata.json` for the auditor. The api process holds the no-op `LoggingRenderSink`, so this is worker/CLI-only (the S-dcr-3b rendering-is-worker-only invariant). **The 6-round Codex saga (the load-bearing story):** an inherently-imperfect heuristic over UNTRUSTED Office formats kept surfacing safe-direction edges — Codex findings ran **7→6→3→6→3→5 across rounds 1–6** (severity decaying P1→P2), and round-6 was 4/5 RE-RAISES of already-decided dispositions. The fixes that landed: hyperlinks excluded (r1); 7 false-negatives closed [INCLUDEPICTURE/INCLUDETEXT match anywhere, LINK only in field-context, case-insensitive, External-mode relative targets, variant mimes] (r2); **parse-and-concatenate split `<w:instrText>` runs** [Word splits `INCLUDE`+`PICTURE` across adjacent runs → a raw-text regex misses it] + ODF embedded-subdocument dir-prefix membership [`./Object 1` stored as `Object 1/content.xml`] + footnote/endnote/comment story parts (r3); **route ODF by top-level `content.xml` first** + scan `.rels` before the budget break + **bounded RTF control words** between `\fldinst` and the command [`\fldinst \lang1033\rtlch INCLUDEPICTURE`] (r4); **independent per-category member budgets** [rels vs field-code parts each get `_MAX_ZIP_MEMBERS` — fixes the symmetric starvation the r4 rels-first sort introduced] (r5); **scan BOTH ODF+OOXML when both zip fingerprints present** [OR-ed, short-circuit — a crafted OOXML with a dummy `content.xml` no longer bypasses its `.rels` scan; supersedes the r4 either/or routing] (r6). ⚠ **Traps a future session MUST carry:** (1) **Codex caught ~17 real scanner edge-cases across 6 rounds that CLEAN diff-critic passes missed** — on a heuristic over untrusted/adversarial input, the diff-critic is necessary but NOT sufficient; the adversarial PR reviewer is the real edge-finder. (2) **It does not converge** — past ~round 4 Codex re-raises the same documented residuals (incl. the owner's own fail-open tradeoffs framed as findings); the discipline is to TRIAGE (fix genuinely-new high-value, respond/defer the rest) and ASK THE OWNER for the merge decision rather than loop. (3) **the auto-formatter strips a just-added unused import** — `import pytest` added BEFORE its first `@pytest.mark.parametrize` use was removed by the PostToolUse ruff hook; add the usage first or re-add after (the recurring S-statusbadge-2 trap). (4) **rels-first ordering is a half-fix** — preferring the most-reliable signal under a SHARED budget starves the other category; the right shape is INDEPENDENT per-category budgets. **Owner decisions:** (a) **KEEP FAIL-OPEN** for a VALID zip whose part is uninspectable (DOCTYPE-bearing or oversized member) or a truncated RTF/legacy prefix — explicitly chosen over fail-closed (D1 trusted authors; source always retained → worst case is an incomplete COPY for an uncommon shape, never lost content; a fail-closed switch stays an easy follow-up). (b) **fix-high-value / defer-rest** triage for rounds 4–6. **Deferred (named-not-faked) → v1.x hardening:** RTF/legacy >4 MiB streaming/chunked scan with overlap (a marker after a huge embedded-hex picture is missed); a ZIP entry-enumeration cap (stdlib `ZipFile` parses the whole central directory on open regardless, so capping `namelist()` can't reduce the dominant cost without a custom incremental reader — D4); a legacy-OLE **drive-path** (`C:\…`) marker (a raw-byte marker would false-positive on the ubiquitous embedded template/author paths every `.doc` carries → needs structural CFB/OLE2 moniker parsing). **By-design (not a bug):** cached-rendition recheck — a cached `rendition_blob_sha256` was produced by the pre-8.34 gotenberg (complete copy), and the single-host Compose deploy (D4) restarts the image + scanner together (no rollout window), so a cache hit correctly returns a complete copy; only a cache MISS runs 8.34 + the scan. **Test delta: +55 api unit** (new `test_linked_resources.py` = **53**: OOXML/ODF/RTF/legacy-OLE + variant mimes + corrupt/fail-open/bounded + every round's regression; **+2** `test_render.py` short-circuit/clean-convert) **+ 1 integration** (`test_render.py` linked-Office → `no_controlled_rendition`); **web unchanged** (backend-only). Full unit `800 passed / 18 skipped` (Windows; 17 POSIX skips + 1 release-ceremony). **diff-critic CLEAN** on rounds 3 & 4 (round-5/6 self-verified + Codex-reviewed). **Merge gate:** all Codex threads resolved + 0 unresolved + green CI (11/11) + owner's explicit go. PR [#180](https://github.com/CoJoA13/EasySynQ/pull/180), squash `97f4192`. (Rides atop the merged Phase-2 design-critique work + the fully-drained Dependabot queue; gotenberg 8.34 was declined as Dependabot #157 pending this slice.)

## v1.x DESIGN-CRITIQUE remediation (whole-app `/impeccable` P1s, baseline 26/40) — systematising the slice-by-slice SPA

- **S-design-p4** (the design-critique **P2/P3 polish tail** — the named follow-up S-design-p3 left open: the compact inline-state primitive + the Skeleton-list loader for the deferred idiom families, plus the residual Drift "Failing" glyph + the as-of-clock consistency gap) — **100% FRONT-END-ONLY** — no migration (head stays `0055`), no key/endpoint/contract; gate = `/check-web`. **Three new `lib/states.tsx` primitives (siblings of the four page-level ones from S-design-p3):** **`InlineState({kind, children, onRetry?})`** — the compact in-tab / in-drawer / in-card `<Text size="sm">` idiom (`loading`→`role="status"` dimmed live region, `forbidden`/`empty`→dimmed, `error`→red + an OPTIONAL inline "Try again" mirroring `ErrorState.onRetry`); **`SkeletonList({rows, height, gap?, label?, role?})`** — N stacked `<Skeleton>` rows for table loaders, ALWAYS a named `role="status"` region (an `aria-label` on a roleless container is an axe `aria-prohibited-attr` violation — hanging it off `role="status"` FIXES the prior nameless `LibraryPage`/`IngestionRunsPage` loaders); **`MutationErrorState({title, error, fallback?})`** — unwraps `ApiError.message` centrally for the calm red `<Alert>` on a failed write (the write-side sibling of `ErrorState`). **Sweeps (size-faithful — the load-bearing discriminator):** `InlineState` forces `size="sm"`, so it was adopted **ONLY where the original was already `size="sm"`** (in-tab/drawer/card/modal) — **8 sites:** `document/HistoryTab`, `RedlineViewer`, `WhereUsedTab`, `ApprovalsTab`, `VisualDiffViewer` (its inline 403/error guards ONLY — the Pending/Failed long-op render card stays bespoke), `AcknowledgementsTab`, `dcr/DcrImpactTable`, `dcr/ImplementCreateDcrModal`. `SkeletonList` → **3:** `library/LibraryPage`, `ingestion/TriageTable`, `ingestion/IngestionRunsPage`. `MutationErrorState` → **5 audits:** `CorrectFindingModal`, `ProgramForm`, `PlanForm`, `NewAuditModal` (ONLY the `create.isError` block; the sibling `programs.isError` read-Alert left as-is), `LogFindingModal`. **Two deliberate consistency fixes (the only behaviour changes):** `AcknowledgementsTab`'s matrix non-forbidden load error went dimmed→red (`kind="error"`) to match its sibling `dist` error which was already red; its two "Loading…" texts went default-size→`size="sm"` to match their `size="sm"` siblings. **Drift DP-5:** `DriftStatusPage`'s `Failing: {n}` count now carries the canonical danger **✕** glyph (`aria-hidden`, on the AA `--es-danger-text` token per the S-obj-rag dark-glyph rule — a glyph filled with the base hue would be the low-contrast mark) when `n>0`, legible by shape + label not colour alone; neutral at 0. The prominent red Alert above it is unchanged. **As-of clocks:** `<AsOf at={dataUpdatedAt} />` added beneath the page header (mirroring `ObjectivesRegisterPage`) to the **9 register pages** that lacked one — `AuditsListPage`, `ImprovementRegisterPage`, `DcrsRegisterPage`, `ManagementReviewsRegisterPage`, `NcrsPage`, `ComplaintsPage`, `CapaBoardPage`, `SupersededCopiesPage`, `IngestionRunsPage` — the hooks already spread `...query`, so `dataUpdatedAt` was present with **NO hook changes**. **Owner forks (AskUserQuestion up-front):** fold in ALL 9 register pages for as-of (vs a 3-page subset/defer); INCLUDE a shared `MutationErrorState` (vs defer the write-error family). **⚠ Traps a future session MUST carry:** (1) **the size-fidelity boundary IS the adopt/skip discriminator** — `InlineState` is `size="sm"`, so adopting it on a page-level default-size `<Text c="dimmed/red">` (the `/tasks`+`/acks` inboxes, `DcrDiffPage`, the `LibraryPage` error line) would SHRINK page-level text — the S-design-p3 "preserve sizes" rule applied as the gate; those stay as-is (deferred-as-idiom-mismatch). (2) **the Drift failing-glyph COLLIDED with the existing tests** — the default `driftStatusFixture` has `failing>0` AND a `DIVERGENT` badge, so adding a 2nd danger `✕` made `getByText(TONE_GLYPH.danger)` multi-match → re-scope the 2 existing badge-glyph assertions via `within(badge)` (the glyph is a `leftSection` span INSIDE the labelled `<Badge>`) and pin the new failing-count tests to a CONTROLLED fixture (both scans `CLEAN` / `BLOB_REHASH:null`) so the only `✕` is the count's. (3) `InlineState.onRetry` is **tested but unwired** in this sweep (a behaviour-preserving consolidation; the inline retry mirrors `ErrorState` and is there for future use) — diff-critic + web-test-trap both flagged it as a benign observation, not a defect. **Execution:** built the 3 primitives + their `states.test.tsx` tests MYSELF (single editor = no cross-partition drift; the primitives own their defaults, so no `EmptyState`-size-style trap can recur) and did the InlineState/SkeletonList/MutationErrorState/Drift sweeps inline; fanned out ONLY the 8 as-of edits to a **4-agent wave** (disjoint files, mechanical — each adds `dataUpdatedAt` + `<AsOf>`, self-checks scoped eslint), then reconciled + ran the full gate centrally. The UNDERSTAND phase was a **6-reader Workflow** mapping the deferred idiom families. **Deferred (named-not-faked):** the page-level default-size inbox text (`TasksInbox`/`AckInbox`) + `DcrDiffPage` + the `LibraryPage` error line (idiom + size mismatch — converting shrinks); **not-found** panels (intentionally gray + back-link, distinct from a retryable read error); the `DocumentDetailPage`/`VisualDiffViewer` bespoke COMPOSITE skeletons (header+grid+block / two-panel — a generic `SkeletonList` can't preserve the layout coherence); the `setError` imperative try/catch error sites (improvement/authoring/`SpawnDcrModal` — a different idiom storing a string, not a react-query `.isError`); `AuditLifecyclePanel`'s deliberately-orange advance error; the Home tile vocabulary (`TileNoAccess`/`TileSkeleton` — already idiomatic). **Test delta: web 1071→1080 (+9):** +7 states-primitive tests in `states.test.tsx` (InlineState per-kind / loading-role / retry-wired / no-retry; SkeletonList rows-in-named-region; MutationErrorState ApiError-unwrap / fallback; + the axe block extended) + 2 Drift failing-glyph tests in `DriftStatusPage.test.tsx` (✕ present `>0` / neutral `=0`); 2 existing Drift badge-glyph assertions re-scoped via `within()`. `/check-web` green (eslint + strict `tsc` + `vite build` + **1080/1080** via `--pool=forks --singleFork`); CI **11/11**. **diff-critic CLEAN** (all 31 conversions traced faithful; the 2 named fixes are the only behaviour changes; AsOf placement after every early-return; Drift glyph decorative + AA-token) **and web-test-trap-reviewer CLEAN** (every new assertion MUTATION-verified real, no false-PASS, no broken queries); **Codex CLEAN** ("Didn't find any major issues. Delightful!"). **Merge gate:** green CI + diff-critic/web-test-trap/Codex all clean + owner's explicit go. PR [#207](https://github.com/CoJoA13/EasySynQ/pull/207), squash `db499fc`. **The whole-app design-critique (baseline 26/40) is now essentially complete** — remaining is only the deferred idiom-mismatch families above (genuinely outside the primitives' fit).

- **S-design-p3** (the FINAL design-critique **Phase-3** remainder — closes the three named Phase-3 items the tracker still had open: **(A)** the shared loading/error/empty primitives + the real `useAckCount` silent-zero bug, **(B)** the PDCA-grouped nav rail + an in-product glyph legend, **(C)** the residual non-badge `▲`) — **100% FRONT-END-ONLY** — no migration (head stays `0055`), no key/endpoint/contract; gate = `/check-web`. **(A) shipped:** new `lib/states.tsx` — `LoadingState` (centered `role="status"` + an accessible name; the old bare `<Loader/>` announced nothing), `ErrorState` (red Alert **+ a "Try again" button** wired to the query's `refetch` — the affordance every hand-rolled error panel LACKED), `NoAccessState` (gray Alert, "No access" title OWNED by the primitive), `EmptyState` (dimmed body-size text + optional action). **Swept across 39 feature files / 85 sites**, each preserving its surrounding Container/Card/Title chrome + verbatim copy. The **silent-zero fix**: `useAckCount` returned `data?.length ?? 0` so a 403/network failure rendered a confident `0` (the bell read "no acks" identically to a genuine zero); it now returns `{count,isError,isLoading}` and the TopBar bell shows a **gray indeterminate dot + "Acknowledgements (count unavailable)"** on error, a numeric badge on >0, and stays silent on a genuine zero; the 2nd consumer (`home/DoCard`) is guarded the same way. **(B) shipped:** `LeftRail` regrouped by **PLAN/DO/CHECK/ACT** (each phase a labeled `role="group"`; every feature link keeps its exact gate/href/active-state; each phase's clause-filter links merged UNDER its heading — one label set, no duplication) + a new `lib/GlyphLegend.tsx` popover surfacing the canonical non-colour vocabulary (✓◔✕●○★ → meanings) sourced from a NEW single-source `TONE_MEANING` map in `lib/status.ts` (one vocabulary, no second drifting set). **(C) shipped:** `RunSummaryTiles` + `PreCommitChecklist` derive their glyphs from the canonical `TONE_GLYPH` on the **AA-tuned `--es-<tone>-text`** colours — `▲` retired (warn→◔); a glyph filled with the base hue would be the low-contrast amber-on-white mark (the S-obj-rag trap), so it rides the darker `-text` token. **Owner design-calls (AskUserQuestion up-front):** all three pieces bundled in ONE slice; the **FULL sweep** (vs incremental adoption); **DCR under ACT** (with CAPA+Improvement, vs the DO doc-control cluster); clause-filter links **merged into the phase headings** (vs a separate block). **Execution = a waved Workflow fan-out** (16 editor agents, one per feature dir). ⚠ **The first run fired all 16 at once → a transient server-side rate-limit ("temporarily limiting requests") killed EVERY agent with ZERO edits** — re-running in **waves of 4** (sequential waves, parallel within) cleared it; a 16-way burst of large-prompt agents is too much. Agents self-checked with **scoped eslint only** (NOT vitest — 16 concurrent vitest runs would thrash, per the full-run-thrash note); the orchestrator ran the full `/check-web` + reconciled cross-surface. ⚠ **The cross-partition consistency lesson REPEATS (orchestrator must reconcile):** the per-dir fan-out couldn't see that `EmptyState` forced `size="sm"` while the audits agent CORRECTLY deferred its empties to preserve their default-`md` look — 9 dirs adopted `sm`, audits stayed `md` (an inconsistency, and a hard-rule-#1 "preserve sizes" violation). Fix at the SINGLE source: `EmptyState` defaults to body size (md) so adoption never shrinks an existing empty, AND audits then adopts cleanly. **Deferred (named-not-faked, documented per-file):** the four PAGE-LEVEL primitives intentionally do NOT replace the compact inline **DP-6 text idiom** (a tab/drawer/card 403/error rendered as `<Text size="sm" c="dimmed/red">`, NOT an Alert panel), bespoke **Skeleton** loaders (`LibraryPage`/`DocumentDetailPage` shape their loaders like the table), the long-op **render card** (`VisualDiffViewer`), **mutation-error** Alerts (a `create.isError`, not a read), **not-found** panels (a distinct title + a back-action), or the Home **tile vocabulary** (`TileNoAccess`/`TileSkeleton` — compact tile bodies). 85 sites migrated, 82 deferred-as-idiom-mismatch → a clean v1.x follow-up could add a compact inline-state primitive + a Skeleton-list loader. **Review:** **diff-critic** 1 MINOR (the audits empties lost `mt="md"` while the sibling registers the sweep touched wrapped theirs in `<Box mt="md">`) → fixed; **web-test-trap-reviewer CLEAN**. ⚠ **Codex (post-PR) found 1 REAL P2 a CLEAN web-test-trap + the diff-critic both missed — the SAME silent-zero class the slice fixes, applied only HALF-way:** `DoCard`'s `allForbidden = dr.forbidden && ackCount === 0` still folded a FAILED ack read (count 0 on error) into a misleading `TileNoAccess` — I guarded the ack LINE on `ackError` but not the no-access DECISION → fix `0b4c235`: `allForbidden = dr.forbidden && !ackError && !ackLoading && ackCount === 0` (a forbidden-drift + failed-ack render now falls through to "Couldn't load this section.") + a regression test; **lesson — when you add an error discriminator, apply it to EVERY consumer of the old silent value, not just the obvious one.** **Test delta: web 1050→1071 (+21):** the 4 new lib/shell test files (`states`/`GlyphLegend`/`useAckCount`) + the TopBar bell error/zero arms + the LeftRail PDCA-grouping/legend tests + the `▲`-gone assertions + the DoCard Codex-fix arm. `/check-web` green (eslint + strict `tsc` + `vite build` + **1071/1071** via `--pool=forks --singleFork`); CI **11/11**; Codex re-review "Didn't find any major issues". **Merge gate:** the one Codex P2 fixed + thread answered + green CI + owner's explicit go. PR [#205](https://github.com/CoJoA13/EasySynQ/pull/205), squash `c070a43` (Codex fix `0b4c235`). **The design-critique Phase-3 remainder is now CLOSED** — remaining critique = P2/P3 polish only (a Skeleton-list / inline-state primitive, the deferred idiom families above).

- **S-obj-rag-legibility** (the objectives-RAG sub-pieces of design-critique **Phase 3** — the DP-5 colour-alone SHAPES tail of critique **#1** + the named clarify **#144** colour-word deferral) — **the objectives surfaces now carry the RAG MEANING + a single non-colour glyph everywhere RAG appears; closes the last colour-only RAG marks the StatusBadge migration (S-statusbadge-2) left in `ObjectiveTrendChart`/`BandPreview`/`CommitmentHero`.** **100% FRONT-END-ONLY** — no migration (head stays `0055`), no key/endpoint/contract; gate = `/check-web`. **What shipped:** `objectives/labels.ts` `RAG_LABEL` flips from the colour WORD (`Green`/`Amber`/`Red`/`Unmeasured`) to the MEANING (`On track`/`Needs attention`/`Action required`/`Not yet measured`; the first three match Home's `RAG_META.label` VERBATIM — one cross-surface vocabulary, so the same objective no longer reads "Green" on the register but "On track" on Home); a NEW `RAG_GLYPH` derived from the canonical `TONE_GLYPH` via `RAG_TONE` (✓/◔/✕/○ — ONE non-colour vocabulary, never a second drifting set); a NEW `RAG_SEVERITY` rank (red<amber<green<unmeasured) for the register's Status-column sort. The relabel auto-propagates to the register row pill + the RAG-filter `SegmentedControl` + `CommitmentHero`'s pill (all already ride `RAG_LABEL`); `ObjectiveScorecardBand` chips read "1 on track" not "1 green"; `ObjectiveTrendChart` point markers become a **RAG-coloured dot + the dark canonical glyph** in a `data-rag` `<g>` (colour rides the dot, the legible shape rides the glyph) + matching legend swatches; `BandPreview` zones gain the glyph in a white inset chip. `CommitmentHero`'s `<Progress>` bar is left **intentionally colour-only** (a magnitude fill — the shaped StatusBadge directly below it carries the status channel) with a rationale comment so a future critic doesn't re-flag it. **Owner design-calls (AskUserQuestion up-front):** reuse the canonical tone GLYPHS (✓◔✕○) as the non-colour channel everywhere (vs a distinct geometric circle/triangle/square set — chosen to keep ONE app-wide vocabulary, zero drift); `unmeasured`→"Not yet measured" (objectives-precise vs Home's generic "No data"); FULL sweep incl. the scorecard chips + the filter segments. **⚠ Codex was THE edge-finder across 3 rounds — a CLEAN diff-critic + web-test-trap-reviewer passed every round (this design class is an a11y/semantics edge-fest):** **R1 (1×P2):** the `BandPreview` glyph chips are `aria-hidden`, but the band's `role="img"` accessible NAME still read the raw colour keys (`red, amber, green` via `model.zones.join`) → the colour-word problem leaked into the AT / a11y-tree channel (the very DP-5 failure the slice removes) → map the name through `RAG_LABEL`. **R2 (1×P2 + 2×P3):** (a) for a **lower-is-better** objective `bandZones` returns the visual zones better→worse left-to-right (`green,amber,red`), so "worse to better: On track, …" announced On track as the WORST zone → order the AT name worst→best by SEVERITY (a fixed `["red","amber","green"]` filter), direction-independent; (b) the Status-column sort returned the RAW `o.rag` string — alphabetical, which *coincidentally* matched the OLD colour words but scrambles the new meaning labels → sort by `RAG_SEVERITY`; (c) wrapping each marker in a `<g>` left `<title>` as the SECOND child → SVG 1.1 user agents need it FIRST for the hover tooltip → reorder. **R3 (2×P2):** (a) `RAG_SEVERITY[unmeasured]=3` made a desc/best-first sort surface "Not yet measured" rows FIRST → return `null` for unmeasured so `sortRows`' null-last comparator keeps no-data rows last in BOTH directions; (b) the chart glyph was filled with the RAG colour, so an **amber `◔` was a low-contrast yellow mark on white** — the shape channel wasn't actually perceivable (a DP-5 fail for amber, the inverse of what the slice claims to fix) → a RAG-coloured dot carries the colour and a **dark glyph on top** carries the legible shape (the BandPreview dark-on-colour pattern; legend swatches match). Each fix is test-guarded (a LOWER_IS_BETTER band-name test, a desc-sort null-last test, a glyph-fill-is-dark + colour-on-dot test, a `<title>`-first assertion, a `RAG_SEVERITY` ordering unit test). **Deferred (named-not-faked) → the remaining design-critique Phase 3:** the PDCA-grouped nav rail + an in-product glyph legend; the shared loading / error-retry / empty primitives (which bundles the real `useAckCount` silent-zero bug — a 403 renders "0 acks"); the non-badge `▲` still in `RunSummaryTiles`/`PreCommitChecklist` (out of this slice's objectives scope). **Test delta: web 1041→1045 (+4):** `RAG_GLYPH` + `RAG_SEVERITY` map assertions (`labels.test`), the trend-marker glyph+colour+`title`-first test (rewritten to query `[data-rag]` not `circle`), the LOWER_IS_BETTER band-name test, the Status-column severity-sort test (asc + desc null-last). `/check-web` green (eslint + strict `tsc` + build + **1045/1045** via `--pool=forks --singleFork`). **diff-critic + web-test-trap-reviewer CLEAN pre-PR AND missed all 6 Codex findings** — Codex is the a11y/semantics edge-finder on this design class. ⚠ **Branch protection:** `main` advanced (the S-obj-freeze docs record [#198](https://github.com/CoJoA13/EasySynQ/pull/198)) mid-review → had to `git merge main` (docs-only, clean) into the branch before the protection's up-to-date gate would let it merge; and rapid repeated `@codex review` debounces the bot's re-review (its 4th re-review didn't fire — no findings were open, all 6 threads resolved). **Merge gate:** all 6 Codex threads resolved + green CI (11/11) + owner's explicit go. PR [#199](https://github.com/CoJoA13/EasySynQ/pull/199), squash `5924f6d`.

- **S-statusbadge-2** (StatusBadge Phase 2, critique **#1** — the remaining badges) — completes the canonical-status migration: the ~12 remaining per-feature badge components now ride the ONE `<StatusBadge tone label kind>` system (Phase 1 = `lib/StatusBadge.tsx`/`lib/status.ts`/`theme/mantine.ts`, #142). **100% FRONT-END-ONLY** — no migration/key/endpoint/contract (head stays `0051`); gate = `/check-web` only. Each component now declares a **feature-local** `META: Record<State,{label,tone}>` (the `StateBadge` exemplar), deleting its ad-hoc colour map + glyph; the **▲ glyph is fully retired** from every badge. **Closes:** critique **P1 #1** for the badges Phase 1 didn't reach; the **[P2] badge-contrast AA risk** (every migrated badge moves off Mantine `variant="light"` raw-hue math onto the AA-tuned `--es-*-soft`/`--es-*-text` pairs — light+dark free via `tokens.css`); the named **clarify deferral** (the Objectives register RAG badge gained a glyph). **Migrated:** document `TaskStateBadge`/`ReviewStateBadge`, dcr `DcrStateBadge`, ingestion `ImportStatusBadge`/`ConfidenceCell`, compliance `CoverageBadge` **+ the `CompliancePage` Overdue-review badge** (the spec's "Overdue" — NOT a `CoverageStatus`, it's a separate `overdue_review` badge), audits `AuditStateBadge`/`FindingTypeBadge`, capa **new `SeverityBadge`** + `columns.SEVERITY_TONE` (consumed by `CapaCard`/`CapaDrawer`/`CapaApprovalContext`), objectives RAG (`ObjectiveScorecardBand`/register/`CommitmentHero`/`labels`), management-review `ReviewInputsSection` RAG **+ `ManagementReviewsRegisterPage` `close_state`** (where the badge actually lives — NOT `ReviewInputsSection` as the spec text said), drift scan-status. **Owner design-calls:** Drift `FAILED`→danger ✕, ConfidenceCell `LOW`+`AMBIGUOUS`→danger ✕, MR `close_state` `Closed`→success ✓ (a deliberate re-grade of the old gray outlier toward DCR/Audit `Closed`=success); `KindCell` LEFT bespoke (a Document/Record domain icon, not a tone glyph). **Severity unified app-wide** — Critical=danger / Major=warning / Minor=neutral, byte-identical in `capa/columns.SEVERITY_TONE` AND `audits/badges.SEVERITY_TONE` (faithful to the prior hues). ⚠ **Traps a future session MUST carry:** (1) **the cross-partition tone-consistency trap** — a per-feature-dir subagent fan-out CANNOT see cross-surface consistency: the parallel agents landed `Major` severity = **warning in capa but danger in audits**; the orchestrator MUST reconcile to ONE convention (faithful-to-old-hue is the tiebreaker absent an owner design-call), and the diff-critic caught a SECOND (MR `Closed` gray→success) needing an owner call. (2) **StatusBadge couples the visible label + the aria-label from ONE `label` prop** — a two-channel split (visible band name vs aria "Ambiguous") can't be preserved; keep the primary value in the label + put the secondary signal in a sibling caption, else you ship a contradictory glyph+label pill (`✓ Ambiguous`) — the `ConfidenceCell` lesson (ambiguity now rides only the `⚖ ambiguous` caption). (3) **the spec target table can MISLOCATE a badge** — migrate where it actually lives (compliance "Overdue" → `CompliancePage`; MR `close_state` → `ManagementReviewsRegisterPage`). (4) **consumer-page tests OUTSIDE the migrated component's partition** assert OLD combined glyph+label nodes (`getByText(/● In progress/)`, `/⚑ Major NC/`) — the StatusBadge split (aria-hidden glyph span + separate label node) breaks them; scope to the label or the aria. A lifecycle STEPPER that renders its own "● Label" (`AuditLifecyclePanel`) is independent and still passes. **Deferred (named-not-faked) → the Phase-3 DP-5 colour-alone SHAPES slice:** the non-badge `▲` still in `RunSummaryTiles` MetricTile + `PreCommitChecklist` AdvisoryRow (not badges, not in the target table); `ObjectiveTrendChart` RAG marker shapes + `BandPreview` zone borders left untouched. **Built via an ultracode subagent fan-out** (9 implementers, one per feature dir; disjoint files, `lib/status.ts`/`StatusBadge.tsx` shared READ-only). **Verification:** `/check-web` green (eslint + strict tsc + vite build + **962 vitest** via `--pool=forks --singleFork`); `web-test-trap-reviewer` + `diff-critic` both CLEAN; **live-smoke** passed on `/compliance`, `/capa`, `/objectives` (computed-style read confirmed `variant="status"` → the AA `-soft`/`-text` pairs + the glyph render). **Test delta: web 943→962 (+19; +1 file `SeverityBadge.test.tsx`).** **Codex post-PR: clean approval ("no major issues"), no inline comments.** PR [#149](https://github.com/CoJoA13/EasySynQ/pull/149), squash `8749e8d`. Spec: `docs/superpowers/specs/s-statusbadge-2.md`.

- **S-optimize-1** (`/impeccable optimize`, critique **#5** — power-user triage) — the surfaces Mara/Ken live in most can now be triaged **in place**. **BACKEND (migration-free, additive contract) + FE across all six high-traffic surfaces.** Head stays `0051`, catalog 100; no new key/enum/endpoint/migration. **Backend:** `GET /tasks` LIST gains `subject_type`/`subject_id`/**`subject_identifier`**/**`subject_title`** via ONE query — `Task→WorkflowInstance` (inner; `instance_id` NOT-NULL) + `LEFT JOIN documented_information` + `LEFT JOIN dcr`, `func.coalesce(di.identifier, dcr.identifier)`/`coalesce(di.title, dcr.reason_text)` (NO N+1; `subject_id` is a polymorphic UUID across DISJOINT id-spaces so ≤1 LEFT JOIN matches — CAPA/MGMT_REVIEW/OBJ are shared-PK subtypes of `documented_information`, PERIODIC_REVIEW/DOC_ACK act on a DI, only DCR lives in its own table). Mirrored on `GET /tasks/{id}` via a single-row `subject_label` (pinned to AGREE with the batched list). A DCR subject's "title" is its `reason_text` capped at 160 by `_short` (applied once in `_task`, both paths). `GET /dcrs` LIST + detail gain **`target_identifier`**/**`target_title`** (`outerjoin documented_information ON di.id = Dcr.target_document_id`; null for CREATE; `ix_dcr_target_document_id` backs it). `_dcr`/`list_dcrs` return tuples — the ONLY caller is the endpoint. `openapi.yaml` `Task`+`Dcr` updated; **the now-dead `list_user_tasks` removed** (sole caller switched to `list_user_tasks_with_subject`). **FE (reusable `lib/` primitives → six surfaces):** `lib/registerControls` (`useUrlParam`/`useDebouncedSearch`[`useDebouncedValue(150)`]/`useTableSort`/`sortRows` [nulls sort LAST both directions, stable]); `RegisterToolbar`/`SortableTh`[`aria-sort` + new chevron icons]/`SubjectCell`; `useRowKeyboardNav` (roving ↑/↓ moves REAL DOM focus over `[data-rownav]`, native Enter — preserves the single-Anchor clean-axe shape); `useBulkSelection` (the DOC_ACK Set pattern extracted, optional `selectable` guard = the ingestion `included_candidate` predicate). Applied: **Tasks inbox** (Subject column = the identifier link + dim title, + search/sort/keyboard) · **Home rail** (names the subject) · **AckInbox** (drops its per-row `useTask→useDocument` N+1, reads the enriched list, selection via `useBulkSelection`) · the **DCR/MR/Audits/Objectives** registers (debounced search + sortable columns + keyboard row-nav + **URL-backed** enum filters migrated from local `useState`→`useUrlParam` so they survive nav). **Owner forks (AskUserQuestion up-front):** enrich serializers (vs client-side); **all six** surfaces this slice; bulk **acknowledge-only** (NO bulk-approve — each approval is a signed SoD-gated act, and #3 just hardened single-click irreversibles); **URL-backed** filter/sort + keyboard nav (fixes the named "filters reset on nav" Mara pain). ⚠ **Traps a future session MUST carry:** (1) the React **ref-typing** trap — `useRowKeyboardNav` must `useRef<E>(null)` → `RefObject<E>` (NOT `useRef<E|null>` → `RefObject<E|null>`, which a Mantine/native `ref` prop rejects across all 6 call sites); (2) **the auto-formatter strips "unused" imports between two edits** — add an import in the SAME edit that first uses it (the `func`/`Dcr`/`DocumentedInformation` F821 round-trip); (3) a **raw-DOM jsx-a11y** test harness for the keyboard hook trips `no-static-element-interactions`/`anchor-is-valid` — use Mantine `Table.Tbody` (a component → not flagged) + `<button>` rows; (4) the **DCR fixture false-PASS** (web-test-trap) — two DCRs sharing a `target_document_id` must resolve the SAME `target_identifier` (the outerjoin is per-target), so give them DISTINCT targets or a search test passes for the wrong reason. **Deferred (named-not-faked):** server-side `/tasks`+register pagination/typeahead (the lists are unbounded + client-filtered — fine at single-org volume); a system-level density toggle (not chosen); bulk beyond DOC_ACK (MR_ACTION "complete" is the only other no-signature candidate). **+9 api unit (`test_optimize_subject_serializers`) + 4 integration (CI-only on Windows: `/tasks` DOCUMENT list↔detail agreement, `/dcrs` target identity, the `/tasks` DCR-subject coalesce branch) / web 916→943.** diff-critic **CLEAN**; web-test-trap one MINOR (the DCR fixture) **fixed** + the search test hardened to prove per-row isolation. **Live smoke** (rebuilt `api`+`web`, org ORG_EXAMPLE): the `/tasks` inbox NAMES every task (MR-GEN-002/OBJ-GEN-004/MR-GEN-001 — the DOCUMENT branch) + search filters by subject title; the DCR Target column names the target (SOP-PUR-002, FRM-QMS-002) vs the old "Document", CREATE→"New document"; Objectives search + sort both directions (`aria-sort` + `?q`/`?sort`/`?dir`); MR+Audits search/sort; the backend join verified on **real PG** (incl. the DCR coalesce branch); no console errors. The 5th critique P1 (after #1 StatusBadge [#142], #2 RAG/breadcrumb labels [#143], #2b enum de-leak + clocks + #4 emoji→SVG [#144], #3 confirm-on-irreversible [#145]). PR [#146](https://github.com/CoJoA13/EasySynQ/pull/146), squash `bbb87c4`. ⚠ **Codex post-PR found 2 REAL P2s in `registerControls` (the diff-critic + web-test-trap + CI all missed; fixed in a same-track follow-up):** (1) `useTableSort.toggleSort` compared the RAW url `sort` param, not the *resolved* sort, so the FIRST click on the **default-active** column wrote the same default → a silent no-op (you had to click twice) — fix: resolve the effective current sort/dir inside the updater. (2) `useDebouncedSearch` **clobbered an EXTERNAL `?q` change** (back/forward, a same-route link) — because **react-router's `setSearchParams` is NOT referentially stable** (it changes on every location change), a write effect keyed on it re-fired on the external change with the stale local value. ⚠ Two insufficient fixes were caught by the new regression test before the right one: a naive `setQ(urlQ)` sync (a same-commit clobber survives), then a `debounced === q` guard (the unstable-setter re-fire survives) → the landing fix **refs both `urlQ` + `setUrlQ` and keys the write effect on `debounced` ONLY**. Also folded in: the Audits **"Started" column sorts by `started_at`** (what it displays), not `created_at`. +2 web regression tests (943→945).

## v1.x DCR UI track — clause §10→§7.5 change control — surfaces the built-but-UI-less DCR domain in the SPA (read spine first)

- **S-dcr-target-typeahead (A3)** server-driven debounced DCR raise target picker — **CLOSES the last-named DCR ui-2a residual** (the target-picker large-list typeahead that S-dcr-ui-2a/S-doc-filters both explicitly deferred: the raise REVISE/RETIRE target `Select` in `DcrRaiseFields` fetched `useDocuments({current_state:"Effective"},{limit:200})` + filtered CLIENT-SIDE by label, so a target beyond the 200-row cap was unreachable for any org with >200 Effective controlled docs). **BACKEND + FE, migration-free, no permission key** (head stays `0055`, next `0056`; rides `document.read`); gates `/check-api` + `/check-contracts` + `/check-web`. **Owner design fork (asked in-plan, A vs B vs a discovered C):** **A — add a top-level `q` to `GET /documents` (substring ILIKE)** — chosen over **B** (the FTS `GET /search`: heavier `snippet`/`rank`/`clause_refs` shape + `websearch_to_tsquery` semantics, awkward for picking an exact doc by an identifier fragment) and **C** (reuse the existing `GET /search/suggest` typeahead — already Effective-only + documents-only + `document.read`-filtered + `{id,identifier,title}`-shaped, BUT its `_SUGGEST_SQL` is a **prefix** match `'<term>%'`, so reusing it would REGRESS finding `SOP-PUR-014` by typing `PUR`, and broadening it to substring would alter the shared ⌘K `CommandPalette`). ⚠ **#138 was NOT a typeahead precedent** (it added the boolean `has_effective_version`/`managed_subtype` *filters*; `ImplementCreateDcrModal` is still client-`searchable`) → there was **no existing server-typeahead picker to mirror**; the only debounced-server-query precedent is `CommandPalette` + `useSuggest`. **Backend:** `list_documents` gains an optional **top-level `q`** (a sibling of `limit`/`offset`, **NOT** part of the `filter[…][…]` eq/has/gte/lte grammar → `_FILTER_ALLOW` untouched, no `unknown_filter` risk) → a case-insensitive **substring** `or_(identifier.ilike, title.ilike)` with pattern `%term%`, wildcards neutralised by a new **`_ilike_escape`** (backslash-first, mirrors `services/search/indexer.py`) + `escape="\\"`, **appended to the `filters` list so it ANDs** with the bracketed grammar + the `org_id`/`kind=DOCUMENT` scope. A blank/whitespace `q` adds **no condition** → byte-identical for the Library + every other `useDocuments` caller. ⚠ **the `document.read` per-row authz filter is unchanged and REMAINS the security boundary** — `q` only narrows the candidate window (UX narrowing, never a visibility change); the backend `_resolve_target` stays the submit-time correctness guard (a REVISE/RETIRE target must be a controlled Document, not a Record). **FE (`DcrRaiseFields`, the single shared component → one change covers Raise + BOTH SpawnDcrModal seams [CAPA + MR-output]):** a 200ms `useDebouncedValue(search)` drives `useDocuments({current_state:"Effective", q: debounced.trim()||undefined},{limit:20})`; **react-query keys by `q`, so a slow earlier response can't clobber a newer one — the hand-rolled stale-guard the `CommandPalette` needs is a non-issue by construction**; Mantine's client filter is disabled (`filter={({options})=>options}`, server already narrowed) and the redundant `kind==="DOCUMENT"` client filter is dropped (server excludes Records); a `Loader` `rightSection` while fetching + an `isError`-aware `nothingFoundMessage` (calm degrade, never crash). The **picked option is unioned into the data** so its label survives a later narrower search (the Codex stale-row edge), **gated on `selected.value === value.target_document_id`** so a stale pick goes inert the instant the parent nulls the target on a CREATE switch (no orphan can leak — derived, no effect). The `CREATE⟺no-target` biconditional (`isDcrFieldsValid`) + the RETIRE-clears-date logic are untouched. ⚠ **The reset story took a mutation-testing pass:** an initial `useEffect` to clear the search on the CREATE switch was a **genuine NO-OP** — Mantine **remounts** the inner `Select` on the CREATE→REVISE round-trip (and fires `onSearchChange("")` on the fresh mount), so the search already resets; it couldn't be discriminatingly tested → **removed it**, and the only real lingering-state path (a stale *committed* pick after a CREATE clear) is closed by the `selected.value === target` union gate instead. ⚠ **Web test traps carried:** (1) the **debounce timing trap** — `SOP-PUR-014` is present in BOTH the `q=""` and `q="PUR"` pages, so `findByRole(PUR)` is a FALSE gate (it matches the pre-narrow list); the real narrowing SIGNAL is `QMS`-gone **AND** `PUR`-present asserted in ONE `waitFor` (retries through the `q=""` both-present state + the in-flight loader blank); (2) the **ruff PostToolUse hook strips a just-added unused import** (the `_ilike_escape` test import) — append the body FIRST, re-add the import AFTER (the recurring trap); (3) MSW fixtures `satisfies DocumentSummary`/`DocumentsPage` pinned to the real `_document` serializer + the real `{limit,offset,returned,has_more}` page envelope (the old fixture's `total` was fictional); (4) both the union test AND the orphan-guard test are **mutation-verified** (each fails when its mechanism is removed). **Reviews:** diff-critic = CLEAN; **web-test-trap-reviewer 2 rounds — both findings the no-op reset effect + the uncovered `selected.value===target` union guard were resolved with mutation-verified discriminating tests** (the round-2 catch: deleting the guard clause kept the suite green → added a "stale pick not re-offered after a CREATE clear" test that fails without it); **Codex CLEAN ("Didn't find any major issues. Swish!")** on the final commit. **Deferred (named, not faked):** true paging / infinite-scroll in the picker (a debounced typeahead + a 20-row page is sufficient — a target is found by typing, not scrolling; the `document.read` row-filter + the `_resolve_target` guard keep it safe regardless); **diacritics-insensitive search** (ILIKE is ASCII-case-only — `unaccent`/a trigram index is a v1.x refinement, the same note S-doc-filters carried); the **CREATE-implement picker** (`ImplementCreateDcrModal`) left on its #138 server-narrowed client-`searchable` form (its never-released-Approved candidate set is small + already server-narrowed — the owner scoped this slice to the raise picker only). **Test deltas: +2 api unit** (`test_documents_filters.py` — `_ilike_escape` leaves plain text untouched + neutralises `% _ \`, order-correct) **+2 api integration** (`test_documents_list.py` — run-scoped via `owner_user_id`+salted titles: substring narrowing over identifier AND title / case-insensitive / identifier-vs-title isolation / non-match→empty; `%`-escaping doesn't broaden + `q` ANDs with `filter[current_state][eq]`; **CI-authoritative in that Windows development environment**); **web 1045→1050 (+5:** DcrRaiseFields 2→6 [debounced narrowing, re-show-clean-after-CREATE, union persistence, orphan-guard], useDocuments 6→7 [top-level `q` emission / omitted-when-blank]). **Verified:** `/check-web` (eslint + strict tsc + build + 1050 vitest, full run via `--pool=forks --singleFork`) · `/check-api` fast (ruff + format + mypy-strict; only the known Windows `O_NOFOLLOW` baseline) · `/check-contracts` (redocly valid; `q` documented as a top-level optional string) — all green; **CI all 11 checks green incl. all 4 integration shards.** PR [#203](https://github.com/CoJoA13/EasySynQ/pull/203), squash `ed4af1d`. **The DCR-UI track now has NO open named residuals.**

- **S-doc-filters** server-side CREATE-picker candidate filters on `GET /documents` — **closes the CREATE-picker half of the long-named picker-pagination v1.x deferral** (the ui-2a/ui-4 first-page-only gap). A cross-cutting `/documents`-API + library-hook slice serving the DCR CREATE-implement picker. **BACKEND + FE, migration-free, no key/enum** (head stays `0051`, catalog 100); gates `/check-api` + `/check-contracts` + `/check-web`. Two opt-in, boolean, `eq`-only filters added to the bracketed grammar in `api/documents.py`: **`filter[has_effective_version][eq]`** (→ `current_effective_version_id IS NULL`/`IS NOT NULL`) and **`filter[managed_subtype][eq]`** (→ `~(EXISTS quality_objective OR EXISTS management_review)` — a shared-PK `NOT EXISTS`; **F2: chosen over a doc-type-code exclude so it's immune to type-code renames** — OBJ/MR carry no discriminator column on `documented_information`). New `_parse_filter_bool` (422 `validation_error` on non-`true`/`false`); `_FILTER_ALLOW` + two `_filter_condition` branches + `or_`/`QualityObjective`/`ManagementReview` imports; **`list_documents`/the limit clamp/`_LIST_SCAN_CAP`/per-row authz untouched**. The CREATE picker (`ImplementCreateDcrModal`) now passes `{has_effective_version:false, managed_subtype:false}`; its client-side `.filter` + `useDocumentTypes`/`managedTypeIds` are DELETED; `DocumentFilters` += two optional booleans; `buildDocumentsQuery` emits each **only when `!== undefined`**. **Owner F-decisions:** **F1 = CREATE picker ONLY** (the ui-2a target picker [`DcrRaiseFields`, Effective docs] is OUT — narrowing can't help a picker that *wants* Effective docs; its fix is server-side typeahead, deferred); **F3 = bracketed grammar, default-off** (omitted-when-unset → the other `GET /documents` consumers — the Library page + the ui-2a DCR target picker — are byte-unaffected; ⚠ global search is **NOT** a `/documents` consumer, it hits OpenSearch `/search`). ⚠ **KEY framing: PURE UX, not a correctness fix** — `services/dcr/service.py::_resolve_implement_version` already rejects an invalid CREATE target (`create_target_not_new`/`create_target_managed_subtype`) regardless of the picker (the S-dcr-ui-4 server guard); this slice only stops invalid candidates from *appearing*. ⚠ **Traps a future session must carry:** (1) the **FE false-emit trap** — a boolean default-off query filter MUST emit on `!== undefined`, never `if (filters.x)` (else `false` is silently dropped — the CREATE picker sends `false`); (2) the **managed-subtype exclusion has no discriminator column** (OBJ/MR are shared-PK subtype rows) → `NOT EXISTS` against the subtype tables, not a doc-type-code exclude; (3) **diff-critic compiling the REAL route query** proved the `EXISTS` correlates correctly when spread via `*filters` — a standalone `_filter_condition` compile shows a *misleading uncorrelated cartesian* (a SQLAlchemy auto-correlation artifact resolved at embedding), so verify correlation on the assembled `select`, not the bare condition; (4) **backend live smoke in that Windows development environment** (PG not host-published, worker runs baked images) — ran the new query SHAPE inside the running worker via a `docker exec -i easysynq-worker-1 sh -c 'cd /app && uv run python -'` heredoc against live PG (**no image rebuild when you INLINE the query** instead of importing the new code): the partition `narrowed+excluded==approved` held and `managed_subtype=true` (8) matched the OBJ(5)+MR(3) subtype-join ground truth exactly. **Deferred (named, not faked):** the ui-2a target-picker large-list typeahead (needs server-side text search — a new length-capped ILIKE facet or OpenSearch `/search` reuse); true paging/infinite-scroll in the pickers (narrowing keeps the CREATE set under the 100-clamp; the `_resolve_implement_version` guard keeps it safe regardless). **+14 api unit (`tests/unit/test_documents_filters.py`) + 2 api integration (`test_documents_list.py` — the four-shape narrowing matrix [Effective-initial / Approved-new / Approved-revision-of-Effective / OBJ] + a non-boolean→422); web 866→869** (the false-emit triple + the modal request-URL + server-trust tests replacing two obsolete client-filter tests). diff-critic + web-test-trap CLEAN; CI 9/9; Codex CLEAN round 1. subagent-driven TDD (3 parallel tasks + per-task quality review, all clean). PR [#138](https://github.com/CoJoA13/EasySynQ/pull/138), squash `8eaaf98`. Spec/plan: `docs/superpowers/{specs,plans}/2026-06-14-s-doc-filters-create-picker*`.

- **S-dcr-ui-4** CREATE-implement + the CREATE resulting-doc deep-link — **CLOSES the LAST DCR residual (C); the DCR domain is now fully operable in the SPA.** A CREATE DCR can be implemented from the cockpit, and its resulting new document is deep-linked. **ONE small backend touch + FE** — a detail-only `resulting_document_id` enrichment on `GET /dcrs/{id}` + a CREATE-implement target guard; **migration-free, no permission key** (head stays `0051`); gates `/check-api` + `/check-contracts` + `/check-web`. **Owner F-decisions** (after a 7-reader code-verification fan-out, all `confirmed`): **F1 — serializer enrichment** (detail-only `resulting_document_id` derived from the resulting version's `document_version.document_id` FK — CREATE → the new doc; REVISE → `== target_document_id`; RETIRE/pre-implement → null — NOT a top-level version-lookup endpoint; `_dcr` + the list endpoint stay pure; +1 `Dcr` contract field). **F2 — full scope** (deep-link **+** wire the cockpit Approved→Implement for CREATE, not just the read side). **The slice:** the FE drawer deep-links an implemented CREATE DCR to `/documents/{resulting_document_id}` (calm-degrades on a `document.read` 403; no visual diff — a new doc's first version has no predecessor); a new **`ImplementCreateDcrModal`** (an Approved-document searchable picker → FE-resolves the doc's Approved version via `useDocumentVersions` → POSTs `resulting_version_id` to the existing `POST /dcrs/{id}/implement`); `DcrAdvancePanel` drops its `change_type !== "CREATE"` exclusion + branches the mounted modal on CREATE. ⚠ **Gate honesty (the load-bearing trap):** for CREATE, `capabilities.implement` reflects only `changeRequest.implement` — the endpoint does **NOT** enforce `document.release` for CREATE (`_underlying_control_target` returns `None`; the release cutover is system-attributed), so the affordance shows on the capability and the modal **submit-and-shows** any server 403/409 (the `CloseAction` precedent); `_dcr_capabilities` is unchanged. ⚠ **Codex (2 rounds, 5 findings — it found real edges the diff-critic + web-test-trap CLEAN pass missed):** **P1 (F1) verified reachable via the document FSM** — a revision (`Effective → start_revision → UnderRevision → submit_review → InReview → approve → **D.Approved**`, `domain/vault/lifecycle.py:77-88`) sits at `current_state="Approved"` **yet keeps its `current_effective_version_id`** until release (T6), so the picker (`current_state:"Approved"`) would offer it and CREATE-implement would release the revision under a CREATE DCR (bypassing the REVISE flow + impact assessment). **Fix = a server-side guard in `_resolve_implement_version` (CREATE): reject a target whose document has a `current_effective_version_id` (409 `create_target_not_new`) or a `quality_objective`/`management_review` row (409 `create_target_managed_subtype`)** — the correctness boundary, since the endpoint doesn't enforce `document.release` for CREATE; + FE picker filters (`current_effective_version_id === null` + exclude OBJ/MR document-types via `useDocumentTypes`, FRM and other plain controlled docs stay valid) so invalid candidates never appear. P2 (F2): surface the version-fetch 403 (a releaser lacking `document.read_draft`) instead of a silently-disabled button. ⚠ **Codex correction folded in:** OBJ/MR release via the **same** generic cutover (S-obj-3/S-mr-1 — there is NO separate subtype release endpoint), so offering them is semantically wrong, not data-corrupting. **Deferred (named, not faked — the owner's v1.x call):** the **picker-pagination** (`GET /documents` clamps `limit` to 100; the client-side filters could hide a valid page-2 candidate) — the same v1.x limitation as the S-dcr-ui-2a target picker; **the server-side guard makes it safe** (validity is *enforced* regardless of the picker; `Approved` is a transient state so >100 simultaneous Approved docs is unrealistic); the proper fix is server-side candidate filtering on `GET /documents`. **Live smoke (rebuilt stack, real data, owner did the login):** the `/dcrs` register rendered the staged CREATE DCR (Approved→Closed reflected over real HTTP), `GET /dcrs/{id}` served 200, and the **backend CREATE-implement + enrichment** were driven end-to-end via the real services (implement DCR-2026-0007 against FRM-QMS-001's Approved version → Implemented → release cutover → Effective → Closed; `get_dcr_endpoint` returned `resulting_document_id`=the new doc for CREATE, `== target` for REVISE); ⚠ **the in-drawer modal/deep-link could NOT be driven via Chrome MCP** — the documented `/dcrs` drawer-open limitation (ref/coordinate/JS `.click()` all no-op; full-navigate deep-link redirects to Home; the page token is blocked by the privacy guardrail) — so the FE rendering is covered by the unit tests + the web-test-trap-reviewer's real-serializer fixture cross-check. **Reviews:** diff-critic + web-test-trap-reviewer **CLEAN**; subagent-driven TDD (4 tasks, per-task spec+quality review); **Codex 2 rounds, all 5 threads replied + resolved**; ⚠ **one CI flake of mine** (the F1-guard integration test gave the approver the `Approver` role instead of `grant_lifecycle`, so `drive_to_effective`'s release 403'd — the releaser needs `document.release`; mirrors `test_revise_implement`). **Test deltas: web 852→861 (+9:** DcrDrawer +2, ImplementCreateDcrModal +4 then +3 [Codex F1/F4/F2], DcrImpactTable +1 [the carry-forward no-cross-DCR-leak pin from #132]); **+2 api integration** (`test_create_implement_rejects_revision_of_existing_doc` + `test_create_implement_rejects_managed_subtype`) + the `resulting_document_id` assertions on the existing CREATE-implement test. **CI 9/9 green.** PR [#134](https://github.com/CoJoA13/EasySynQ/pull/134), squash `5268a25`. Spec/plan: `docs/superpowers/{specs,plans}/2026-06-14-s-dcr-ui-4-create-implement*`. **The DCR domain is now feature-complete end-to-end (read + write + diff + annotate + CREATE-implement); no residuals remain.**

- **S-dcr-impact-annotation + the `CapaApprovalContext` a11y follow-up** — two small follow-ups closing two named residuals of the (now-complete) DCR-UI track; both **100% FRONT-END-ONLY** (no migration, no permission key, no new endpoint, no contract change; gate = `/check-web` only — head stays `0051`).
  - **#132 — inline annotation of the 7 DCR impact dimensions.** The SPA could *view* a DCR's impact dimensions (the ui-1 read-only `DcrImpactTable`) but not *annotate* them; this wires the requester annotation through the already-built **`PUT /dcrs/{id}/impact`** (S-dcr-3b/-5 — the service `annotate_impact` is a **partial merge**: it sets `requester_annotation` only on the dimensions present in the body dict, leaves `auto_populated` untouched, emits `DCR_UPDATED`, has **no FSM-state gate** — it only requires the impact rows to exist; unknown dimension → 422 `unknown_dimension`, an un-assessed dimension → 409 `impact_not_assessed`, both unreachable from the UI which only edits existing rows). **Owner F-decisions (2026-06-14, after verifying the backend):** F1 — the Annotation column is editable when **`impact.length > 0 && <capability> && state ∉ {Closed,Cancelled,Rejected}`** (matches the backend's permissiveness — a requester/approver can refine annotations right up through approval, where they inform the decision; only the three terminals hide it, where an edit is pointless); F2 — **inline-editable cells + one batch Save** (the Annotation cells become `Textarea`s; a single "Save annotations" button PUTs only the changed dimensions), not a modal. **The slice:** `lib/api.ts` `api.send`/`apiSend` method union widened `+"PUT"` (additive — existing callers byte-unaffected); `useAnnotateImpact(id)` (`PUT … {annotations}`, **`onSuccess` writes the PUT response into the `["dcr-impact",id]` cache via `setQueryData(resp.data)`** + invalidates `["dcr",id]` — non-optimistic, the server returns the refreshed rows); `DcrImpactTable` gains an editable path; `DcrDrawer` passes `editable={(impact?.length ?? 0) > 0 && dcr.capabilities?.assess === true && !["Closed","Cancelled","Rejected"].includes(dcr.state)}`. ⚠ **The editor draft-state design took FIVE Codex P2/P1 rounds — the landed answer is "KEY the editor, never reconcile server↔draft with a render-sync effect":** **(1)** the original spec gated on the SYSTEM-scoped `can("changeRequest.assess")` → Codex steered it to **`dcr.capabilities?.assess`** (PROCESS-scoped — the ui-2b `_dcr_capabilities` precedent; a PROCESS/DOC_CLASS grant-holder is no longer hidden); **(2)** a naïve re-seed on every `impact`-prop change **lost the user's unsaved edits on a background refetch** → preserve unsaved edits across an identical refetch (+ a fixture trap: order the MSW impact handler before the detail one); **(3)** but then a genuine server change (another user annotated) wouldn't surface on a **pristine** draft → adopt fresh server values when the draft is pristine; **(4)+(5)** the converging fix that subsumed (2)/(3) and killed a **cross-DCR leak** (a dirty draft for DCR-A surviving a switch to DCR-B → Save would PUT to the wrong DCR) and a **post-save stale window**: the read-only `<DcrImpactTable>` delegates the editable path to a child **`EditableImpactTable` keyed on `` `${dcrId}::${annotationSignature(impact)}` ``** (signature = `id=requester_annotation` joined over the rows). The key **remounts** (re-seeds a plain `useState` draft) on ANY genuine server change — a different DCR, a content refetch that changed a value, or **our own post-save `setQueryData` cache write** — but NOT on an identical background refetch (same signature → same key → edits preserved). So the editor holds a plain local draft with **zero server↔draft reconciliation**; Save computes `draft[dim] !== seed[dim]` and sends only the changed subset, disabled when nothing changed; a calm inline error on failure. Paired with the cache-write `onSuccess` there is **no stale-refetch window** (the saved values are in cache before any refetch, and they change the signature → the editor remounts to them). **Reviews:** diff-critic + web-test-trap-reviewer CLEAN; **5 Codex P2/P1 rounds all fixed + threads resolved** (gate-scope, MSW order, dirty-loss, pristine-clobber, cross-DCR leak, post-save stale). **Live smoke (Chrome MCP):** edited one Annotation cell → Save → confirmed the PUT carried exactly the one changed dimension and the value persisted on re-open. **Test delta: web 842→852 (+10).** PR [#132](https://github.com/CoJoA13/EasySynQ/pull/132), squash `e6b8256`. Spec/plan: `docs/superpowers/{specs,plans}/2026-06-14-s-dcr-impact-annotation*`.
  - **#131 — `CapaApprovalContext` heading-order a11y fix** (a parked residual flagged during ui-2b). The `/tasks` CAPA approval context skipped the page h2 straight to h4 (missing h3) — the **same** heading-order skip already fixed in `DcrApprovalContext` during ui-2b. Bumped `CapaApprovalContext`'s levels h4/h5 → h3/h4 + added a page-context CAPA **jest-axe smoke** (failing-first on the old levels) so it can't regress. FE-only; touches `features/review/CapaApprovalContext.tsx` + `ReviewApprovePage.test.tsx`. **Test delta: web 841→842 (+1).** PR [#131](https://github.com/CoJoA13/EasySynQ/pull/131), squash `e377492`.
  - **After these two, the DCR-UI track has exactly ONE residual remaining: C — CREATE-implement / the CREATE resulting-doc deep-link** (a CREATE DCR's `resulting_version_id` lives on a NEW document whose id `_dcr` does not expose, and there is no top-level `GET /versions/{id}` → the SPA can't resolve it; the read-spine "Resulting version" link shows a bare id and the ui-2b cockpit defers CREATE-implement. Needs a register-level call: a `resulting_document_id` serializer enrichment vs a version-lookup endpoint).

- **S-dcr-ui-3** DCR page-image visual diff + redline — **CLOSES the DCR-UI track** (the last named deferral): a reviewer/auditor can now *see* what a REVISE change altered. A dedicated **`/dcrs/:id/diff`** full route shows the worker-async page-image visual diff **and** the text/metadata redline of a DCR's resulting version against the version it supersedes. **100% FRONT-END-ONLY** — no migration, no permission key, no new endpoint, no contract change; **gate = `/check-web` only** (head stays `0051`). Reuses the S-web-4b document-diff machinery **verbatim** (`VisualDiffViewer`/`RedlineViewer`/`useVisualDiff`/`useVersionDiff`). **Owner brainstorm F-decisions** (a 5-reader source-verification fan-out over the render path + the diff backend + the DCR serializer FIRST — it resolved two agent contradictions by direct code reads): **F1 — FRONT-END-ONLY/reuse** (the `VisualDiff` cache is keyed by `(from_version_id, to_version_id)` ONLY [not document-bound — `visual_diff.py:36`]; the three endpoints + the redline endpoint all already exist gated `document.read_draft`; for a REVISE DCR the resulting version AND its predecessor are both versions of `target_document_id`, so the same-document endpoint validation passes untouched — no backend touch). **F2 — already solved, no special handling** (the central finding: `services/diff/visual.py::_ensure_rendition_pdf` renders a not-yet-Effective version **transiently** — `copy_status=version.version_state`, no verify QR — and **deliberately does NOT write the shared `rendition_blob_sha256`** [the mirror's controlled-copy cache, `:112-117`], so previewing an Approved/pre-cutover resulting version is safe). **F3 — a dedicated full-page route** (not a drawer section: the page-image viewer is cramped in a 360–640px portal; full routes drive cleanly in Chrome-MCP whereas the drawer is flaky; a deep-linkable diff URL is auditor-friendly). **F4 — REVISE + Implemented/Closed** (`resulting_version_id` is set only at implement, only for REVISE/CREATE; CREATE has no client `version_id→document_id` resolution, RETIRE has no resulting version). **F5 — both visual + redline** via a `?mode=text|visual` toggle (parity with the doc-detail page). **F6 — impact-annotation kept out of scope** (an unrelated lifecycle phase — annotation at assess-time, the diff at Implemented/Closed). **The slice (`features/dcr/`):** `resolvePredecessor.ts` (a pure helper — succession-link post-cutover [`superseded_by_version_id`], else the governing **Effective** version pre-cutover, else a degenerate seq-fallback); `DcrDiffPage.tsx` (the route — header + back-link to `/dcrs?dcr=:id`, the mode toggle, the pinned-pair viewers, calm states: not-eligible/403-no-access/no-predecessor/load-error); the `DcrDrawer` "View visual diff →" link in the Resulting-version field (REVISE + `resulting_version_id` + `target_document_id`); the `App.tsx` `dcrs/:id/diff` route; `useDocumentVersions` widened with an optional `{retry?:boolean}` (the S-web-8 `useTask` conditional-spread precedent — existing callers byte-untouched — so the diff page skips retries on a deterministic `document.read_draft` 403). ⚠ **Folded-in fix (pre-existing, surfaced by the live smoke; the reused `useVisualDiff` hook — reachable from the doc-detail Compare tab too, NOT introduced by this slice's feature):** the worker-async poll could **stall on first mount** (it seeded the cache from the POST's `onSuccess setQueryData`, then gated the poll's `enabled` on a NON-reactive `qc.getQueryData(key)?.status==="Pending"` — when the POST seeded fresh data at the instant `enabled` flipped true, React Query v5's `shouldFetchOptionally→isStaleByTime` [`staleTime:0`] read it as "fresh" and SKIPPED the initial GET; no `refetchInterval` fallback → it hung on "Rendering…" with zero GET traffic until a manual "Re-request render"). Fix: **the poll owns its own fetch** — drop the seed, enable off the reactive `request.isSuccess` so the first GET runs against an empty cache (`data===undefined` always fetches). Then a **Codex P2** caught that `request.isSuccess` isn't pair-scoped → on a pair change while mounted (navigating between `/dcrs/:id/diff` pages, or the doc-detail picker) the new pair's poll could GET an unrequested row before its own POST → 404; fix: **pair-scope the gate** (`request.isSuccess && request.variables === url`, the POST's url passed as the mutation variable) — proven by a **genuine failing-first test** (confirmed it fails on the pre-fix code; models the backend's 404-until-POST). ⚠ **Traps carried:** the authed page-PNG is `apiGetBlob`→objectURL (the only API-proxied binary in the SPA); every MSW fixture `satisfies <Type>` (incl. the inline no-predecessor `DocumentVersion`, a web-test-trap-reviewer nit); `import {expect,it} from "vitest"`; a per-page jest-axe smoke; the deep-link/navigate `waitFor` discipline; `ApiError.message` = `problem.detail ?? title`. **Codex P2 #2 (resolvePredecessor):** the raw seq-fallback would pick an abandoned higher-seq Draft left by a changes-requested loop instead of the governing Effective version — fixed by preferring `version_state==="Effective"` pre-cutover (INV-1 guarantees one); +1 test. **Reviews:** diff-critic = CLEAN ("safe to PR"; verified the succession direction vs `lifecycle.py:505`, the hook widening's backward-compat, the casts); web-test-trap-reviewer = CLEAN on both the slice and the useVisualDiff fix (the GET overrides are required + faithful — they return the same status, not a silent Ready upgrade); **both Codex P2s fixed + threads resolved**; a final `@codex review` triggered on the merge commit. **Pre-merge live smoke (Chrome MCP):** built a real Implemented REVISE DCR on SOP-PUR-002 (text/plain) via the services (author≠approver≠implementer SoD; approvers picked from each task's `candidate_pool`); verified the drawer link → the diff route → the Text redline (real metadata + text diff against the resolved predecessor→resulting pair) → the Visual page-image diff (authed-PNG `blob:` + layer toggle + the changed-page rail) → the back-link; the useVisualDiff fix verified on a cold pair (POST 202 → GET 202 = the poll now fires; it didn't before) — and the "poll stops after one tick" turned out to be the automated tab being **hidden** (`visibilityState:hidden` + React Query `refetchIntervalInBackground:false`), environmental, not a bug. **Deferred (named, not faked):** **CREATE-implement visual diff** (no client `version_id→document_id` — needs a `resulting_document_id` enrichment or a version-lookup endpoint); **impact-dimension annotation** (`PUT /dcrs/{id}/impact` exists but `api.send`'s method union lacks `PUT`); the **`CapaApprovalContext` heading-order a11y fix** (same h2→h4 bug fixed in `DcrApprovalContext` during ui-2b). **Test deltas: web 823→841 (+18:** resolvePredecessor 6, DcrDiffPage 7, DcrDrawer +3, useVisualDiff +2 [first-mount + pair-change]); no api/migration/contract change. **CI green (all 9 checks; one `cf15a88` `integration-shard-3` 49-min timeout flake on a backend shard cleared on the next run); Codex 2×P2 valid + fixed.** PR [#129](https://github.com/CoJoA13/EasySynQ/pull/129), squash `66cad5e`. Spec/plan: `docs/superpowers/{specs,plans}/2026-06-14-s-dcr-ui-3-dcr-visual-diff*`.

- **S-dcr-ui-2b** DCR lifecycle cockpit + the `/tasks` DCR-approval leg + the `capabilities` enrichment — **CLOSES the operable DCR cockpit**: a routed DCR is now drivable **assess → route → approve (in `/tasks`) → implement → close** entirely in the SPA. **ONE backend touch** (a detail-only `capabilities` block on `GET /dcrs/{id}`) + a contract addition + the §8.7 prose-drift fix; everything else front-end. **No migration, no permission key, no new endpoint** — **head stays `0051`**; gates = `/check-web` + `/check-api` + `/check-contracts`. **Owner F-decisions (F1–F6):** F1 — **one slice** (the cockpit + the `/tasks` leg are FSM-coupled: a routed DCR sits `InApproval` with no SPA way to advance unless both ship together); F2 — **honest `implement` capability** (expanded from the ui-2a-deferred `{implement}` to **`{assess,route,implement,close}`**); F5 — **fold the prose drift**; F6 — **MINOR + MAJOR two-approver live smoke**. **The one backend touch — `_dcr_capabilities` (the `_mr_capabilities`/`_objective_capabilities` precedent), detail-only on `GET /dcrs/{id}` (list/create/raise stay capabilities-free):** ⚠ **a ground-truth correction to ui-2a's "DCR gating is SYSTEM-scoped" note** — that was a *client* limitation only; server-side `_dcr_doc_scope(target)` already resolves the FULL PROCESS scope from the target doc's process-links, so the block is **PROCESS-scoped** (SYSTEM fallback for CREATE/no-target). Four `changeRequest.*` PDP probes (`authorize(grants,key,scope,ctx).allow`, no audit) + the **honest `implement`** = `changeRequest.implement` **AND** the underlying `document.release`(REVISE)/`document.obsolete`(RETIRE) **SoD-2** answer — extracted into a NEW shared **`_underlying_control_target(dcr) → (key,scope)|None`** that ALSO refactors `_enforce_underlying_document_control` so capability + enforcement read ONE source and **cannot drift** (CREATE returns `None` → `implement` falls back to the `changeRequest.implement` answer since CREATE-implement has no SPA affordance; a drift-pin integration test asserts capability==enforce for REVISE+RETIRE). The FE gates the cockpit on **`dcr.capabilities?.<key> === true && <FSM state>`** (the `ManagementReviewDetailPage` no-show-then-403 pattern), **replacing ui-2a's SYSTEM-scoped `can()`** (Codex #124's steer — a PROCESS/DOC_CLASS grant-holder is no longer hidden). **FE cockpit:** `DcrAdvancePanel` grown into a switch-on-state panel (the CAPA `AdvancePanel` precedent, simpler — no propose-vs-awaiting): Open → Assess(one-click)/Edit/Cancel; Assessed → Route/Cancel; **InApproval → an "Awaiting approval — decided in My Tasks" banner, no advance**; Approved → **Implement change** (REVISE confirm / RETIRE force-retire escalation; **CREATE-implement deferred** — a calm "implemented from the document workspace" note); Implemented → **Close** (submit-and-show); terminals → nothing. New `RouteDcrModal` (confirm), `ImplementDcrModal` (REVISE `{}` / RETIRE: on a `409 obsoletion_blocked` reveal the coverage-gap detail + a force-retire checkbox + a **required** `override_justification`, then re-submit `{force_retire:true,…}`), `CloseDcrAction` (NO client gate — submit-and-show the three `dcr_effectivity_pending` strings verbatim, the CAPA `CloseAction` precedent), + 4 `onSettled` mutation hooks (`useAssessDcr`/`useRouteDcr`/`useImplementDcr`/`useCloseDcr`, never optimistic). **The `/tasks` DCR-approval leg (signing):** `DecisionSubjectType += 'DCR'` (tsc-forces the 3 `Record`s — `OUTCOMES`=approve/changes_requested/reject, `SIGN_OUTCOME`=approve, `SIGN_MEANING`=approval), a `DcrApprovalContext` (reads `useDcr`, calm-403), the `ReviewApprovePage` DCR branch **with the load-bearing `isDocumentSubject` negation extended `&& !isDcr`** (else a DCR task falls into the DOCUMENT branch → wrong gate + a phantom redline), and the `useDecideTask` DCR invalidation leg inserted **BEFORE the CAPA catch-all `else`** (else a DCR decision silently invalidates capa caches). Authority is candidate-pool membership (server 404-collapse), NOT a `changeRequest.approve` `can()` — the inbox LIST needs no change (DCR tasks are `type=APPROVE`/`action_expected=approve_dcr`; the branch is on the detail page). ⚠ **Traps confirmed:** the panel "Implement change" trigger is **distinct** from the modal's "Implement"/"Retire document"/"Force-retire" submits (avoids the duplicate-accessible-name trap when the modal opens over the panel — a per-task quality-review catch); `ApiError.message` is built from `problem.detail ?? title` (NOT `message`) so every error fixture uses `detail`; the per-page **jest-axe smoke caught a real heading-order bug** in `DcrApprovalContext` (h2→h4 fixed to h3/h4 — the sibling `CapaApprovalContext` has the same latent bug, flagged as a separate out-of-scope task). **Deferred (named, not faked):** CREATE-implement (no client `version_id→document_id`); impact-dimension annotation (`api.send` has no `PUT`; `DcrImpactTable` read-only); → **S-dcr-ui-3** the page-image visual diff + redline. **Test deltas: +18 web (805→823); +3 api integration** (`test_dcr_capabilities`, CI-only on the owner's Windows box). **diff-critic = CLEAN** (re-verified all 7 hunt areas vs the actual backend: refactor parity, the honest `implement` no-show-then-403, the negation, the invalidation ordering, MSW fixtures `satisfies` real serializers, accessible-name collisions, no WORM/blob touch). subagent-driven TDD (12 tasks, per-task two-stage spec+quality review on a fresh subagent each; ⚠ TWO implementers stopped pre-commit — caught via git checks + finished; precedent code extracted verbatim via a parallel-reader fan-out). **Pre-merge live smoke (Chrome MCP):** MINOR REVISE driven through the UI (cockpit gating across Open/Assessed/InApproval/Approved · Assess · Route · the `/tasks` signing approval → backend `Approved` + `signature_event(meaning=approval, type=dcr)` · the honest Implement affordance + the calm `no_approved_draft` 409); MAJOR REVISE: sequential `major_proc`→`major_qms` routing, stage-1 approve via UI, the **`dcr_approver_conflict` guard surfaced live**, a distinct QMS Owner completing stage 2 → `Approved` with two signatures. ⚠ **Live-smoke reality:** the live `demo` login JIT-maps to the `Demo (Admin)` row (not `Demo User`) → the **JIT-safe "assign roles to ALL org users"** approach (the grant-overrides precedent) was load-bearing for the candidate-pool approval; the `/dcrs` drawer-open via Chrome-MCP is flaky (a transient "Couldn't load" frame before the 200 settles — coordinate-click + wait + JS-read-after-settle worked; `/tasks` full-route pages drove cleanly). ⚠ **CI took two rounds** — both web failures were the **same pre-existing ui-2a deep-link flake** (`findByTestId("loc")` resolves on the LocationProbe existing, racing the post-mutation `navigate` → flakes under CI parallel load but passes single-fork; grep-found + hardened all 3 instances with `waitFor`), plus one real fix (the Task-1 capabilities test's `_CR_KEYS` missing `changeRequest.create` → its create 403'd, invisible locally since integration is CI-only). **CI green; Codex post-PR 👍 CLEAN (no findings).** PR [#127](https://github.com/CoJoA13/EasySynQ/pull/127), squash `8ab8a31`. Spec/plan: `docs/superpowers/{specs,plans}/2026-06-13-s-dcr-ui-2b*`.

- **S-dcr-ui-2a** DCR intake & early-state writes — **OPENS the DCR-UI WRITE track** (the first write slice atop the S-dcr-ui-1 read spine). **100% FRONT-END-ONLY** — no migration, no permission key, no endpoint, no contract change; **gate = `/check-web` only** (head stays `0051`). Every backend endpoint already existed (S-dcr-1…5); this slice is purely the SPA's create + early-edit affordances. **Owner brainstorm F-decisions** (each grounded in a 9-reader source-verification sweep over `api/dcr.py`/`services/dcr/service.py`/`domain/dcr/fsm.py`, NOT narrative): **F1 — split the S-dcr-ui-2 epic into ui-2a (Raise + edit/cancel, front-end-only) + ui-2b (the assess/route/implement/close cockpit + the `/tasks` DCR-approval leg + the `capabilities.implement` backend touch, own spec)**; **F2 — add a detail-only `capabilities.implement` enrichment** (the implement button is double-gated [`changeRequest.implement` + an in-handler `document.release`/`.obsolete` SoD-2 the `_dcr` serializer doesn't reflect] → deferred to ui-2b with the enrichment, the S-mr-3 Codex-#1 precedent, to avoid show-then-403); **F3 — deep-link-on-success only** (grep-verified there is **no `spawned_dcr_id` latch** — only `Complaint`/`ReviewOutput` carry `spawned_capa_id` — and the MR→DCR/CAPA→DCR spawns are **1:N idempotent** with the link living only on the *DCR* side → a persistent "View DCR" would need a backend "spawned DCRs" enrichment, declined; spawn rows are **Raise-only** and a successful raise `navigate("/dcrs?dcr=<id>")`); **F5 — wire the `/tasks` DCR-approval candidate-pool leg in ui-2b** (a signing `DecisionCard`, not a key). **The new `features/dcr/` write surface:** `mutations.ts` — `useRaiseDcr` (POST `/dcrs`, 201, `onSuccess` invalidates `["dcrs"]`), `usePatchDcr`/`useCancelDcr` (**`onSettled`** invalidate `["dcrs"]`+`["dcr",id]`+`["dcr-impact",id]` — a racing 409 `dcr_not_editable`/`dcr_not_cancellable` self-heals the drawer to the real state, the `useNcrDisposition` precedent), `useRaiseDcrFromCapa`/`useRaiseDcrFromMrOutput` (the two `Idempotency-Key` spawns; `SpawnDcrVars` shared) — all **non-optimistic** (the server is the truth). `DcrRaiseFields` — the shared controlled (value+onChange) form core: a `change_type` SegmentedControl, a **conditional target-document picker** (a searchable Select over `useDocuments({current_state:"Effective"},…)` — the hook has no free-text filter so the Select filters client-side; **hidden for CREATE, required for REVISE/RETIRE**, and switching to CREATE clears the target → the CREATE⟺no-target biconditional is enforced client-side so `create_has_target`/`target_required` 422s are unreachable from the UI), a significance SegmentedControl, a required reason Textarea, and an optional native `<TextInput type="date">` (no `@mantine/dates` dep) → `proposedEffectiveIso` (UTC-midnight ISO of the picked calendar date, R8). `RaiseDcrModal` (standalone create + a `reason_class` Select that **omits the MR-reserved `mgmt_review`** — the `RaiseCapaModal` SOURCES precedent), `SpawnDcrModal` (**parameterized — takes the bound mutation as a PROP** so ONE modal serves both the CAPA-drawer and the MR-output seams; a per-mount `crypto.randomUUID()` Idempotency-Key; on success `navigate("/dcrs?dcr=<id>")`; its submit is labelled **"Raise"** to match `RaiseDcrModal` and never share an accessible name with its caller's trigger), `EditDcrModal` (PATCH-while-Open, seeded; its dismiss button is **"Discard"** not "Cancel" since the DCR's own Cancel/withdraw is a sibling button in the same drawer panel), `CancelDcrModal` (optional comment), and `DcrAdvancePanel` (the drawer's action area — Edit gated `changeRequest.assess`+`state==="Open"`, Cancel gated `changeRequest.close`+`{Open,Assessed,Routed}` [the exact FSM cancel window]; **SYSTEM-scoped** `usePermissions()` because `_dcr` carries no `process_id` — the read-spine precedent; ui-2b grows this into the full assess/route/implement/close panel). **Wiring:** a `changeRequest.create`-gated "Raise DCR" header button on `DcrsRegisterPage` (`onCreated → setSelected(id)` opens the new DCR's drawer via the existing `?dcr=` seam), the `DcrAdvancePanel` in `DcrDrawer`, a "Raise change request" button on `CapaDrawer` (the **CAPA→DCR** seam), and a "Raise DCR" button on `ReviewOutputsSection` ACTION rows (`tracking && changeRequest.create`;
