the **MR→DCR** seam — **this closes the long-deferred S-mr-1/-2/-3 "MR→DCR front-end" deferral**). ⚠ **Patterns/traps confirmed:** **Mantine v7 `required` fields inject an aria-hidden ` *` into the label `textContent`** → query required-field labels with a **regex** (`getByLabelText(/Reason for change/)`); the exact string misses, and only `tsc`/the full run (not a per-file `vitest`) catches a wrong matcher. The **duplicate-accessible-name** trap (a trigger + a modal submit sharing "Raise change request" forces a `within(dialog)` test workaround) — resolved by the "Raise" submit rename. Every MSW fixture `satisfies` the real type (`dcrCreatedFixture satisfies Dcr`, the ReviewOutput fixture `satisfies ReviewOutput` — pinned to the real 8-field type incl. `management_review_id`). **Per-task two-stage review caught real issues:** the standalone dropdown leaking `mgmt_review`; the Edit "Cancel"/DCR-Cancel label collision (→ "Discard"); the trigger/submit accessible-name collision (→ "Raise"); a missing spawn error-path test (added — calm Alert, no navigate). **diff-critic = CLEAN** (it re-verified all 8 invariants against the actual backend + FE, not just the diff: invalidators mirror the read keys, the CREATE⟺target biconditional is doubly enforced, the affordance windows match the FSM exactly, idempotent 201/200 spawns, response-id deep-links, hook-order safety, `satisfies`-pinned fixtures, no CAPA/read regression). **Deferred (named, not faked) → S-dcr-ui-2b** (own spec): the assess/route/implement/close cockpit + the `capabilities.implement` enrichment + RETIRE `force_retire`/`override_justification` (409 `obsoletion_blocked`) + the close 409 `dcr_effectivity_pending` submit-and-show + the `/tasks` DCR-approval candidate-pool `DecisionCard` (outcomes approve/reject/changes_requested, `meaning=approval`) + **CREATE-implement** (no client `version_id→document_id` resolution exists — the matching write-side of the read-spine's CREATE-edge deferral) + the doc-15 §8.7/OpenAPI prose-drift fix (rides ui-2b's contract touch); → **S-dcr-ui-3**: the page-image visual diff + redline. **+21 web tests → the full suite is 800 green** (eslint + strict tsc + build + the whole vitest run; the full run via `--pool=forks --poolOptions.forks.singleFork=true` for a clean signal, per the known full-run-thrash note). **Codex post-PR triage: 4 of 8 P2s valid + fixed** (`a49fdd2`, +5 → **805**): the edit-path `mgmt_review` filter (symmetry with the standalone-Raise fix; kept selectable only when the DCR is already mgmt_review-classed), the silent cleared-effective-date no-op (refused honestly — PATCH can't clear a field), the terminal-CAPA Raise show-then-409 (hidden for Closed/Rejected), and the RETIRE effective-date misleading affordance (hidden for RETIRE). The other 4 are acknowledged-not-regressions: the SYSTEM-scoped affordance gating (#1/#4 — the documented v1 SYSTEM-fallback posture across the whole web track; the proper fix is **expanding the ui-2b `capabilities` enrichment** from `{implement}` to cover all DCR write affordances + gating the FE on `capabilities.*` not `can()`), the target-picker first-page-only (#3 — a named v1.x deferral needing a backend search param), and the create-without-read deep-link (#7 — a degenerate grant combo that degrades calmly to the no-access panel). subagent-driven TDD (10 tasks, per-task two-stage spec+quality review; precedent code extracted verbatim into the plan via a parallel-reader fan-out — and ⚠ one extraction Workflow **hung on a wedged agent** [a `parallel()` barrier cannot return past a single looping agent; the journal recorded only 1/5 results] → killed it and pivoted to targeted direct reads, no quality lost). Spec/plan: `docs/superpowers/{specs,plans}/2026-06-13-s-dcr-ui-2a-dcr-intake-writes*`.

- **S-dcr-ui-1** the DCR read spine — **the FIRST front-end for the Document Change Request domain** (the S-dcr-1…5 + S-dcr-3b backend shipped with **ZERO** SPA surface — `features/dcr/` didn't exist; the existing CAPA→DCR + the new S-mr-3 MR→DCR spawn loops both returned a deep-linkable DCR that spawned into a black hole). **100% FRONT-END-ONLY** — no migration, no new permission key, no new endpoint, no contract change; **gate = `/check-web` only** (head stays `0051`). **Owner brainstorm decisions** (each preceded by a six-reader source-verification sweep over `api/dcr.py`/`domain/dcr/fsm.py`, NOT slice-history narrative): **F1 read-spine-only** (lifecycle writes → S-dcr-ui-2, the page-image visual diff → S-dcr-ui-3 — the CAPA-epic cadence); **F5 strictly-front-end** (resolve the bare-UUID references client-side, no read-enrichment); **F3 `DcrStateBadge` + the append-only stage-event timeline** (no progress stepper — the 9-state machine branches: Rejected/Cancelled are off-happy-path terminals the timeline already encodes); **F4 a read-only resulting-version deep-link**. **The new `features/dcr/` module:** the three calm-403 read hooks (`useDcrs`/`useDcr`/`useDcrImpact` — `retry:false` + `forbidden = ApiError.status===403`, the CAPA precedent; the detail returns a bare `_dcr` + `stage_events[]`, the list/impact unwrap `{data}`), `DcrStateBadge` (a token-driven 9-state `Record` META map → label + a non-color glyph + `aria-label` "State: …", DP-7; the implementer found the real `--es-danger` token), `DcrStageTimeline` (Mantine `<Timeline>`, genesis renders bare `to_state` / transitions `from→to`, actor via the shared roster, `payload` JSONB deliberately NOT rendered — no XSS surface), `DcrImpactTable` (the 7-dimension assessment; `auto_populated` rendered as generic TEXT via `summarizeAuto`, never raw HTML), `DcrDrawer` (the `DetailDrawer` shell — NOT a raw Mantine `<Drawer>`; header gated `dcr && !isError`, body `isError || !dcr`; composes the three leaf components), `DcrsRegisterPage` (the `/dcrs` route — a filterable register table + the `?dcr=<id>` deep-link drawer seam mirroring `CapaBoardPage`; the **Identifier `Anchor component="button"` is the single interactive element per row** — no clickable `<tr>`, no inner Target link → a clean axe tree, *cleaner* than the CAPA list it descends from), a shared `labels.ts` (total `Record<Dcr*, string>` maps reused by the drawer + the register's title-case columns + the **exhaustive** filter `Select` data — a new enum member now breaks the build), and a `changeRequest.read`-gated `/dcrs` nav entry + flat route (the `/objectives`/`/management-reviews` precedent). **Strictly-front-end reference resolution (F5):** the drawer resolves the **target** doc identifier/title via the existing `useDocument(targetId, {enabled, retry:false})` and **calm-degrades** on a `document.read` 403/404 → a bare-id `/documents/{id}` link, never a crash (the requester may hold `changeRequest.read` but not `document.read` on the target); the **source** is navigation-only — `capa` → a `/capa?capa=<id>` deep-link, `finding`/`mgmt_review` → a labeled reference + short id (no clean client link — `source_link_id` is the finding/output id with no addressable SPA route); user names (`created_by`, stage `actor_id`) resolve through the codebase-wide `useUserDirectory()` roster (`find → display_name ?? id.slice(0,8)…`; null actor → "system"). ⚠ **CREATE-edge CONFIRMED deferred** (the verification sweep, not assumed): a CREATE DCR exposes only `resulting_version_id` on a NEW document whose id `_dcr` never emits, and there is **no top-level `GET /versions/{id}`** — every version read is nested under `/documents/{document_id}/` and rejects a mismatched doc id — so the SPA cannot resolve a bare `version_id → document_id`; the resulting-version link is REVISE/RETIRE-only (lands on the doc page, no `?version=` deep-link exists), CREATE shows the id as text. ⚠ **Web traps carried (verified, most in engineering-patterns "Web SPA testing"):** every fixture `satisfies <Type>` pinned to `api/dcr.py::_dcr` (the sweep CONFIRMED **15 `_dcr` fields, not the brief's "16"**, and **reused the existing `ChangeSignificance`** union rather than redeclaring it); `import { expect, it } from "vitest"` in every test (the jest-dom×tsc trap); a **jest-axe smoke on the register page** (heading-order guard); the **Mantine-v7 duplicate-`aria-label` `Select` trap** — `getByLabelText("State")` throws (the input AND the listbox share the label) → `getAllByLabelText("State")[0]`, the `CapaBoardPage` precedent; the **`?dcr` close-clears-URL test** (a `LocationProbe` + `{Escape}` asserting `replace:true` + the `params.has("dcr")` guard prevent a re-open-on-close regression). ⚠ **One review finding was a verified FALSE POSITIVE** (the receiving-code-review discipline paid off): a quality reviewer claimed `useDcr` returns a raw response needing `dcr.data?.data` — wrong; `lib/api.ts` `get<T>` returns the *parsed body* (confirmed by `CapaDrawer` consuming `useCapa().data` as the bare object), and the detail endpoint returns a bare `_dcr` with no `{data}` envelope, so the `.data` unwrap correctly lives only on the two list-shaped hooks. Two polish rounds folded review nits in (the exhaustive `CHANGE_TYPE_LABEL`; the shared `labels.ts` + title-case columns + the close-URL test); `formatDate` duplication was LEFT (the codebase has no shared date util by design — every component copies the inline `new Date(iso).toISOString().slice(0,10)` idiom). **Deferred (named, not faked) → S-dcr-ui-2/-3:** the Raise modal + the three spawn seams (a standalone `/dcrs` Raise + the CAPA-drawer + MR-output "Raise DCR" affordances — **this slice is the landing page that finally unblocks the S-mr-3 "MR→DCR front-end" deferral**); the lifecycle writes (edit-while-Open/cancel/assess/route/implement/close + the per-state/per-key `AdvancePanel` + the progress stepper); the **implement double-gate** (`changeRequest.implement` + the in-handler `document.release`/`.obsolete` SoD-2 → needs a detail-only `capabilities.implement` enrichment, the S-mr-3 Codex-#1 precedent, to avoid show-then-403) + the RETIRE `force_retire`/`override_justification` (409 `obsoletion_blocked`); the close 409 (`dcr_effectivity_pending`) surfaced calmly (submit-and-show, no client gate — the CAPA `CloseAction` precedent); the `/tasks` DCR-approval candidate-pool leg (the `DCR_TRIAGE` literal stays unwired); the **page-image visual diff + the text/metadata redline** against `resulting_version_id` (reuses `features/document/` `VisualDiffViewer`/`useVisualDiff`); the CREATE resulting-doc link; the stale doc-15 §8.7 prose (`reason_for_change`/nested `source_link`) + the cosmetic OpenAPI `dcr`-tag/`resulting_version_id` descriptions (touching the contract pulls in `/check-contracts` → left out to keep the slice front-end-only). **+17 web tests** (the `dcr` folder: badge ×2, timeline ×2, impact ×2, drawer ×4, register ×7) → the full suite is **779 green** (eslint + strict tsc + build + the whole vitest run). subagent-driven TDD (8 tasks, per-task two-stage spec+quality review; the precedent patterns were extracted verbatim into the plan via a parallel-reader fan-out so every step carried real code). Spec/plan: `docs/superpowers/{specs,plans}/2026-06-13-s-dcr-ui-1-dcr-read-spine*`.

## v1.x CONTINUAL IMPROVEMENT family — clause 10.3 (0052–0053, R46/R2) — opens the last named-but-unbuilt doc-14 §9 entity; a NON-★ own-table mutable-state workflow object (the DCR/R22 doctrine), NOT a shared-PK kind=DOCUMENT subtype; S-improvement-2 wires the OFI-finding + MR-output spawn endpoints (zero-migration); S-improvement-4 (0053) adds the signed, engine-routed Top-Management authorization — **family COMPLETE end-to-end**

- **S-improvement-4** the **engine-routed, signed Top-Management authorization** (clause 10.3, R46/R2) — **CLOSES the Improvement family** (the last, opt-in slice): the initiative's management-authorization now routes through the multi-stage workflow engine to a signed leadership sign-off — the **CAPA action-plan-approval template (`decide_capa_action_plan`) ported to an own-table subject.** **BACKEND (migration 0053) + thin FE.** A Completed initiative's manager opts in via **`POST /improvement-initiatives/{id}/request-authorization`**, opening an engine workflow routed to the seeded **"Top Management"** role; when a member signs (**`meaning=verify`**) via `POST /tasks/{id}/decision` (a new `IMPROVEMENT_INITIATIVE` dispatch leg), the sign-off **closes** the initiative, binding the `signature_event` to the new `Closed` `improvement_initiative_stage_event` via the **pre-generated-UUID seam** (two mutually-referencing INSERTs, never an UPDATE — the table is REVOKE-immutable). **`GET /{id}/authorization`** surfaces the cycle (the `GET /documents/{id}/approval` analogue). **Migration `0053`** = three additive enum `ADD VALUE`s in an `autocommit_block` — `workflow_subject_type.IMPROVEMENT_INITIATIVE` (the engine subject — ⚠ **the FIRST migration to USE a just-added enum value in its OWN same-migration seed**, so the autocommit-commit-then-seed ordering is load-bearing), `signed_object_type.improvement_initiative_stage_event`, `event_type.INITIATIVE_AUTHORIZED` — + a seeded `improvement_initiative_authorization` workflow_definition (single Top-Management **ANY** stage, `signature={meaning:verify}`; reuses the 0038 "Top Management" role + the 0052 `signed_event_id` hook; NOT-EXISTS-guarded downgrade). **NO new permission key** — the SIGN is candidate-pool (Top-Management) membership (the self-scoped-task doctrine); the request gates the existing `improvement.manage` @ PROCESS, the read gates `improvement.read`. **Contract:** the two endpoints + `WorkflowInstance.subject_type`/`Decision.outcome`/`DecisionResult.outcome` `+= IMPROVEMENT_INITIATIVE`/`verify` + `DecisionResult.initiative_stage` + `ImprovementInitiativeStageEvent.signed_event_id` + the two new auth-cycle schemas. **Owner decisions (asked in-plan):** `meaning=verify` (leadership verifies the realized benefit; reuses the closed R2 set — **no new meaning**); **additive/opt-in** (the existing unsigned `Completed→Closed` `/transition` close is UNTOUCHED — honors R46's "10.3 mandates no per-initiative sign-off"); a **single Top-Management ANY stage** (proportionate; fails closed to `NEEDS_ATTENTION` when no member is assigned); **backend + thin FE.** **Thin FE:** a `/tasks` `IMPROVEMENT_INITIATIVE` decision arm (`DecisionCard` verify/reject maps + `InitiativeApprovalContext` + the `isDocumentSubject` negation extended `&& !isImprovement`), a cockpit **"Request management authorization"** affordance (suppressed while a cycle is pending, "no Top-Management approver" on `NEEDS_ATTENTION`) + `RequestAuthorizationModal`, and an `InitiativeStageTimeline` "verified by leadership" marker. ⚠ **Codex found 9 real findings across 2 rounds that a CLEAN diff-critic + migration-reviewer + web-test-trap-reviewer ALL missed (the edge-finder lesson repeats HARD on a signed/engine-routed slice):** **round 1 (5×P2 + 1×P3):** (1) the generic engine ANY quorum treats EVERY positive `TaskOutcomeKind` (approve/complete/acknowledge/verify) as completing → a Top-Management caller could POST `{outcome:"approve"}` and mint a **`verify` signature** + close the initiative without a real verify decision → **a `{verify,reject}` allow-list** (the PERIODIC_REVIEW complete-only precedent); (2) the unsigned-close FE race (Close rendered during the auth-cycle load window) → gate close/request on the cycle query having **resolved** (`!isLoading && !isError`); (3) the required request body 422'd a body-less POST → `Model | None = None`; (4) `verify`/`initiative_stage` missing from the Decision contract; (5/P3) the IMPROVEMENT_INITIATIVE decision branch didn't invalidate `["my-tasks"]`; (6) **a single decline left the cycle pending** (ANY quorum fails only on all-reject) → **a decline is now DECISIVE** — force the instance `REJECTED` + skip sibling PENDING tasks (the **`decide_dcr_approval`** precedent), re-requestable. **round 2 (3×P2):** (7) the **unsigned `/transition` Closed must be 409'd while a non-terminal authorization instance exists** (the FE suppressed it, but a direct call would close unsigned + orphan the pending sign-off) → a backend guard in `transition_initiative`; (8) `WorkflowInstance.subject_type` enum in openapi missing `IMPROVEMENT_INITIATIVE`; (9) the verify/reject allow-list must run **AFTER** `_assert_initiative_authorizer` (else a non-pool caller gets a leaky 422 instead of the 404-collapse). ⚠ **The diff-critic DID catch its own P1 pre-PR:** the original reject test assumed a single-member pool (the shared CI DB always has ≥2 Top-Management members from `test_capa` + this file → one decline = PENDING, not REJECTED, under ANY) — made deterministic by the decisive-decline change (#6). ⚠ **the PostToolUse ruff hook strips a just-added unused import** bit THREE times (re-add AFTER the usage edit — the recurring S-statusbadge-2 trap). **Deferred (named, not faked):** document-backed leadership routing (MR/OBJ/POL sign-off — the named-enhancement deferral persists); a distinct unsigned "Verified" FSM node (folded into the authorized close); `improvement_initiative_action` milestone rows; objective-miss / 9.1.3 auto-seed; an effectiveness re-loop ("CAPA's 10.2 job"); OrgRole-based candidate-pool resolution (stays on the `Role.name` seam, R39); **no cancel/withdraw of a pending authorization** (re-requestable only after it terminalizes — verify→Closed or reject→REJECTED). **Test deltas: +7 api integration** (`test_improvement_authorization.py` — request→sign→close+`verify`-signature, request-requires-Completed, request-twice-409, decline-is-decisive+re-requestable+422-on-bad-outcome, non-member-404-collapse, idempotent-replay, unsigned-close-409-while-pending; **CI-authoritative in that Windows development environment**) **+ web 1009→1017 (+8:** ReviewApprovePage IMPROVEMENT_INITIATIVE arm ×4 + a11y smoke; InitiativeAdvancePanel request/suppress/needs-attention ×3). Verified: `/check-api` (ruff+mypy-strict+format) · **`/check-migrations` full round-trip up↔down↔`alembic check` on a throwaway PG16 — CLEAN** (the autocommit-then-seed ordering proven) · `/check-contracts` (redocly) · `/check-web` (eslint+strict-tsc+build+vitest) — all green; CI all 11 checks green on `ef4344b`. **Merge gate:** Codex 2 rounds (9 findings) all fixed + threads replied + resolved + green CI + owner's explicit go; merged `--admin` (behind only the docs-only #187, CI green — the documented fast-lane). One **generic `SpawnInitiativeModal`** (the `SpawnDcrModal` parameterized-modal precedent — takes a bound mutation + heading + `showProcessPicker`), reusing the manual `RaiseInitiativeModal` field layout; mints a **per-mount `Idempotency-Key`** (`useState(() => crypto.randomUUID())`); two `onSuccess`-invalidate spawn mutations (`useRaiseInitiativeFromFinding`/`useRaiseInitiativeFromMrOutput` — 200-replay IS success, no FSM race) + an `InitiativeSpawnBody` pinned to the `FindingInitiativeCreate`/`OutputInitiativeCreate` Pydantic models. **Owner decisions (asked in-plan):** (1) **Raise-only, no View-latch** — `spawned_initiative_id` stays reserved-null + no server `?source_link_id=` filter exists → no persistent "View" link (the Raise-DCR precedent); on success **deep-link `/improvement?initiative=<id>`** for instant visibility. (2) the **MR modal offers an optional process picker** (default none → SYSTEM fallback, homes the initiative to a process); the **finding side has NO picker** (process derived from the audit's auditee process server-side). (3) dashboard **"in progress" = `Open`+`InProgress`+`Completed`** (excludes terminal `Closed`/`Cancelled`). **Gating:** finding side gates `improvement.manage` at the **auditee-process scope** (`usePermissions(scope)`, the FE mirror of the backend `_finding_scope`) + hidden for NC/superseded; MR side gates `tracking && can("improvement.manage")` at **SYSTEM-default**, IDENTICAL to its sibling Raise-CAPA/Raise-DCR buttons; the **neutral line is NOT pushed to `rags`** so it can never drag the tile RAG. ⚠ **diff-critic + web-test-trap-reviewer BOTH CLEAN, but Codex found 2 P2s the clean reviewers missed (the edge-finder lesson repeats AGAIN):** (1) **FIXED `f03c012`** — `ActCard.allForbidden` folded in `init.forbidden`, but the initiatives **list endpoint is auth-only / filter-not-403** (`api/improvement.py:209` — returns an empty 200 for a no-grant caller, never a 403), so `init.forbidden` is **~never true** → a no-ACT-access user (all 3 actionable reads 403) rendered a misleading **"0 initiatives in progress" instead of `TileNoAccess`**; reverted `allForbidden`/`loading` to the **actionable reads only** (the init line is purely additive — when no-access wins the ternary shows TileNoAccess and the pushed line is never rendered). ⚠ **the GAP-2 coverage test I had added (a diff-critic-flagged uncovered branch) had itself CODIFIED the bug** (asserting the init line renders when the 3 actionable reads are forbidden) → flipped into a **regression guard** (no-access wins even when the filter-not-403 read returns data). **Lesson: a coverage-gap test written without re-deriving the CORRECT behaviour can entrench a defect — the adversarial reviewer that NAMES a gap is not the one that says what the gap's RIGHT answer is.** (2) **DEFERRED (pre-approved)** — the MR Raise button gates on SYSTEM `improvement.manage`, hiding it from a PROCESS-only holder the endpoint would authorize via the selected `process_id`; **identical to the sibling Raise-CAPA/Raise-DCR SYSTEM-gating** on the same section — the proper fix is an **any-process capability** covering all three MR raise buttons (`usePermissions` can't ask "at *any* process"); replied + resolved on-thread. **Deferred (named, not faked):** no persistent View link (no server `source_link_id` filter; reserved-null latch); un-reserving `review_output.spawned_initiative_id` (a `0053` owner call); MR-page any-process discoverability (v1.x, must cover CAPA/DCR/initiative together); **S-improvement-4** (opt-in) the Verified benefit stage / engine-routed mgmt-authorization. **Test delta: web 984→1009 (+25** — `SpawnInitiativeModal` disable-until-title / Idempotency-Key + onCreated / process-picker-iff-`showProcessPicker` / 422+409 calm Alert; ActCard count-non-terminal-only / neutral-never-raises-tile-RAG / **no-access-wins-over-filter-not-403** regression guard / partial-access / forbidden-degrades; **api unchanged, FE-only).** Verified: first-party `/check-web` green (eslint + strict tsc + build + **vitest 192 files / 1009 tests**); - **S-improvement-2** the Improvement Initiative **SPAWN endpoints** (clause 9.2/9.3 → 10.3) — wires the two ways to **raise** an initiative from an origin: **POST `/findings/{finding_id}/raise-initiative`** (OBSERVATION/OFI finding → `source=OFI`, `source_link_id=finding.id`; **422 `finding_not_improvable`** on an NC, **409 `finding_superseded`** on a corrected finding; inherits the audit's auditee process per R28 — `_auto_capa_for_finding`'s `plan.auditee_process_id`, not the soft `process_ref`) + **POST `/management-reviews/{review_id}/outputs/{output_id}/raise-initiative`** (ACTION/IMPROVEMENT output → `source=review`, `source_link_id=output.id`; **422 `output_not_improvable`** on a DECISION, **409 `review_not_tracking`** unless `close_state==ActionsTracked`; emits `MGMT_REVIEW_INITIATIVE_SPAWNED` on the MR doc). **ZERO-migration** — every seam shipped in `0052`: the `source_link_id`/`spawn_idempotency_key` columns + the `uq_improvement_initiative_spawn` partial-UNIQUE, the `MGMT_REVIEW_INITIATIVE_SPAWNED` event type + `improvement_initiative` audit object type, the `create_initiative(_commit=False)` compose seam, the `improvement.read`/`improvement.manage` keys (head stays `0052`, next `0053`). Both are **1:N + optional `Idempotency-Key`** (201 new / 200 replay via the `(org, source_link_id, key)` partial-UNIQUE), compose `create_initiative(_commit=False)` in ONE txn, and mint **no signature** (R43); the idempotent replay runs **before** the mutable close-state gate so a retry after the review closes still replays (the `spawn_dcr_for_output` precedent), with the `IntegrityError` rollback+re-query backstop (the `allocate_seq` IMP-seq increment rolls back with the txn → no counter leak). **Owner decisions (asked in-plan):** MR-output eligibility = **{ACTION, IMPROVEMENT}** (the reserved `IMPROVEMENT` output type is exactly this — broader than the CAPA/DCR siblings' ACTION-only); the MR back-link **`review_output.spawned_initiative_id` stays reserved-null** (1:N one-way via `initiative.source_link_id`; un-reserving the reciprocal latch is a future owner-flipped `0053` per the 0051 `spawned_capa_id` recipe — so the S-improvement-3 MR drawer finds initiatives by `source='review' AND source_link_id=output.id`, like DCRs). **Placement (parent-domain modules):** `services/mgmt_review/actions.py::spawn_initiative_for_output` (beside `spawn_capa`/`spawn_dcr`) + `services/audits/service.py::raise_initiative_from_finding` (beside `_auto_capa_for_finding`); both routes reuse `_initiative`/`_scope_for` imported from `api/improvement` (the `api/mgmt_review`→`api/dcr` cross-router precedent; `api/improvement` imports only services → no cycle). ⚠ **diff-critic CLEAN but Codex found 3 real P2s (all valid; the lesson repeats HARD — Codex, not the diff-critic, is the edge-finder):** (1) **replay scope authz** — the idempotent replay returned an initiative by `(org, source_link, key)` without re-authorizing its STORED scope → a process-B caller could read a process-A initiative via a known key; **fix:** both endpoints re-enforce `improvement.manage` against the returned `initiative.process_id` on a replay (gate-2; the finding endpoint keeps its `require()` gate-1 + adds an imperative gate-2). (2) **superseded findings** — a corrected finding (the audit close gate already excludes it) could still seed an initiative (an OBS later corrected to NC); **fix:** load the `Record` and reject `superseded_by_correction` → **409 `finding_superseded`**, checked AFTER the replay lookup so a prior valid raise still replays. (3) **tracking-window race** — the unlocked `close_state` read; **documented benign**, matching the `spawn_capa`/`spawn_dcr` siblings (the close gate reads only the spawned MR_ACTION task state, never spawned objects, so a just-after-close spawn is coherent) — a uniform lock across all three is a separate cross-cutting call (deferred). **Contract:** +2 paths in `openapi.yaml` (inline `additionalProperties:false` bodies, `Idempotency-Key` header, 201/200; the finding 409 added in the fix round) + a new **`docs/15 §8.12b` Improvement Initiatives** section (the family had no docs/15 entry). **Deferred (named, not faked):** **S-improvement-3** (the SPA register/InitiativeDrawer/`improvement_stage` StatusBadge/raise affordances/PDCA-ACT tile); **S-improvement-4** (opt-in Verified benefit stage / mgmt-authorization routing); the reciprocal `spawned_initiative_id` latch (a `0053` owner call); a uniform spawn-lock across capa/dcr/initiative. **Test delta: +15 integration (`test_improvement_spawn.py` — both origins × {happy, 422 ineligible, 404 unknown, idempotent replay incl. replay-after-close, authz deny}, the superseded→409, the cross-process-replay→403, `source`/`source_link_id`/process-inheritance, the MR-side audit event, no-signature, `spawned_initiative_id`-stays-null); api unit + web unchanged (backend-only).** Verified locally (Windows): ruff + ruff format --check + mypy `src` clean; redocly lint valid; import smoke + unit collection clean — integration is CI-authoritative (ProactorEventLoop/psycopg), all 4 shards green across both commits. Register **R46**. PR [#182](https://github.com/CoJoA13/EasySynQ/pull/182), squash `dcf9ea8`.
- **S-improvement-1** the Improvement Initiatives **BACKEND CORE** (clause 10.3 continual improvement) — **OPENS the Improvement Initiatives family** (mig `0052`, **R46**), the last named-but-unbuilt entity in doc 14 §9. **The doctrine:** per R46 an initiative is an **own-table mutable-state workflow object** (the DCR/R22 posture), NOT a kind=RECORD artifact and NOT a `documented_information` subtype — because clause 10.3 is **NON-★** (`is_mandatory_star=False`, no checklist node to flip), so the R44/R45 shared-PK kind=DOCUMENT rationale that OBJ/MR ride is *absent* and copying it here would be a category error. Mutable `stage` is the headline; the append-only `improvement_initiative_stage_event` (REVOKE UPDATE,DELETE; no `updated_at`) is the immutable history; events key on a fresh `audit_object_type='improvement_initiative'` (the ncr/dcr own-table precedent), `scope_ref=<identifier>`. **FSM** (`domain/improvement/fsm.py`, the dcr/capa pure-FSM precedent): unsigned `Open→InProgress→Completed→Closed` (+`Cancelled` reachable ONLY from `{Open,InProgress}`); `Closed`/`Cancelled` terminal + set `closed_at`; the `Closed` transition folds a free-text `outcome` into the sealed `stage_event.payload` (the lightweight 10.3 evidence — NOT a signed gate, NOT recomputed; no new `SignatureMeaning`, R2 closed). `transition_initiative` loads FOR UPDATE with `.execution_options(populate_existing=True)` (the S-drift-1 stale-identity-map trap) + a 409 `improvement_transition_invalid`, writing stage-event + stage flip + `INITIATIVE_TRANSITIONED` in ONE txn. Identifier `IMP-{YYYY}-{NNNN}` via `vault_repo.allocate_seq`. **6 endpoints:** POST/GET `/improvement-initiatives`, GET/PATCH `/improvement-initiatives/{id}`, GET `…/{id}/stage-events`, POST `…/{id}/transition`. **Authz:** list is auth-only + a **row-filter populating the FULL `ResourceContext.process_ids`** (the api/records.py filter-not-403 / S-pack-1 R28 precedent — never a hard 403); get + stage-events gate `improvement.read`; create enforces `improvement.manage` on the body's optional `process_id`; PATCH enforces manage on BOTH the current AND (on reassignment) the target process; create/patch 422 an unknown `owner_user_id`/`process_id` (closes a 500). ⚠ **diff-critic + migration-reviewer CRITICAL (fixed):** the four `improvement_initiative_stage_event` FKs were **bare** in the ORM → `Base.metadata` derived 63+-char convention names PG truncates (`initiative_id` 74, `signed_event_id` 69) → `alembic check` phantom-DROP → migrations CI red; fixed with explicit `name=fk_imp_init_stage_event_*` mirroring the migration (the clause_mapping/process_link 63-char precedent). ⚠ **diff-critic MAJOR (fixed):** reads originally gated at the default SYSTEM scope → hard-403'd a PROCESS-only holder; re-scoped via the full ResourceContext (a documented limitation remains: the `require(resolver)→locked-service` authz/lock TOCTOU is codebase-wide, not exploitable in v1 since every `improvement.manage` holder is effectively SYSTEM-scoped — the real fix lands with owner-assignment binding). ⚠ **Codex post-PR 2 P2s (fixed):** the migration's PROCESS role-grant used an unbound `:assignment_process` template that matched nothing → QMS Owner + Internal Auditor now get a real `{level: SYSTEM}` grant (Process Owner stays PROCESS-scoped, riding SYSTEM overrides until owner-assignment binding lands); + the unknown-UUID 500 above. **Migration `0052`:** two fresh native-PG enums from the ORM `*_VALUES` tuples (the 0010/0040 no-drift rule) — `improvement_stage` (Open/InProgress/Completed/Closed/Cancelled, title-case like `dcr_state`) + `improvement_source` (`OFI` uppercase to match `FindingType.OFI`; `review`/`manual` lowercase per R2/R16); `improvement_initiative` (mutable `stage` server_default 'Open', UNIQUE(org,identifier), RESTRICT FKs) **with all slice-2 spawn seams shipped now** — `source_link_id` (polymorphic 1:N one-way origin: finding.id for OFI, review_output.id for review, NULL for manual) + `spawn_idempotency_key` + the `uq_improvement_initiative_spawn` partial-UNIQUE (excluded from alembic-check in `migrations/env.py`) → **slice 2 is ZERO-migration**; the append-only `improvement_initiative_stage_event` (REVOKE UPDATE,DELETE from `easysynq_app`; `signed_event_id` is the **D3 Part-11 hook — a real FK present but NULL/unsigned in v1.x**, the `dcr_stage_event` precedent); `audit_object_type` += `improvement_initiative` + 4 event-type ADD VALUEs (`INITIATIVE_RAISED`/`_UPDATED`/`_TRANSITIONED` emitted now + `MGMT_REVIEW_INITIATIVE_SPAWNED` pre-provisioned for slice 2) in an autocommit_block; the two additive (R38) CONTENT-domain keys `improvement.read` + `improvement.manage` (**catalog 100→102**, `sig_hook=False`) + role grants. `openapi.yaml` +6 endpoints in-PR; the `create_initiative(_commit=False)` seam lets slice-2 spawn endpoints compose creation inside their own txn (the dcr/capa precedent). **Deferred (named, not faked):** **S-improvement-2** spawn wiring (POST `/findings/{id}/raise-initiative` — 422 `finding_not_improvable` unless `finding_type∈{OBSERVATION,OFI}`, Idempotency-Key — + POST `/management-reviews/{id}/outputs/{oid}/raise-initiative`; no `signature_event` per R43; leaves `review_output.spawned_initiative_id` reserved-null unless the owner flips the latch, a 0053 call per the 0051 `spawned_capa_id` recipe); **S-improvement-3** web (the Improvement › Continual Improvement register + InitiativeDrawer/stage-timeline + an `improvement_stage` StatusBadge + raise affordances on OFI/Observation findings & MR IMPROVEMENT/ACTION outputs + the PDCA-ACT quadrant tile); **S-improvement-4** (opt-in only) the optional unsigned **Verified benefit-review** stage (reuse `meaning='verify'` + the pre-generated-UUID seam if ever signed — never an UPDATE, the table is REVOKE-immutable) and/or an engine-routed management-authorization approval (a new `WorkflowSubjectType`); and the not-yet-slotted `improvement_initiative_action` **milestone rows** + the **objective-miss / 9.1.3 auto-seed**. **Test delta: +9 api unit (`test_improvement_fsm.py`) + 8 integration (`test_improvement.py` — lifecycle, FSM 409s, the cancel-window, the PROCESS-scoped row-filter authz regression, the REVOKE UPDATE,DELETE append-only proof [SQLSTATE 42501], R43 no-`signature_event`); `test_authz.py` + `test_quality_objectives.py` catalog-count assertions bumped 100→102; web unchanged (backend-only).** Verified locally (Windows): ruff + ruff format --check + mypy-strict clean; `test_improvement_fsm.py` 9/9; redocly lint valid; throwaway-PG16 alembic up↔down↔check clean — integration + full-unit are CI-only in that development environment. Register **R46**. Spec/plan: `docs/superpowers/{specs,plans}/2026-06-15-s-improvement-1-continual-improvement*`.
