Owner decisions **R40**: DCR is an own-table mutable-state workflow object [R22, NOT a record] · the InApproval changes-requested loop targets **Open** [doc 15 §8.7, superseding doc 05 §5.5's Routed] · keys = the seeded `changeRequest.*` family [R5] · scope-forks: **full** diff [meta+text+visual] + a **doc↔doc link table** for where-used · scheduled re-review D5 + drift D1–D4 stay in the v1.x drift family.

- **S-dcr-1** the DCR core + intake (`0040`) — `dcr` own-table (mutable `state` FSM Open→…→Closed/Cancelled/Rejected; `DCR-{YYYY}-{SEQ}` 4-digit id; `target_document_id` NULL⟺CREATE via the `ck_dcr_create_iff_no_target` CHECK; `source_link_id` polymorphic no-FK; `resulting_version_id` nullable seam) + append-only `dcr_stage_event` (`REVOKE UPDATE,DELETE`; `signed_event_id` FK present, populated S-dcr-4) + 4 enums + pure `domain/dcr/fsm.py` (InApproval changes-requested loop → Open; cancel from {Open,Assessed,Routed} only) + `audit_object_type=dcr` + `DCR_RAISED`/`DCR_UPDATED`/`DCR_TRANSITIONED`. Endpoints `POST/GET /dcrs`, `GET/PATCH /dcrs/{id}` (assess-gated edit-while-Open), `POST /dcrs/{id}/cancel`. Grant-backfill: orphaned `changeRequest.assess` + `changeRequest.close` → Process Owner + QMS Owner. NO new permission keys.
- **S-dcr-2** where-used / impact + assess (`0041`) — the NEW `document_link` doc↔doc graph (`parent_of`/`child_of`/`references`/`supersedes`; editable metadata `GRANT SELECT,INSERT,DELETE`, the `clause_mapping` precedent; CRUD `POST/GET/DELETE /documents/{id}/links` on `document.manage_metadata`) + `impact_assessment` (one row per the 7 doc-05-§5.3 dimensions, `UNIQUE(dcr_id,dimension)`) + `GET /documents/{id}/where-used` (`document.read` — the §7.2 categories over `process_link`/`document_link`/`clause_mapping`/records/`dcr.source_link`) + the pure §7.3 `obsoletion_blocked` predicate SURFACED as a `where-used.obsoletion_safety` advisory (governs-active-process / referenced-by-Effective / sole-★-coverage) — **enforcement deferred to S-dcr-5** per the R40 addendum (the shipped `document.obsolete` untouched) + `POST /dcrs/{id}/assess` (Open→Assessed; mirrors `cancel_dcr` + UPSERTs the impact rows from where-used in one txn; a CREATE DCR → all-N/A) + `GET/PUT /dcrs/{id}/impact`. NO new keys, NO Celery task. The S-dcr-1 `_dcr_doc_scope` (R28 process_ids) gates assess.
- **S-dcr-3a** redline/diff core (**ZERO-migration**, head stays `0041`) — `GET /documents/{id}/versions/{vid}/diff?from={vid2}` (gate **`document.read_draft`** — the diff exposes non-released version content, so `document.read` alone would leak Draft text to an Employee/Guest). The two doc-05-§8.1 core dimensions: **metadata diff** (pure `domain/diff/metadata.py` over the frozen `metadata_snapshot` SNAPSHOT_FIELDS) + **text redline** (pure `domain/diff/text.py` `difflib` line-LCS over on-demand Tika text via a `TextExtractor` seam; fail-closed → `text_diff: unavailable`) + the **provenance header** (`signature_event[]` projected to `{signer_user_id}` only). `document.diff` NOT seeded — the diff rides `document.read_draft` (R5). Owner chose **full diff incl. visual** → delivered as 3a + 3b.
- **S-dcr-3b** the visual page-image diff (`0042`) — **worker-async** (owner chose full coverage over in-request-PDF-only, since the **API can't render**: `LoggingRenderSink` no-op; rendering is worker-only). Deps **pypdfium2** (Apache/BSD prebuilt wheels — NOT PyMuPDF/fitz, passes `test_no_pymupdf_or_fitz_in_lockfile`; no system dep / air-gap impact) + Pillow `ImageChops`. NEW `visual_diff` cache table (`UNIQUE(from,to)` idempotency latch + cache key; `VisualDiffStatus` Pending/Ready/Failed/Unavailable; GRANT SELECT,INSERT,UPDATE — regenerable cache, not append-only) + `easysynq.visual_diff` Celery task (FOR-UPDATE + early-return-on-terminal idempotent; `acks_late` redelivery + re-POST self-heal — **no Beat reaper**) + pure `domain/diff/visual.py` (pypdfium2 rasterize @ scale 2.0 / cap 100 pages + Pillow per-page `ImageChops` overlay) + `services/diff/visual.py`. The task obtains each version's PDF (cache-hit on the mirror `rendition_blob_sha256`; else render via the worker's `GotenbergRenderSink` **TRANSIENTLY — NEVER writes `rendition_blob_sha256`**, else a Draft's diff-rendition [copy_status=state, no verify QR] would POISON the mirror's controlled-copy cache when it goes Effective — the diff-critic-preempted catch), rasterizes both, diffs page-by-page, caches each page's from/to/diff PNG as a content-addressed non-WORM `Blob` (no purge wiring — verified no path deletes a `document_version`'s blobs). Contract = **POST-compute (202) + pure-GET-poll (404-before-request) + `GET .../visual-diff/page/{n}?layer=from|to|diff` PNG stream** (the design-critic-mandated shape; the packs/imports async precedent), all gated `document.read_draft`. Watermark band-noise (footer band differs by rev → a changed footer region) accepted + documented for v1 (raw-render = v1.x). NO new permission key / event type. **The doc 05 §8 full diff (metadata + text + visual) is COMPLETE.**

- **S-dcr-4** routing + approval (`0043`) — DCR approval via the declarative engine (subject_type=DCR, the S-capa-2 pattern). Migration 0043 = ONE `signed_object_type ADD VALUE 'dcr'` (workflow_subject_type.DCR already existed from 0008) + the seeded `dcr_approval` `workflow_definition` (**ROUTER on `change_significance`**: MAJOR → `major_proc` [Process Owner, ANY] → `major_qms` [QMS Owner, ANY] SEQUENTIAL; MINOR → `minor_qms` [QMS Owner, ANY]; reuses the existing roles, no new role) + the `changeRequest.route` grant-backfill (Process Owner + QMS Owner, PROCESS-scoped). `route_dcr` (gate `changeRequest.route`): Assessed→Routed→InApproval atomically — instantiates the workflow w/ `context={change_significance}`; NEEDS_ATTENTION (empty pool) → fail-fast 409 `dcr_no_approvers` (rolls back, DCR stays Assessed). `POST /tasks/{id}/decision` extended with a **DCR dispatch** (DOCUMENT/CAPA byte-identical) → `decide_dcr_approval`: **per-approver signatures** (doc 05 §5.4 — EACH approve writes `signature_event(meaning=approval, signed_object_type='dcr', signed_object_id=<DCR id>)`; a MAJOR DCR → TWO; the `dcr_stage_event(InApproval→Approved).signed_event_id` links the sealing one); on the COMPLETING approve it flips →Approved + sets `decision/decided_by/decided_at`. **reject (→Rejected) / changes_requested (→Open, R40 loop) is DECISIVE** — one approver force-terminates the instance (`current_state=REJECTED` + skip sibling PENDING tasks under the instance lock), since the engine's ANY quorum won't fail on a single negative with other live candidates; a re-route opens a FRESH instance. Authz = `_assert_dcr_approver` (candidate-pool + live-role 404-collapse + cross-stage distinct-approver 409; NO key — `changeRequest.approve` stays ungranted, the CAPA precedent). NO new permission key / event type / Celery task.

- **S-dcr-5** implement/close + the shared-path obsoletion gate + the CAPA→DCR loop (`0044`) — **CLOSES the DCR family.** **DCR-as-orchestrator** (owner, reconciling doc 05/15's "DCR drives the release" over doc 10's "spawn-a-revision"): `POST /dcrs/{id}/implement` (gate `changeRequest.implement`, already-seeded) drives the vault action **atomically by construction** — REVISE releases the target's Approved revision / CREATE releases the authored `resulting_version_id` / RETIRE obsoletes — by setting the version's `effective_from` (`proposed_effective_from` or now) + the cross-FK link + flip→Implemented in ONE commit, then the EXISTING `release_due` Beat sweep performs the SERIALIZABLE single-Effective cutover (DCR-driven release = system-attributed; **no `_cutover` change, no new reaper**); RETIRE folds the flip into `lifecycle.obsolete`'s own commit. **No DCR side-door past document control**: the implement endpoint ALSO `enforce`s `document.release` (REVISE/CREATE, full SoD-2 overlay — the PDP keys SoD on `document.release`, so a hand-rolled check would skip the approver-side leg) / `document.obsolete` (RETIRE) IN ADDITION to `changeRequest.implement` — so a revision's author cannot self-implement (403 `sod_violation`). Shared `enrich_release_sod_scope` (extracted from `documents.py::_release_scope`) is reused by both the direct release endpoint + the implement path. `POST /dcrs/{id}/close` (gate `changeRequest.close`): Implemented→Closed requires the change actually-effective (§5.5; 409 `dcr_effectivity_pending`). **The §7.3 obsoletion gate moves to the SHARED `lifecycle.obsolete()`** — BOTH the direct `POST /documents/{id}/obsolete` AND the DCR RETIRE-implement now 409 `obsoletion_blocked` on a coverage gap unless `force_retire` + `override_justification` (recorded on the signature intent + audit), **superseding the S-dcr-2 addendum** (no bypass; T11-only). The §7.3 reads + `evaluate_obsoletion` centralized in NEW `services/vault/obsoletion.py` (the where-used advisory consumes the same fn — gate + advisory can't diverge; dcr→vault, no cycle; `sole_star_coverage` moved out of `dcr_repo`). **CAPA→DCR loop = dedicated `POST /capas/{id}/raise-dcr`** (gate `changeRequest.create`; `raise_dcr_from_capa` → `raise_dcr(_commit=False, source_link_type=capa)`): **1:N** (a CAPA may spawn child DCRs — NO latch), an `Idempotency-Key` (NEW `dcr.spawn_idempotency_key` partial-UNIQUE) makes a retry return the same DCR (201 new / 200 replay); a terminal CAPA → 409 `capa_terminal`. **The deferred cross-FK** `document_version.dcr_id` ↔ `dcr.resulting_version_id` lands (`use_alter` 2-table cycle, both named FKs via `op.create_foreign_key`, the `capa.origin_finding_id`↔`audit_finding` precedent). Mig `0044` = the cross-FK pair + `spawn_idempotency_key` + its partial-UNIQUE (excluded in `env.py`) + the `changeRequest.implement` grant-backfill (Process Owner + QMS Owner). NO new permission key (R5) / event type (reuse `DCR_TRANSITIONED`) / enum value (`Implemented`/`Closed` exist since 0040) / Celery task.

**The DCR family (doc 05, the Revision & Change Depth family) is now COMPLETE ✅.** Deferred to the v1.x drift family: scheduled re-review (D5) + drift detection (D1–D4).

## v1 WEB UI track (apps/web — React 18 + Mantine 7 SPA) — STARTED

The SPA was MVP-deferred to just the setup wizard + admin stubs while the API matured. This track ports the owner-approved mockup (`mockup/easysynq-mockup.html`, 8 screens; `styles.css` = 722 design tokens) into the live app as a **vertical operational journey** (UJ-3 review→approve) in 3 slices. Stack: React 18 + Mantine 7 + TanStack Query 5 + react-router 7 + Tailwind 3; tests are **stack-free** via vitest + @testing-library/react + **MSW** + **jest-axe** (the web CI job now also runs `npm test`). WCAG 2.2 AA is a release gate (jest-axe + eslint-jsx-a11y). Spec + plan: `docs/superpowers/{specs,plans}/2026-06-06-*`.

- **S-web-1** app shell + design-system foundation (**front-end only — NO migration**) — the single-source **token port** (`mockup/styles.css` `--es-*` → `src/theme/tokens.css`, consumed by BOTH the Mantine theme [system fonts, indigo primary] AND Tailwind; the mockup's `[data-theme="dark"]` re-keyed to `:root[data-mantine-color-scheme="dark"]`); the **app shell** on Mantine `AppShell` (TopBar [disabled ⌘K slot, task/ack bells, user-menu logout] · LeftRail [PLAN/DO/CHECK/ACT clause spine from `GET /clauses` + Home/Library] · Breadcrumb · a deep-linkable `?detail=` resizable focus-trapped **DetailDrawer** · skip-link [zIndex above the header — WCAG 2.2 Focus-Not-Obscured] + ARIA landmarks); an **AuthProvider** context + a token-aware **`useApi()`** client + React Query hooks (`useDocuments`, `useClauses`); **routing** = `AppShell` at `/` (Home index + `/library` live; `/documents/:id` + `/tasks/:id` reserved; `/setup` + `/admin` unchanged); a **thin Library list** from `GET /documents` (Identifier · Title · State badge · Clause chips — friendly type/owner deferred [the list endpoint returns only IDs] + no next-review column [`next_review_due` doesn't exist until the drift family]); a calm **Home placeholder** (full PDCA dashboard = a later slice). 12 vitest tests incl. axe; lint/typecheck/build/test all green. Method: brainstorm (visual companion) → owner forks (vertical journey · 3-slice plan) → spec+plan → subagent-driven TDD with per-logic-task spec+quality review (caught & fixed pre-merge: a `render.tsx` task-ordering bug, the Task-5/6 `StateBadge`↔`DocumentDrawer` import cycle, an `api.ts` import-placement nit, the LibraryPage deep-link empty-shell flash, and a skip-link z-index a11y bug) → whole-branch review **READY TO MERGE**.

- **S-web-2** faceted Library + read-only tabbed detail drawer (**full-stack; NO migration**) — owner forks: API fidelity = **add minimal read endpoints**; **split into two PRs** (S-web-2 = Library; the standalone Document *page* → S-web-3; Review & Approve → S-web-4); drawer tabs = **Overview + History + Where-used**. **Backend** (no migration, no new permission keys): `GET /document-types` (`api/document_types.py`) + `GET /directory/users` (`api/directory.py`) — both **authentication-only** (matching `GET /documents`' entry gate; `require("document.read")` resolves at SYSTEM scope → would exclude ordinary readers), the directory returns **`{id, display_name}` ONLY** (minimal disclosure, ACTIVE non-guest); `GET /documents` gains a **`{data, page}` pagination envelope** with `offset` (**authz-correct**: scan a bounded candidate window → per-row `document.read` filter → in-memory slice + derived `has_more`, NOT a pre-authz SQL OFFSET; `_LIST_SCAN_CAP=2000`, scale-to-SQL deferred per R34), a `(effective_from, gte/lte)` **date filter** (join the current effective version), and an **`effective_from`** field on the row (batch `_effective_from_map`, the Effective column + header date). `/me/permissions` deliberately deferred to the write slice (S-web-4). **Front-end** (`apps/web`): a 2-col Library — an in-page PDCA-banded **ClauseTree** filter (top+child clauses, exact-number match, no counts) + a 5-facet **FacetBar** (Type/Status/Owner/Clause/Effective-date) with **URL-driven** removable chips + a real **Pagination** (page-size + prev/next on `has_more`) + a results table (resolved Type/Owner via the cached `useDocumentTypes`/`useUserDirectory` maps, 7-state `StateBadge` [now icon+label, moved to `features/document/`], Effective date) + density toggle + skeleton/empty/permission-empty states; a deep-linkable (`?detail=`) **tabbed `DocumentDrawer`** with a shared **`ArtifactHeader`** (DP-5, reused by S-web-3) always-on above lazy-loaded Overview (control-metadata + authenticated controlled-copy download) / History (version timeline; **403→quiet no-access** for a reader lacking `document.read_draft`) / Where-used (§7.2 categories) tabs. Clean dependency direction: `features/library/` → `features/document/` (no cycle). 23 vitest tests incl. jest-axe; api unit + integration (authz-correct pagination proven with a scoped owner filter; directory minimal-disclosure asserted); openapi.yaml + redocly updated. Deferred (honest, mockup features the backend can't yet serve): the standalone Document page (S-web-3), Approvals/Acks/Audit tabs, per-clause counts, the Process-map/PDCA lens switcher, all write actions, `next_review_due`. **Next web slices:** S-web-3 (Document detail page) · S-web-4 (Review & Approve — closes UJ-3).

- **S-web-3** Document Authoring — the author's half of UJ-3 (**full-stack; NO migration; head stays `0044`**). **Re-sequence (supersedes S-web-2's note):** S-web-3 = **Authoring** (this) · S-web-4 = read-only Document **detail page** · S-web-5 = **Review & Approve** (closes UJ-3). Owner forks (3): scope = **create → submit-review** (approve/reviewer-inbox/release deferred to S-web-5, forced by SoD-1 HARD_DENY + the self-scoped `GET /tasks`); affordance gating = **`GET /me/permissions` + a per-document `capabilities` block** (migration-free, **NO new key** — own-data, R5/R38 untouched); UX = a **New-Document wizard + "Author actions" in the existing drawer**. **Backend:** `GET /me/permissions` (auth-only, self-scoped — NOT `user.read`-gated; reuses a shared `compute_effective_permissions` extracted from the admin `effective-permissions`, byte-identical, so the old endpoint is unchanged) + a **detail-only** `capabilities` map on `GET /documents/{id}` (`_document_capabilities` mirrors the PEP's `evaluate()` calling the pure PDP **directly — no audit row per probe**; **`release` is SoD-correct**: enriched scope over the latest Approved version + `sod`/`allow_approver_release`, so the version author is HARD-denied; `obsolete` sig_hook; never on the list serializer). **Front-end** (`features/authoring/`, one-way dep → `features/document/`): the **New-Document wizard** (Mantine Stepper metadata→upload→clauses→submit; creates the Draft at step 1 — orphan-on-abandon is the documented v1 limit, no T8 discard), an **`AuthorActions`** section in the S-web-2 `DocumentDrawer` (capability + state + lock gated, DP-6 quiet-absence; Draft/UnderRevision → check-in + clause-map + submit, Effective → start-revision, InReview → "awaiting review"), a shared **`CheckInPanel`** (doc-11 §5.4: check-out → presigned PUT upload [**raw cross-origin `fetch`, NO bearer** — `lib/upload.ts` PUTs an ArrayBuffer; client SHA-256 `lib/hash.ts`] → check-in, MAJOR/MINOR + INV-3 reason; break-lock on a 409) + **`ClauseMapper`** + `usePermissions().can()` gating the Library "New document" entry (coarse SYSTEM scope; the DOC_CLASS under-claim documented). **Tests:** 6 api integration (the SoD-correct `release` — author=False, neutral=True, approver=False; `/me/permissions` not-`user.read`-gated; run-scoped) + **46 web** (vitest+MSW+jest-axe: the wizard E2E, the upload-no-bearer + dedup-skip, AuthorActions gating, a test-only jsdom `Blob.arrayBuffer` polyfill); openapi.yaml + redocly updated. Method: 6-agent exploration → owner forks (3 decisions) → spec+plan (`docs/superpowers/.../2026-06-07-*s-web-3*`) → TDD → 4-lens diff-critic review (**1 minor fix folded:** `CheckInPanel` now resets `checkedOut` after check-in — the lock is released server-side, so a 2nd drawer revision re-checks-out instead of 409-ing). **⚠ Demo precondition:** grant the demo System Administrator the authoring keys via **SYSTEM overrides** — the brief's `grant-role "QMS Owner"` is reads-only (QMS Owner holds no authoring write keys). **Next:** S-web-4 (read-only Document detail page) · S-web-5 (Review & Approve — closes UJ-3).

- **S-web-3 follow-ups** (post-merge, dev/infra — PRs #89/#90/#91; all verified live, the browser authoring flow now works end-to-end): (1) **`just seed-personas`** (#89) — a SoD-correct author/approver/releaser CLI+recipe (`cli/seed_personas.py` + `scripts/easysynq seed-personas` + the `just` recipe that also provisions the Keycloak logins `priya`/`ken`/`mara`); idempotent, single-org-fallback (an operational install may use a non-`DEFAULT` org short code), the S-web-5 fixture. (2) **Browser-reachable presigned MinIO** (#90) — `storage._presign` now signs **AGAINST** `s3_public_endpoint` via a separate `_presign_client` (set `S3_PUBLIC_ENDPOINT=http://localhost:9000`; the `s` profile publishes 9000), because **SigV4 signs the host** so a post-signing host-rewrite → `SignatureDoesNotMatch`; internal worker fetches keep using the internal `_client`/`fetch_bytes`, so they're unaffected (MinIO default CORS `*` covers the cross-origin PUT). (3) **Reload re-auth** (#91) — the SPA's **in-memory-only tokens** (`lib/auth`) left an operational reload stranded in 401s with no sign-in path; `App.tsx` now auto-bounces a token-less operational load to Keycloak (one-shot `sessionStorage` loop-guard + a manual "Sign in again" fallback + a "Signing in…" interstitial). ⚠ Gotcha: a new CLI module / `storage.py` change isn't in the running image until `docker compose … build api` (CI runs from source, not the image).

- **S-web-4** read-only Document detail page + the text/metadata redline (**front-end only; NO migration [head stays `0044`], NO new permission key, NO `openapi.yaml` change** — every read already existed AND was already contracted: `GET /documents/{id}` `capabilities`, `…/versions`, `…/versions/{vid}/diff?from=`, `…/where-used`, `…/download`). Owner forks (3): **D-A** the `/documents/:id` page **reuses the capability-gated `AuthorActions`** (not strictly read-only — readers see read-only, authors see Check out / Start revision / Submit, DP-6 quiet-absence); **D-B** ship the **synchronous** text redline + metadata-diff this slice, the worker-async **visual page-image diff → S-web-4b**; **D-C** the rendition preview = a **card with Open/Download links** (no embedded PDF.js). **Page** (`features/document/DocumentDetailPage.tsx`, swaps the `App.tsx` `/documents/:id` Reserved stub): `ArtifactHeader` (verbatim) + `AuthorActions` (verbatim) + reduced metric tiles (Governing rev [resolved from the version matching `current_effective_version_id`] · Mapped clauses · Versions; **Acknowledged + Days-to-review omitted** — drift family, not faked) + a responsive two-column body (left: `RenditionCard` + Where-used [`WhereUsedTab`]; right: Version history [`HistoryTab`] + the Compare/redline + Control metadata). **Redline** (doc 11 §4.7): `useVersionDiff` (`GET …/versions/{vid}/diff?from=`, gate **`document.read_draft`**, 403→quiet) + `RedlineViewer` (metadata-diff table over `changed` rows + the inline text redline — backend **line-level** LCS hunks rendered `<ins>`/`<del>` with **non-color** `+`/`−` markers (DP-7), `n`/`p` keyboard nav + a screen-reader change index; `text_diff:"unavailable"` → a calm source-download fallback, NOT an error) + `VersionCompare` (**URL-driven** `?from=&to=` version-pair picker on the History card — the S-web-2/3 URL-state discipline). **Reuse + small extractions:** `ControlMetadata` + `useControlledCopyDownload` extracted from `OverviewTab` (the drawer's rendered output is **byte-identical** — parity-tested); the shell `Breadcrumb` resolves a `/documents/:id` leaf to the **identifier** (not the raw UUID) via the query cache; `DocumentDrawer` gains an **"⤢ Open full page"** link (doc 11 §4.3 promote-to-page). New code lives in `features/document/` and reuses `features/authoring/AuthorActions` exactly as the drawer does (**no new import cycle** — `AuthorActions` imports nothing from `features/document/`). **Tests:** **+34 web** (vitest+MSW+jest-axe: page load/404/403/cap-gating/export-gating; the redline markers/`n`-`p`-nav/unavailable/403; the rendition card; `ControlMetadata` parity; `VersionCompare` URL-state; the breadcrumb + the drawer link) — **jest-axe `toHaveNoViolations` release gate** on the page (read-only **and** with the author cluster), the `RedlineViewer`, and the `RenditionCard`. Method: spec+plan (`docs/superpowers/.../2026-06-08-*s-web-4*`) → TDD → diff-critic (MINOR, 3 fixes folded) → PR #93 → **Codex review** (2× P2): the shell `Breadcrumb` now subscribes to `['document', id]` via a fetch-less `useQuery` observer (a bare `getQueryData()` read never re-rendered, so a cold/bookmarked link stuck on the "Document" fallback) + the reactive test that was missing; the mockup's **Export-controlled-copy button was dropped** — its only correct (document-scoped) gate is a backend `capabilities.export` addition (out of this front-end-only slice), and no seeded role holds `document.export` so it was invisible in v1 anyway (controlled-copy access stays on the rendition card's `document.read` download; reinstating Export later is a one-field capabilities addition). **Honest deferrals:** the visual page-image diff → **S-web-4b**; the **Approvals** tab (no document→workflow-instance lookup exists) → S-web-5; **Acknowledgements** + the **Next-review** tile (drift family); the **Audit** tab (`system.audit_log.read`, SYSTEM-gated); the Process-map lens; the DCR-orchestrated "New revision (DCR)" (the page uses plain `start-revision`). **Next:** S-web-4b (visual page-image diff viewer) · S-web-5 (Review & Approve — closes UJ-3).

- **S-web-4b** the worker-async **visual page-image diff viewer** — the doc 11 §4.7 Side-by-side / page-image mode S-web-4 deferred (**front-end only; NO migration [head stays `0044`], NO new permission key, NO `openapi.yaml` change** — the worker-async backend trio was already built + contracted in **S-dcr-3b**). Owner forks (3): **D-A** layer UX = a **single image pane + a Before/After/Diff toggle** (default Diff = the server-composed overlay), rendered via the **authed `fetch → blob → objectURL → <img>`** path — the page-PNG endpoint is **authenticated, NOT presigned**, so a bare `<img src>` would 403 (NOT side-by-side synced-scroll — PDF.js is out of the track per S-web-4 D-C; NOT onion-skin/canvas); **D-B** the **Text | Visual `SegmentedControl` lives in `VersionCompare`**, swapping the **byte-identical** S-web-4 `RedlineViewer` (Text) and a new `VisualDiffViewer` (Visual) as siblings — URL-driven `?mode=visual`, reusing the same `?from=&to=` pair; **D-C** the **full** viewer (the §4.7/§6.2 bar), not a minimal single-layer image. **The worker-async contract** (`useVisualDiff`, the S-dcr-3b "POST-compute / pure-GET-poll / stream a page sub-resource" shape): a **POST trigger** (idempotent — UPSERTs the cache row + enqueues only while Pending) seeds the poll cache so the **GET poll** never races the 404-before-request, and `refetchInterval` runs **only while `status==="Pending"`** and halts at any terminal status (the `SetupWizard` precedent); a terminal POST never fires a redundant GET. **`VisualDiffViewer`** (`features/document/`): the single image pane (a new **`apiGetBlob`/`useApi().getBlob`** authed-binary helper — the **only** API-proxied binary in the SPA, every other download is a presigned URL — fetches each page+layer PNG → `objectURL`, **revoked** on every page/layer change + unmount, no leak) + a **changed-page rail** (the §4.7 minimap AND the §6.2 screen-reader change index, driven by `pages[].changed` with a **non-color** glyph + textual "changed") + the Before/After/Diff toggle + **`n`/`p`** next/previous-changed-page nav + a phased **§4.9 long-op** poll (skeletons matching final layout, an `aria-live` region, a manual "Re-request render" for the dev renderer-off "Pending forever" case — never a frozen UI). **Calm terminal states** (not errors): `Unavailable` (a non-renderable R26 version) → a source-download fallback; a page-endpoint **404** ("No image for this page/layer" — an added page's Before / a removed page's After) → a per-side note; `Failed` → a calm terminal with the same source-download fallback (**NOT re-drivable** — `get_or_create_visual_diff` re-enqueues only a `Pending` row, so the viewer offers **no dead Retry** on a terminal Failed; the re-request affordance lives on `Pending` only, for the dev renderer-off case); **403 → quiet** (DP-6). **The hook is keyed by the pair** — `status` is read from the pair-keyed poll cache (seeded by the POST), so changing versions never flashes the prior pair's pages or fires page requests for a not-yet-requested diff; the viewer resets the selected page on a pair change. **0-based** `pages[].page`, labelled 1-based in the UI. **Honest v1 caveat surfaced in-copy:** the watermarked rendition's footer band differs by revision → a near-universal "changed" footer region (raw render = v1.x). **Tests:** **+26 web** across `useVisualDiff` (POST-then-poll→Ready, terminal-without-redundant-GET, Failed/Unavailable/403, `retry()` re-POST), `VisualDiffViewer` (authed page fetch + alt + objectURL, the layer toggle re-fetching `?layer=`, the 404 no-image + non-404 error branches, `n`/`p`, the objectURL **revoke-on-change**, Pending/Failed/Unavailable/403), and `VersionCompare` (the Text|Visual switch + `?mode=` deep-link, RedlineViewer unchanged) — **jest-axe `toHaveNoViolations` release gate** on `VisualDiffViewer` (each state) + `VersionCompare` (both modes); the full web suite stays green (`RedlineViewer.test.tsx` byte-identical). Method: spec+plan (`docs/superpowers/.../2026-06-08-*s-web-4b*`) → TDD → **diff-critic = CLEAN** (2 minor coverage gaps folded: the objectURL revoke + the non-404 image-error branch). Live: the 3 visual-diff routes confirmed **mounted on the running api** (401 vs a 404 control); the full visual click-through needs a 2-version Effective doc (the dev vault has none) + a real render (renderer/worker up). **Next:** S-web-5 (Review & Approve — closes UJ-3).

- **S-web-5** Review & Approve — **CLOSES UJ-3** (author → review → approve → release) (**full-stack but thin: ONE migration-free read endpoint, NO migration [head stays `0044`], NO new permission key**; every write [`submit-review`, `POST /tasks/{id}/decision`, `release`] + most reads [`GET /tasks`, `/tasks/{id}`, `/workflow-instances/{id}`, the per-doc `capabilities`, the redline] were already contracted + implemented). Owner forks (4, via `AskUserQuestion`): **both surfaces** (a `/tasks` inbox route + a per-task Review & Approve page **and** an Approvals stepper card on the doc page); **include release** (full UJ-3, gated on the already-SoD-2-enriched `capabilities.release`); the discovery endpoint gated **`document.read` on the subject** (migration-free, no new key); **DP-6 gating sources** = approve/reject via **task candidate-pool membership** (task visibility — there is intentionally no `capabilities.approve`; approve is task-routed) + release via `capabilities.release`. **Backend (the only addition):** `GET /documents/{id}/approval` (`api/workflow.py`, tag `documents`) → the document's **latest** `workflow_instance` (+ tasks) or **`null`** when never submitted (calm 200-null, not 404; React-Query needs non-`undefined`). New repo helper `latest_instance_for_subject` = **latest, ANY state** (NOT `find_nonterminal_instance`): `lifecycle.release` never closes the instance (it lingers `APPROVED` for an Effective doc — which the stepper *wants* to show) and a `NEEDS_ATTENTION` empty-pool instance must still surface. Gate + serializer reuse `get_instance_endpoint` verbatim (the closed catalog has **no `task.*`/`workflow.*` key** — `document.read` on the subject is the real-world gate). 5 integration tests (instance+tasks / null-never-submitted / 404-unknown / 403-no-read / latest-returns-the-decided-APPROVED-instance; the empty-pool NEEDS_ATTENTION case isn't isolable in the shared-DB integration suite — other tests' org-wide Approver grants populate the pool — so it rides code review). **Front-end** (new `features/review/` + doc-side additions in `features/document/`, dep direction `review → document → lib`, acyclic): a `/tasks` **TasksInbox** (self-scoped `GET /tasks`; task/stage/state/due + link — the per-row Document column deferred, it'd N+1 instance→doc; shown on the review page instead), a per-task **ReviewApprovePage** (task → instance → subject doc → the **reused `VersionCompare`/redline** + the **`DecisionCard`** for a PENDING task; a DONE task shows a read-only "Decided"), the **`DecisionCard`** (Approve / Request-changes / Reject radiogroup; comment **conditional-required** for changes/reject [`aria-describedby` rule]; a DP-10 **signature slot** [a "meaning = approval" confirm checkbox + the honest "v1 — single-factor logged confirmation"]; 403 `sod_violation`/409-already-decided/`step_up_required` all rendered **calmly**; a per-mount `crypto.randomUUID()` **Idempotency-Key** so a retry replays not 409s), an **`ApprovalStepper`** (`features/document/`; the doc-11 §4.4 vertical stepper, **derives nodes from the instance + tasks + the doc's own state** — submit / quality-approval [current/done/rejected per task+instance] / release-to-effective [from the doc `Effective`/`effective_from`, authoritative] — **non-color** glyph+label DP-7, `aria-current="step"`; renders the *actual* single stage, NOT the mockup's aspirational multi-stage spine), the **`ApprovalsTab`** card on `DocumentDetailPage` (stepper + a `capabilities.release`+`Approved`-gated **Release** button + a candidate-only "Review & approve →" deep-link), a **`TaskStateBadge`** (DP-7), and the hooks (`useDocumentApproval`, `useTasks`/`useTask`/`useWorkflowInstance`/`useDecideTask`, `useReleaseDocument`, `useMe`). `lib/api.ts` gains an optional `headers` arg on `send` (the Idempotency-Key path, backward-compatible); `App.tsx` swaps the `tasks/:id` `Reserved` stub for the real `/tasks` + `/tasks/:id` routes; `LeftRail` adds the "Review & Approve" nav. **Tests:** 5 api integration (CI/Linux gate — see the Windows note below) + **+28 web** (vitest+MSW+jest-axe across the hooks, stepper node-derivation per state, DecisionCard conditional-comment/SoD-403/a11y, inbox, review page, the doc-page card; the full suite = **132 web tests** green). Method: 7-agent exploration → owner forks (4 decisions) → spec+plan (`docs/superpowers/.../2026-06-08-*s-web-5*`) → TDD → **diff-critic = 1 CRITICAL, fixed + regression-tested**: `ApprovalsTab` gated the approve CTA on `user.profile.sub` (the OIDC **Keycloak subject**) vs `candidate_pool`/`assignee_user_id`, which are **`app_user.id` UUIDs** — a *different* identifier in production, so the "Review & approve" link would never render for the real approver; **the fixtures had collapsed `sub == /me.id == candidate_pool[0]` (a textbook false-PASS).** Fix = a new `useMe()` (`GET /me` → `app_user.id`), gate on `me.id`, + a regression test with `sub ≠ me.id`. **⚠ The full create→approve→release loop needs 3 DISTINCT users** (SoD-1/2 non-overridable): the `priya`/`ken`/`mara` `just seed-personas` fixture. **Live smoke (DONE — full 3-persona loop in the browser):** priya authored SOP-PUR-002 (Rev A, clause 8.4) → submit-review; **ken** saw it in the new `/tasks` inbox → Review & Approve page → Approve (+ the DP-10 signature confirm) → Approved; **mara** opened the doc page → the Approvals stepper (Submitted ✓ / Approved by Ken ✓ / Released ◉) → **Release** → **Effective**. The `/me.id` fix was genuinely exercised (ken's app_user id was in the candidate pool; his Keycloak `sub` was not). Two **fixture** gaps surfaced + fixed for the demo: the seeded author lacked **`clauseMap.read`** (added to `seed_personas._AUTHOR_KEYS`, the UI clause picker needs it) and the seeded Keycloak users lacked an email/name profile (set via `kcadm` to clear the VERIFY_PROFILE required action — a one-time dev step, not code). Browser-automation note for next time: in-memory tokens mean **full navigations re-auth to `/`** (move via client-side nav). **Browser presigned upload/download fix (post-smoke, now verified live — real browser PUT → 200):** two parts were needed — (1) `S3_PUBLIC_ENDPOINT=http://localhost:9000` so the presign targets a browser-reachable host (the #90 setting; it was empty), AND (2) the SPA CSP **`connect-src`** must include that object-store origin — `connect-src 'self'` alone blocked the direct browser→MinIO call (a gap #90 missed). Fixed generally: the Caddyfile CSP is now `connect-src 'self' {$S3_PUBLIC_ENDPOINT}` (templated; the `proxy` service gets `S3_PUBLIC_ENDPOINT` from `.env`), so browser presigned upload/download works in dev (localhost:9000) and prod alike (set the public object-store host; empty when it's same-origin behind Caddy). `S3_PUBLIC_ENDPOINT` now **defaults to `http://localhost:9000` in `.env.example`** (and `install.sh` `cp`s that, so a fresh clone gets in-app upload/download working out of the box); the live value lives in the gitignored `.env`. Both browser paths verified live: presigned PUT → 200 + check-in 201; controlled-copy download → a 15.9 KB watermarked PDF (application/pdf) → 200 through the CSP.

- **S-web-6** Global **Search + Compliance Checklist** — surfaces the **S10 search/reporting backend** (PR #38) in the SPA (**front-end only; NO migration [head stays `0044`], NO new permission key, NO `openapi.yaml` change** — `GET /search`, `/search/suggest`, `/reports/compliance-checklist` were all already shipped + contracted). Picked via a **9-agent scout** ranking the candidate next-slices (search vs ingestion-review vs CAPA-board vs dashboard vs drift) → search won on backend-readiness + every-user value + honest data. Owner forks (3, via `AskUserQuestion`): **palette + results page**; **dedicated gated `/compliance` route**; **`/suggest` quick-jump + a "Search →" footer**. **Search surface** (`features/search/`): a hand-rolled **⌘K command palette** (a Mantine `Modal` — **NO `@mantine/spotlight` dep**; `AppShell` owns the open-state + binds **two** `useHotkeys` — `mod+K` with **empty `tagsToIgnore`** so ⌘K fires even inside an input, and `/` with the default ignore-list so it never hijacks typing; the TopBar's formerly-`disabled` "Search (⌘K)" box is now the live trigger [`aria-label="Open search"`, readOnly, click/Enter/Space]); inside, a `combobox`+`aria-activedescendant` listbox over **debounced** (`useDebouncedValue`, 150 ms) `/suggest` rows (↑/↓/Enter keyboard nav) + a fixed **"Search '<q>' →"** footer action; **Enter = top-hit** when suggestions are loaded (Spotlight/VS-Code style; the default-`active=0` first suggestion), else the full search. The **`/search?q=` results page** (`SearchResultsPage`, URL-driven via `useSearchParams`) = `useSearch` ranked rows (`SearchResultRow`: identifier · title→`/documents/:id` · reused `StateBadge` · clause chips→`/library?clause=N` · the **XSS-safe `Snippet`** — splits PostgreSQL `ts_headline`'s literal `<b>…</b>` into `<Mark>`, renders every other segment as a React **text node**, **NO `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`** [a `<script>` in a title renders as literal text]) + the **`hidden_by_scope` footer** ("N hidden by your access scope") + an **honesty hint** ("title/identifier/clause refs — Effective documents only") + **calm** empty/no-results/**error** states (search **filters, never 403s**). **Compliance surface** (`features/compliance/`): a dedicated **`/compliance`** route + a **`LeftRail` entry gated on `usePermissions().can("report.compliance_checklist.read")`** (hidden unless held — the SYSTEM key sits with QMS Owner + Internal Auditor, **not** the demo admin; `useComplianceChecklist` surfaces a **`forbidden`** flag from `ApiError.status===403`+`retry:false` → a **calm no-access panel**, not a crash); the page = a **RAG rollup** (a **non-color** glyph+label+count legend — `✓ Covered / ◔ Partial / ✕ Gap`, *not* badges, to avoid a duplicate-`aria-label` clash with the table) + the **20★ clause `Table`** (`CoverageBadge` COVERED/PARTIAL/GAP, **DP-7** glyph+label) with each clause row → `/library?clause=N` (the doc-13 §3.1 drill-through; reuses the existing Library clause filter). **Tests:** **+30 web** (vitest + MSW + jest-axe) across the hooks (`useSearch`/`useSuggest` enabled-guards · `useComplianceChecklist` success+403), the **XSS-safe `Snippet`** (incl. a `<script>`-as-text guard), the `CommandPalette` (suggest→doc nav · footer→`/search` · Enter · **axe**), the `SearchResultsPage` (URL-driven · `hidden_by_scope` · no-results · **error** · axe), `CoverageBadge`, the `CompliancePage` (rollup · drill-through href · **403-calm** · axe rows+403), the **gated nav** (shown/hidden by `/me/permissions`), the shell wiring (⌘K + click open the palette), and the `/search`+`/compliance` routes — the full suite = **162 web tests** green (lint + tsc-strict + build all clean; tsc caught a `noUncheckedIndexedAccess` nit the per-task vitest runs missed). Method: scout → owner forks (3) → spec+plan (`docs/superpowers/.../2026-06-08-*s-web-6*`) → **subagent-driven TDD** (fresh implementer per task + per-task spec+quality review) — confirmed fixes folded: a **calm search-error state** (the results page silently showed nothing on a fetch error), the rollup **duplicate-`aria-label`** → the plain glyph+label legend, and the strict-index typecheck fix. **Honest deferrals** (the backend can't serve them): server-side **facets** + **saved searches** + **content-plane/body-text** search + the §2.3 owner/process/date facets + auditor **exports** (§7) + the Home **center-hub ★ tile** (the PDCA dashboard stays deferred until its acknowledgement/objective engines exist — the Compliance Checklist is the one *honest* health signal available now, and the future dashboard's center-hub). **Demo precondition:** the Checklist needs a **QMS-Owner / Internal-Auditor login** (or a SYSTEM override of `report.compliance_checklist.read` for the demo admin — the authoring-keys precedent); **search** works for any logged-in user. Minor accepted-as-noted: `aria-expanded` on the palette input is effectively always-true (the listbox is always open while the Modal is). **Next:** S-ing-4b (Ingestion Review UI, closes UJ-2) · the CAPA board · then the PDCA dashboard once acks/objectives land.

- **S-web-7a** CAPA Board (read spine) — first installment of the **S-web-7 NC & CAPA front-door epic** (7a–7d) (**front-end only; NO migration [head stays `0044`], NO new permission key, NO `openapi.yaml` change** — surfaces the S-aud-2/S-capa-1..3 `/capa` backend already shipped + contracted). **ONE thin backend enrichment** only: the `_capa` serializer was extended to include `title` + `created_at` on both list + detail responses and `raised_by` (user name) on the detail — no migration, no new endpoint, no new key. **New `features/capa/` module**: a **`CapaBoardPage`** at `/capa` with a **Board/List toggle** (default Board); a **Board view** = a horizontal kanban over **6 `close_state` columns** (Open · ActionPlan · Implement → the three active ACT-phase columns; Closed-Resolved / Closed-Rejected / Closed-Verified → the closed tail) driven by `useCapaBoard` (`GET /capa?close_state=…` per column, React Query); an **Open/by-source tile row** (count by `source_type`: Audit Finding / Customer Complaint / Process Deviation / Self-Identified / Import); a **read-only `CapaDetailDrawer`** (slides in on card click) showing the **closed-loop timeline** built from `stages[]` (stage cards with status badges + timestamps), a generic **XSS-safe `content_block` renderer** (plain-Text / Markdown-safe literal; no `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`) for stage body fields, the **close-gate stepper** (a compact stepper showing which stage gates remain open), and a **`cycle_marker > 0` re-opened loop indicator** (re-open count badge). Nav entry "Nonconformity & CAPA" is **UNCONDITIONAL / always discoverable** in `LeftRail` (NOT wrapped in a `can()` guard — unlike Compliance/Import) because CAPA is a core ACT-phase area; the page itself renders a **calm no-access panel** for callers without `capa.read` (the same 403-calm pattern as S-web-6 Compliance). **Demo precondition:** `demo` (System Administrator) holds **no `capa.*`** → the calm-403 panel is the expected experience; grant a SYSTEM override of `capa.read` (the authoring-keys precedent) to see live board data. **Tests: +46 web → the full suite = 364 web tests** green (lint + tsc-strict + build all clean). **Next:** S-web-7b (CAPA authoring — raise/progress a CAPA) · S-web-7c · S-web-7d · then the PDCA dashboard once acks/objectives land.

- **S-ing-4b** Ingestion Review UI — **CLOSES UJ-2** (import an existing QMS, end-to-end) (**front-end only; NO migration [head stays `0044`], NO new permission key, NO `openapi.yaml` change** — the S-ing-1..5 `/admin/imports/*` surface [migs 0029–0033] was already shipped + contracted). Owner forks (2, via `AskUserQuestion`): **full UJ-2 in one slice** (runs landing + New-Import + scan-progress + review cockpit + merge/split + commit + resume — not a thin carve); **server `offset`/`limit` pagination, NO virtualization + NO new SPA dep** — the DOM never holds >1 page so it already scales to any run size, and whole-bucket bulk actions use the server `selector` (never load all rows), so the brief's "virtualize to 5k+" risk was a non-issue. Brief-correction baked in: the commit gate is the **checklist `blocking[]`** (`ready && commit_ready≥1 && can("import.commit")`), and **unconfirmed-kind is advisory, not a hard block** (the mockup's "Commit N confirmed" commits the ready subset; unconfirmed items are silently skipped at commit). **New `features/ingestion/` module** (49 files): the **four-faces run page** (`IngestionRunPage` switches on `run.status` → ScanProgress [pre-Proposed stepper + Cancel] / ReviewCockpit [Proposed/Reviewing] / CommitProgress [Committing poll] / RunTerminalSummary [Completed/PartiallyCommitted+**Resume**/Failed/Cancelled — `onResume`→`useCommitRun`]); the **runs landing** (`IngestionRunsPage` + a gated New-Import modal → POST → poll); the **review cockpit** (`ReviewCockpit` owns the selection Set + drawer id + queue/conf/offset URL state) = 4 metric tiles + drift-safe import-plan banner + 5 queue tabs + confidence facet + a 9-col **TriageTable** (multi-select, per-row Accept/Correct/Merge/Confirm, cell renderers, quarantine-simplified rows) + offset pager + a multi-select **BulkActionBar** (bulk dimensional decisions + **Bulk-accept-all-High** via the server selector — **does NOT confirm kind**, R10) + an **ItemDetailDrawer** (classification evidence/extract/dedup-members/proposal/decision-history + per-item actions incl. **Split**) + **MergeMenu** (effective-member pick + per-family `reconstruct_revision_chain` opt-in) + the **PreCommitChecklist** (blocking RAG rows + advisory ★-coverage/`unknown_low`/`kind_unconfirmed`) + the **CommitCard** ("Commit N confirmed", enabled iff `ready && commit_ready≥1 && can(import.commit)`, else a calm held-by-another-role note). **R10 kind-confirm is a separate human act** throughout (`KindCell` renders the engine guess dimmed-with-`?`; only an explicit per-row/bulk Confirm sets `after.kind ∈ {DOCUMENT,RECORD}`). **Merge/split server-authoritative** (submit→invalidate, never optimistic reshape; the cockpit builds `dupeMap`/`familyMap`/`splitTargetMap` from the `/dupe-clusters`+`/version-families` lists since the row list carries no membership). Every write carries ONE `crypto.randomUUID()` `Idempotency-Key` per op. The `/files` endpoint filters only `band/kind/disposition/review_status` (clause/process/type facets + an in-drawer page-image preview + a source-root file-browser **deferred**; "Already in vault" ships as a documented v1-partial tab). **Tests: +155 web → the full suite = 318 web tests** green (lint + tsc-strict + build all clean). Method: brainstorm → owner forks (2) → spec+plan (`docs/superpowers/.../2026-06-08-*s-ing-4b*`) → a 4-lens **plan**-verification pass (foundation-binding / codebase-accuracy / composition / false-PASS, folded inline) → **subagent-driven TDD** (a sequential implement→review→fix workflow: 19 tasks, each TDD'd + committed) → full web gate → **diff-critic = 1 CRITICAL + 1 MAJOR, both fixed + regression-tested**: (CRITICAL) `run.counts` is a **flat top-level-merged bag** (`by_band.HIGH`, top-level `quarantine`, `proposal`, `commit` — **NOT** a `classify`/`queues`/`review` namespace; the folded review stats live on the **checklist** endpoint), and a fabricated MSW fixture hid that the tiles/tabs/pager would read **0** in production — now the tiles read `by_band` + the checklist `review` stats, the cockpit derives queue counts from `by_band`+`quarantine`+`review.undecided`, and the fixture mirrors the real shape; (MAJOR) `IngestionRunPage` now wires `RunTerminalSummary onResume → useCommitRun` so a `PartiallyCommitted` run can resume (idempotent re-commit). **Demo precondition:** the `demo` System Administrator holds **all three** import keys (`import.execute/review/commit` are in `_SYSTEM_KEYS` → the admin bundle, `0004`), so it drives the whole loop with no personas/overrides — unlike the compliance checklist. **Next:** the CAPA board · the PDCA dashboard (once acks/objectives land).

- **S-web-7b** CAPA **lifecycle writes** — second installment of the **S-web-7 NC & CAPA front-door epic**; **CLOSES the ACT-phase write loop** (raise → containment → root-cause → action-plan[approved] → implement → verify[SIGNED] → close, with the Verify→RootCause effectiveness loop + the M4 evidence close gate). **Not pure front-end** (the epic labelled it so, but the action-plan approval loop can't work without it): a **thin read-enrichment mirroring 7a — NO migration [head stays `0044`], NO new permission key** (every read reuses `capa.read`/self-scope). Owner forks (2, via `AskUserQuestion`): **the full honest backend layer** (vs a pure-front-end-blind variant); **one cohesive slice** (vs split 7b-i/7b-ii — the approval is load-bearing for the whole loop, so a partial slice can't be demoed end-to-end). **The integration finding that drove the design:** the seeded **Top-Management** approver of a Critical CAPA holds **only `capa.read`** (mig 0038), so `GET /workflow-instances/{id}` (`document.read`-gated) **403s them**, and the S-web-5 `/tasks` `ReviewApprovePage` blindly `GET /documents/{capa_id}`→404 — so a CAPA approver literally couldn't approve in the UI. **Backend (3 additive reads):** `subject_type`/`subject_id` on the `_task` **detail** serializer (list untouched) so the SPA routes a CAPA task without the document.read-gated instance read; **`GET /capas/{id}/approval`** (gated `capa.read`, the `GET /documents/{id}/approval` mirror) → `{instance(+tasks), proposed_action_plan}` from `workflow_instance.context` or `null`; **`evidence_links` per `_stage`** (a `list_stage_evidence` join to `documented_information.identifier`) so the drawer shows linked records + the close-gate stepper reads real evidence. `openapi.yaml` updated (`Task`/`CapaStage`/new path + `CapaApproval` schema); 2 CI integration tests. **Front-end (`features/capa/` + `features/review/`):** a contextual **`AdvancePanel`** (switches on `close_state` → the ONE legal next-stage form, `usePermissions`-gated at the CAPA's **PROCESS** scope [SYSTEM fallback], a calm read-only line when the key is absent, an "awaiting approval"/"no approver assigned" panel at RootCause from the approval read); **`StageForms`** (the 5 structured stage forms whose `content_block` keys round-trip through the 7a `ContentBlock` renderer + a server-authoritative **`CloseAction`** [always-enabled; the 409 `capa_close_incomplete`/`capa_not_verified` is the gate, surfaced calmly; a `not_effective` Verify → "Return to root cause"]); a signed **VerifyForm** (the S-web-5 v1 logged-confirmation checkbox; `409 sod_self_verify` → a calm SoD-4 message); an **`EvidenceLinker`** (link an EXISTING record → `POST /records/{id}/evidence-links target_type=capa_stage`; no upload — epic §7) rendered **only on the current cycle's** Implement/Verify stages (a looped CAPA's prior-cycle linkers would duplicate the "Record (Verify)" accessible name — the S-web-6 trap); the **`CloseGateStepper`** `deriveGate` made **evidence-aware + 2-arg** `(stages, cycleMarker)`, matching the backend `close_capa` EXACTLY (root_cause cycle-AGNOSTIC; implemented-action + effectiveness CURRENT-cycle + ≥1 evidence; a `not_effective` Verify never satisfies effectiveness — the 7a `closeState==="Closed"` proxy is gone); a board-level **`RaiseCapaModal`** (gated `capa.create`; `review_output` source omitted — the API 422s it); and the **subject-aware `/tasks` `ReviewApprovePage`** (branches on `task.subject_type`: CAPA → a new **`CapaApprovalContext`** [identity + the proposed plan, gated `capa.read` only] + a **generalized `DecisionCard`** [`documentId`→`{subjectType, subjectId}`; the decision POST already dispatches CAPA→`decide_capa_action_plan` server-side]; the DOCUMENT path stays **byte-identical**, decision-pane still `decidable && docId`-gated). Mutations **invalidate + refetch**, never optimistic reshape. Added a global jsdom **`Element.scrollIntoView` stub** (`test/setup.ts`) — a Mantine Combobox left open throws on its scroll timer otherwise (the matchMedia/ResizeObserver precedent). **Tests: +51 web → the full suite = 395 web tests** green (lint + tsc-strict + build all clean) + 2 CI integration. Method: brainstorm → owner forks (2) → spec+plan (`docs/superpowers/.../2026-06-09-*s-web-7b*`) → **subagent-driven TDD** (12 tasks, fresh implementer + a two-stage spec→quality review per task, committed per task) → full web gate → **diff-critic = MINOR** (1 confirmed bug fixed + tested: the looped-CAPA duplicate-label linker → current-cycle-only; + the evidence-link POST fixture pinned to the real `_evidence_link` serializer shape). The per-task reviews caught real bugs the TDD-by-plan missed: an `ActionPlanForm` `onChange` crash (reading `e.currentTarget.value` inside the `setState` updater — fixed by capturing the value first) and an `AdvancePanel` approval-loading flash. **Demo precondition:** `demo` (System Administrator) holds **no `capa.*`** → grant SYSTEM overrides of the `capa.*` keys (org short_code **ORG_EXAMPLE**) for a live smoke; a full **Critical/Major** create→…→close needs **≥2 distinct users** (SoD-4 verifier ≠ implementer, non-overridable) — use `priya`/`ken`/`mara`; **or** a **Minor** CAPA with `allow_capa_self_verify` for a 1-user smoke (the Critical two-tier approval also needs a Top-Management user). **⏟ Deferred decision (Codex review P1, PR #102 — re-evaluate later):** the board's **Raise CAPA** affordance gates on **SYSTEM `capa.create`** and the modal creates **SYSTEM-scoped CAPAs** (no `process_id` picker), so a Process-Owner who holds `capa.create` ONLY at their assigned PROCESS scope can't see the button or raise a process-scoped CAPA from the board. This is the spec §3.2 v1 choice (capa.create rides SYSTEM overrides in v1 — the "ride SYSTEM overrides until the role/UI lands" precedent); a real process picker also needs `process.read`. **Re-evaluate when owner-assignment binding lands** (then the create scope can resolve a real `process_id` and the button can gate per-process). The Codex thread on `CapaBoardPage.tsx` was left **open** for visibility. **Next:** S-web-7c (complaints/NCR intake) · S-web-7d (audits/findings) · then the PDCA dashboard once acks/objectives land.

- **S-web-7c** Complaint & NCR **intake** — third installment of the **S-web-7 NC & CAPA front-door epic**; surfaces the two lightweight front doors that feed the 7a board. **FRONT-END ONLY — NO migration [head stays `0044`], NO new permission key, NO `openapi.yaml` change** (the `/complaints*` + `/ncrs*` surface [S-capa-1, `api/capa.py:493-617`] was already shipped + contracted). Owner fork (1, via `AskUserQuestion`): the page structure — **tabbed sub-routes under `/capa`** (vs local-state tabs / separate nav entries). **A thin `CapaLayout`** (a Mantine `Tabs` strip Board·Complaints·NCRs + `<Outlet/>`, active tab from `useLocation().pathname`, `onChange`→`useNavigate`) wraps three child routes — `/capa` index (**`CapaBoardPage`**, keeps its own title), `/capa/complaints`, `/capa/ncrs`; the board's existing flows stay unchanged (it gained only one additive `?capa=<id>` deep-link seam — see Codex below); `LeftRail` stays a single "Nonconformity & CAPA" entry, each tab deep-linkable. **Complaints** (`ComplaintsPage` + `ComplaintForm`): a table (Identifier/Customer/Channel/Severity/Description/CAPA) over `useComplaints` (`GET /complaints`, `{data}`, `forbidden`-flag calm-403); a `record.create`-gated "Log complaint" modal (description\* + customer + channel + optional severity); and a per-row **idempotent spawn-CAPA** — the `_complaint` row's **`spawned_capa_id` drives the affordance**: null → a `capa.create`-gated "Spawn CAPA" button → a **`SpawnCapaModal` severity-confirm** step (pre-filled from the complaint, **required** — a CAPA needs a severity, the backend 422s without one, and a complaint can be logged severity-less; also the backend's "request severity = late triage" intent), set → a "View CAPA" link to `/capa` (no process picker — the 7b deferred Raise-scope decision). **The spawn's 201-new / 200-replay distinction is surfaced as STATE, not the HTTP status** — `api.send` discards the status, so the row flips via `spawned_capa_id` after the invalidate; a racing replay just resolves (`["complaints"]` + `["capas"]` both invalidated). **NCRs** (`NcrsPage` + `NcrForm` + `DispositionModal`): a table (Identifier/Source/Severity/Description/Disposition) over `useNcrs` (`ncr.read` calm-403); an `ncr.create`-gated "Raise NCR" modal (source\* + severity\* + description\*); and a per-row **one-shot ISO 8.7 disposition** — an undisposed NCR with `ncr.record_correction` gets a "Record disposition" modal (the 6 dispositions `use_as_is/rework/scrap/return/concession/regrade` + notes; `PATCH /ncrs/{id}/disposition`), a **disposed NCR renders its disposition read-only with NO action button** (the one-shot is structural in the UI), and a `409 ncr_already_dispositioned` (a race) is surfaced **calmly** in the modal, never a crash. All affordances gate via `usePermissions().can(key)` at **SYSTEM** scope (the 7b precedent — v1 grants ride SYSTEM overrides). Free-form text (descriptions) renders via Mantine `Text lineClamp` (**XSS-safe**, no `dangerouslySetInnerHTML`). Filters/summary tiles **deferred** (YAGNI — intake lists are bounded + scannable). **Per-key gating diverges from the board:** complaints ride `record.read`/`record.create`/spawn=`capa.create`; NCRs ride `ncr.read`/`ncr.create`/`ncr.record_correction` — the **`demo` System-Administrator holds NONE** (both tabs calm-403, like the board), and **`ncr.create` + `ncr.record_correction` are seeded but granted to NO role** (SYSTEM-override-only in v1). Fixtures pinned to the real `_complaint`/`_ncr` serializers (NOT the mockup — the #1 false-PASS lesson). **Tests: +35 web → the full suite = 429 web tests** green (lint + tsc-strict + build all clean). Method: brainstorm → owner fork (1) → spec+plan (`docs/superpowers/.../2026-06-09-*s-web-7c*`) → **subagent-driven TDD** (8 tasks, fresh implementer + a two-stage spec→quality review per task, committed per task) → full web gate → **diff-critic (CLEAN)** → PR (#103) → **pre-merge live smoke caught a real dead-end**: spawning a CAPA from a severity-LESS complaint 422'd (a CAPA requires a severity) behind a generic "try again" — fixed by the `SpawnCapaModal` severity-confirm step (the silent one-click inherit is gone). The smoke also confirmed the real App.tsx→AppShell→CapaLayout wiring + the per-key calm-403 panels live (incl. a full complaint→spawn→board + raise-NCR→disposition loop against the real backend). **Codex review: all 4 P2 threads addressed** — (1) a disposition `409`-race refetch via **invalidate-on-settle** so the row flips behind the calm error; (2) the disposed NCR row now shows the one-shot **who/when** audit context (authorizer resolved via the user directory, degrade-to-id) since the table is the only NCR surface; (3) the "require severity" P2 was pre-empted by the live-smoke `SpawnCapaModal`; (4) **deep-link "View CAPA"** — `CapaBoardPage` now opens its detail drawer from a **`?capa=<id>` URL param** (the S-web-4 `?from=&to=` pattern; local card/raise opens stay URL-untouched, so the board's existing flows are unchanged + history is left clean) and the Complaints "View CAPA" link deep-links the spawned CAPA's drawer. **Demo precondition:** for a live smoke grant `demo` SYSTEM overrides of `record.read record.create capa.create ncr.read ncr.create ncr.record_correction` (org short_code **ORG_EXAMPLE**) — one admin drives the whole loop (no SoD on complaints/NCRs/spawn). **Next:** S-web-7d (audits/findings) · then the PDCA dashboard once acks/objectives land.
- **S-web-7d** Audits & **findings** — the FOURTH and FINAL installment of the **S-web-7 NC & CAPA front-door epic**; **COMPLETES the epic** by surfacing the CHECK-phase internal-audit module (S-aud-1/2 backend, ISO 9001 Cl 9.2): programmes/plans/audits + the 7-state FSM, findings with the NC→auto-CAPA link, and the **R39 block-until-corrected close gate**. **ONE thin backend read-enrichment, NO migration [head stays `0044`], NO new permission key, NO new endpoint** (the 7a `_capa` precedent): `_audit` gains `identifier`/`title`/`created_at` (a same-PK `documented_information` join in `list_audits` + a `get_audit_header`/`_audit_full` builder used by create + get + ALL SIX transition responses, so a write never returns a null header) and `_finding` gains `title` (the logged summary / correction reason — one column added to the already-joined `_finding_select`; `title` is the 3rd positional with the chain args forced keyword-only so no call site silently shifts); `openapi.yaml` field-adds; the now-caller-less `audits_repo.get_identifier` deleted; 2 CI integration tests. Owner forks (4, via `AskUserQuestion`): **one cohesive PR** (the 7b precedent — the close gate is load-bearing across both halves); **enrich audits + findings** (vs UUID rows / textless finding cards); **full `/audits/:id` page** (the `documents/:id` precedent — findings CRUD + stepper outgrow a drawer); **Programme tab with tables** (the mockup's calendar strip + coverage-% dropped, unbacked). **New `features/audits/` module** (~30 files): `LeftRail` gains an **unconditional "Internal Audit"** entry (the CAPA precedent; calm-403 on the page) → an **`AuditsLayout`** tab strip (Audits · Programme, the `CapaLayout` shape) + the standalone detail route (react-router ranks the static `programme` over `:id` — route-shadow-test-proven). **`AuditsListPage`**: honest tiles (Total/Active/Closed; **Active = state ≠ Closed**; the mockup's coverage-%/open-findings/closure-% tiles dropped — unbacked), All/Active/Closed segmented filter, newest-first client sort, lead via `useUserDirectory` degrade-to-id, "—" for an unstarted audit (a `created_at` fallback under a "Started" header would mislead — review catch); an `audit.create`-gated **`NewAuditModal`** (programme→plan cascade; submit disabled until a plan is picked; calm guidance to the Programme tab when none exist). **`ProgrammePage`**: programmes table + `audit.plan`-gated create/edit/archive (`ProgramForm`, keyed remount; `coverage` not exposed) + the selected programme's **plans table** + `PlanForm` (plain `type="date"` input — no `@mantine/dates` dep; the auditee-process picker rides `process.read` and is **omitted on 403**; the lead picker rides the directory; `409 program_archived` calm). **`AuditDetailPage`**: header (identifier · title · `AuditStateBadge`) + plan/programme context + the **write scope resolved ONCE** (PROCESS at the plan's auditee process, SYSTEM fallback — the `_audit_scope` mirror) and passed to both panels; **`AuditLifecyclePanel`** = the 7-node stepper (✓/●/○, `aria-current="step"`) + **exactly ONE legal Advance action** from a `NEXT_TRANSITION` map (honest labels "Finalize plan"…"Close audit"; the gate **swaps `audit.conduct`→`audit.close`** at the close phase; a calm read-only line without the key; `useAdvanceAudit` invalidates **on settle** so a stale-tab `409 invalid_audit_transition` refetches behind the calm error; `409 audit_close_blocked` renders the server message under a "Close blocked" title); **`FindingsCard`/`FindingPanel`** = findings created-asc (type badge ⚑NC[severity]/◆Observation/➚OFI · title · clause/process chips · the correction chain rendered honestly [superseded = muted ✕, successor = ↪ corrects] · per-NC **CAPA state chip cross-ref'd from `useCapas`** [degrades entirely without `capa.read`] + "View CAPA →" through the 7c `?capa=<id>` board-drawer seam) + an advisory **close-readiness note** (Reported/Closing only, `blocking>0`, gated on `capas.isSuccess` so a loading list never over-counts) whose `isBlocking` mirrors the backend `finding_blocks_close` EXACTLY (live NC + auto-CAPA not Closed; superseded/OBS/OFI never block) — the server 409 stays the authority; **`LogFindingModal`** (NC requires a severity — confirm disabled until picked, the 7c SpawnCapaModal no-dead-end lesson; an NC success shows the **auto-CAPA confirmation + deep-link**, and the modal is **conditionally rendered so close unmounts it** — diff-critic MAJOR: the plan's own persistent mount left the stale confirmation on reopen, a dead-end the suite missed because no test reopened it) + **`CorrectFindingModal`** (pre-filled any-direction retype; to-NC severity rule; `409 finding_already_corrected` calm; cleared clause/process refs sent as explicit `""` — an omitted field **inherits** server-side, silently undoing the user's clear; same fix for `ProgramForm.period`). **Tests: +70 web → the full suite = 499 web tests** green (lint + tsc-strict + build clean) + 2 CI integration. Method: brainstorm → owner forks (4) → spec+plan (`docs/superpowers/.../2026-06-09-*s-web-7d*`) → a 4-lens **plan**-verification pass (6 fixes folded, incl. a Task-12/13 duplicate-text collision and a `Paper opacity`→`style` portability fix) → **subagent-driven TDD** (16 tasks, fresh implementer + spec/quality review per task, committed per task; reviews caught the misleading "Started" fallback + a Rules-of-Hooks placement the plan got wrong) → full web gate → **diff-critic = 1 MAJOR + 1 MINOR, both fixed + regression-tested** (above) → **pre-merge live smoke (FULL loop in the browser)**: programme AUDPROG-001 → plan → audit REC-GEN-006 (the enrichment verified live — identifier+title from the real serializer) → 6-step FSM walk → Major NC REC-GEN-007 → auto-CAPA REC-GEN-008 on the board via the deep-link → **Close blocked (409, calm, with the live readiness note)** → NC→OFI correction REC-GEN-009 (chain rendered) → **Close succeeded** — plus the calm-403 default experience and the no-`process.read` picker degrade verified incidentally, and the smoke's grant landed on the WRONG of two demo `app_user` rows first (Keycloak re-creates mint new subjects → new JIT rows; grant overrides to the row matching the LIVE login's subject). **Demo precondition:** grant `demo` SYSTEM overrides of `audit.read audit.plan audit.create audit.conduct audit.close finding.create finding.read capa.read` (org **ORG_EXAMPLE**) — one admin drives the whole loop (no SoD on the audit FSM); without them every face calm-403s by design. **Honest deferrals:** the mockup calendar strip · coverage editing/display · `result_summary` (never written in v1) · plan edit/delete (no endpoint) · `finding.link_capa` UI (no v1 endpoint) · org-wide findings metrics (N+1). **Next:** the PDCA dashboard once acks/objectives land · the v1.x drift family.

- **S-web-8** the trailing **drift-family UI** — surfaces the completed D1–D5 backend (S-drift-1…3) in the SPA (**FRONT-END ONLY: NO migration [head stays `0047`], NO new permission key, NO endpoint, NO `openapi.yaml` change** — consumes exactly `GET /admin/drift/status` + `GET /admin/drift/superseded-copies` [`drift.read`, R41] and the S-drift-1 read surface). Design calls settled in brainstorming (none register-level): **(1)** the drift surface = a main-`AppShell` **`/drift`** route + a `can("drift.read")`-gated LeftRail entry (the S-ing-4b `/ingestion` precedent — NOT the token-threaded AdminShell) with a `DriftLayout` tab pair (Status · **Superseded copies as a PAGE, not a drawer** — it's a paginated recall list, server `offset`/`limit`, no virtualization); **(2)** the `PERIODIC_REVIEW` task leg is **front-end-only** — the task detail already carries `subject_type`/`subject_id` (S-web-7b), the doc context loads **BEST-EFFORT** via `GET /documents/{id}` with **graceful 403 degradation that never blocks the decision card** (ownership is the server-side authority — the CAPA-path lesson generalized) and **never touches `GET /workflow-instances/*`** (spy-proven); **(3)** review-period editing = rows in the Control-metadata card + a **conditionally-rendered modal** gated on **`doc.capabilities.manage_metadata`** (the server-computed per-doc answer; clear = an **explicit `null`** — `model_fields_set`). **Four surfaces:** `features/drift/` (StatusPage = two DP-7-badged scan cards rendering `counts` as a **generic sorted OPEN bag** [unknown-key test; §10a] + the `blob_coverage.failing` re-alarm Alert + the D4 headline; SupersededCopiesPage = the 7-col recall table + Mantine `Pagination` driving the server offset; both calm-403); the `ReviewApprovePage` third branch (+`PeriodicReviewContext`, the obsolete-is-a-LINK note per S-drift-1 §5); the `DecisionCard` `PERIODIC_REVIEW` variant (outcomes **`complete` | `changes_requested`** via per-subject OUTCOMES tables — DOCUMENT/CAPA **byte-identical**, regression-pinned; the signature checkbox = "meaning: review confirmed"); the doc page's **deferred-since-S-web-1 "Next review" tile** (`daysUntil` days + date + `ReviewStateBadge` [server-derived, org-tz authoritative]) + `ControlMetadata` review rows; the compliance checklist's **overdue-review leg** (plain-text rollup counter + a per-row DP-7 badge — orthogonal to status, never a 4th coverage state). **Tests: +52 web → the full suite = 551** (lint + tsc-strict + build clean). Method: brainstorm → spec+plan (`docs/superpowers/.../2026-06-10-*s-web-8*`) → **subagent-driven TDD** (11 tasks, fresh implementer + two-stage spec→quality review per task; per-task reviews caught real bugs: a `retry: opts.retry` spread that would CLOBBER QueryClient defaultOptions with `undefined`, a reopen-pristine test that couldn't distinguish remount from persistence until the field was dirtied first, the DP-7 badge idiom on every new badge, an error-branch heading spine) → **diff-critic = 3 confirmed MINORs, all folded**: the periodic 409 now disambiguates by problem title (a stale double-decide ["Task already decided", a 2nd tab with its own per-mount idempotency key] no longer reads as "obsoleted"); `PeriodicReviewContext` passes `retry:false` (an EXPECTED 403 must not re-hammer the deny 3× under production QueryClient defaults — plus a **production-defaults test** pinning exactly one request, since the global test wrapper's `retry:false` masks retry regressions); the PATCH fixture dropped `clause_refs` (the real write response is bare `_document`). **Live smoke (the inherited S-drift-3 obligation DISCHARGED):** both drift endpoints hit authed-200 as the logged-in `demo` with real data — the status page rendered the REAL beat scans whose MIRROR counts carried keys the fixture never had (`baseline`/`pointer`/`build_name`/`symlink_divergent` — the open-bag contract proven live), and the smoke caught a **fixture-vs-serializer drift** (the real BLOB_REHASH key is **`sample_limit`**, not the spec's `sample_size` — pinned); superseded-copies = the honest **empty state** (this install has NO Superseded versions; minting one needs the full 3-persona FSM loop — the row rendering is CI-integration-proven, accepted residual). Full D5 loop live: backdate → sweep (`tasks_created: 1`) → checklist `⏰ Review overdue: 1` + the 8.4 row **COVERED + ▲ OVERDUE** (orthogonality live) → the periodic task page → **complete + review-confirmed signature** (the `signature_event` + `REVIEW_CONFIRMED` audit verified in PG) → the tile reset to **365 days ✓ CURRENT** → the PATCH modal (24 months → **731 days / 2028-06-10**, server-recomputed) → checklist cleared to 0. ⚠ **Live-login trap (new flavor):** the demo login's **System Administrator ROLE sat on the OLD bootstrap `app_user` row** — a re-created Keycloak `demo` JIT-mints a new row that holds NO role, so `drift.read`/import keys vanish from the rail; check `role_assignment` (not just overrides) against the LIVE subject's row and re-assign. **Honest deferrals:** a live D4 table row (above); the Library "Next review" column (mockup 2196); DCR deep-link prefill on `changes_requested` (no SPA DCR UI exists); the `/tasks` inbox row label stays the raw `action_expected` token. **The PDCA dashboard remains the web track's open item** (deferred until acks/objectives land).
